atha puraścaryārṇavaḥ

śrīdakṣiṇāmūrtigurubhyo namaḥ |

śuṇḍādaṇḍapracaṇḍaprahatihaṭhadalatprauḍhadorddaṇḍadṛpya-
ddaityendrānīkaśuṇḍotkararacitasamittāṇḍavakṣoṇiśobhaḥ |
pratyūhadhvāntadhāmādhipatiradhipatiḥ siddhasiddho gaṇānāṃ
dadyādadrīndrakanyāsmaraharanayanānandasaṃvarddhano vaḥ || 1-1 ||

brahmomādhavamādhavātmakatanuśchāyānanaprasphuran-
mugdhāmbhojamadhuvratābhanayanaḥ sākṣī jagatkarmaṇaḥ |
dhvāntadhvastasamastaviṣṭapaparitrāṇe nirastaśrama-
stigmāṃśustridaśāgraṇīstribhuvanastutyo'stu vaḥ śreyase || 1-2 ||

sadbhaktipraṇamadvidhitripurajidvṛtrāhitādyāmara-
śreṣṭhaśreṇikirīṭakoṭivilasadratnāṃśunīrājitam |
yat smṛtvā sudhiyastaranti sahasā saṃsāravārāṃ nidhiṃ
tadviṣṇoścaraṇāmbujaṃ diśatu vaḥ śreyo nṛsiṃhākṛteḥ || 1-3 ||

yacchaktiḥ prakṛtistato mahadabhūttatvaṃ tato'haṃkṛti-
stanmātrāṇi tataśca bhūtanivahastebhyaḥ pṛthivyādikaḥ |
bhūtebhyaḥ karaṇāni ceti jagadutpannaṃ yataḥ so'vyayaḥ 
pāyāccandrakalāvataṃsaruciraḥ sānugraho vaḥ śivaḥ || 1-4 ||

p. 2) avyakto muktimārgo nigamanigadito durga ityāgamajñāḥ
svāvidyādīnapodyat padayugamamṛtaṃ lebhire bhāvayantaḥ |
pāyātsā sauribhaghnī bhavasalilanidhestāriṇī rakṣaṇīti
brahmādyairadvitīyeti ca satatanutā siddhavidyādito vaḥ || 1-5 ||

natvā śrīgaṇanāyakaṃ dinamaṇiṃ viṣṇuṃ paśūnāṃ patiṃ
durgāṃ durgatitāriṇīṃ bhagavatīṃ guhyeśvarīmīśvarīm |
śrīmatsāhapratāpasiṃhadharaṇīpālena nānāgamān
dṛṣṭvā sarvajanopakārakapuraścaryārṇavastanyate || 1-6 ||

audāryeṇa tapaḥsutaṃ sumahatā śauryeṇa karṇadviṣaṃ
saundaryeṇa manobhavaṃ jalanidhiṃ gāmbhīryadhairyādibhiḥ |
kīrtyā candrarucaṃ śriyā harihayaṃ bhānuṃ pratāpena ca
kṣmāyāṃ nirjarapādapān vitaraṇairnirjitya yo rājate || 1-7 ||

tasya kṣoṇīmaghonaḥ samaravijayino'śeṣaśāstrārthavidbhiḥ
sadbhiḥ saṃśodhanīyaḥ suvimalamatibhiḥ samyageṣa prabandhaḥ |
vidvadvidveṣatarṣairvimalaparaguṇotkarṣavidhvastaharṣaiḥ
roṣāddoṣaikajijñāsubhiratimalinairdūṣyatāṃ durvidagdhaiḥ || 1-8 ||

vidvadbhāvānabhijñaiḥ prasabhamayamavajñāyamāno'pyanāryai-
rnna syādasmatprabandhaḥ kathamiva viduṣāṃ mānasollāsahetuḥ |
guñjāpuñjānuraktairvipinaparisare bhilladārairvimuktā
muktāḥ kiṃ nādriyante daradalitanavāmbhojapatrāyatākṣyaḥ || 1-9 ||

atrādau devatānāṃ smaraharagaditān sādhu sāmnāyabhedān
bhedānādye taraṅge kati cana viditān darśayāmastadūrdhvam |
kālīkaṃsāritārāraghuvaramurajicchailajāvallabhānā-
maikyaṃ yugmakrameṇāgamagaditamabhedaśca śaktitrayasya || 1-10 ||

p. 3) rāmasya vakṣye śivaśaktirūpatā-
maikyaṃ ca sītāhimaśailakanyayoḥ |
śrīrāmakāmāntakayorapi kramā-
daikyaṃ tato daivatapañcakasya || 1-11 ||

tataḥ paraṃ tāntrikamārgabhedān
vakṣyāmi teṣāmadhikāriṇaśca |
tataśca bhedānapi vaiṣṇavānāṃ
tataḥ paraṃ pāśupataprabhedān || 1-12 ||

upāsanāṃ cāpi bahuprabhedā-
madīkṣitānāṃ tadanarhatāṃ ca |
tataśca dīkṣāmanusadgurūṇāṃ
vakṣyāmi māhātmyamanukrameṇa || 1-13 ||

tataḥ pravakṣye guruśiṣyalakṣaṇaṃ
niṣiddhayorlakṣaṇametayostataḥ |
tato gṛhītasya manoraheyatā-
masadgurūpāttamanośca heyatām || 1-14 ||

pitrādimantragrahaṇe niṣedhaṃ
vidhiṃ ca mantreṣvadhikāriṇaśca |
svapnāptamantrasya ca saṃskriyāśca 
vakṣyāmi cakrāṇi ca tanniṣedhān || 1-15 ||

tato dvitīye rucire taraṅge
vakṣyāmi dīkṣocitakāladeśān |
mantreṣu śāpānapi devatānā-
mukttvā taduddhāravidhiṃ ca vakṣye || 1-16 ||

p. 4) tataśca saṃskāravidhiṃ manūnāṃ
tajjāgṛtiṃ bhūmiparīkṣaṇaṃ ca |
vakṣyāmi śalyoddharaṇaprakārān
vāstorvidhiṃ maṇḍapalakṣaṇaṃ ca || 1-17 ||

kuṇḍāni ca sruksruvayośca lakṣaṇaṃ
tatraiva vakṣyāmi tato'ṅkurārpaṇam |
dīkṣāṃ pravakṣyāmi tataḥ kriyāvatīṃ
taraṅgayostatra tṛtīyaturyayoḥ || 1-18 ||

dīkṣāvidhau tatra tṛtīyavīcau
vakṣyāmi tāvadvaraṇaṃ gurūṇām |
tataśca ṛtvigvaraṇaṃ pravakṣye
yaddantakāṣṭhasya parīkṣaṇaṃ tat || 1-19 ||

dvārārcanaṃ yāgagṛhapraveśaṃ
pūjopayuktāni ca maṇḍalāni |
tataḥ saparyāvidhimāgamoktaṃ
vakṣye ghaṭasthāpanapūrvakaṃ ca || 1-20 ||

turye taraṅge'khilamagnikāryaṃ
vakṣye tataḥ pāyasabhojanaṃ ca |
guroḥ saśiṣyasya tato'bhidhāsye
svāpaṃ tataḥ svapnaparīkṣaṇaṃ ca || 1-21 ||

tatraiva dīkṣādivasocitāni
karmāṇyaśeṣāṇi manoḥ pradānam |
vyaktīkariṣyāmyatha pañcame tu
prayogamasyāḥ sakalaṃ taraṅge || 1-22 ||

p. 5) saṃkṣepadīkṣāṃ ca tataḥ pravakṣye
tataḥ paraṃ varṇamayīṃ ca dīkṣām |
kalāvatīṃ nāma tataśca dīkṣāṃ
tataḥ paraṃ vedhamayīṃ ca dīkṣām || 1-23 ||

vakṣyāmi pāñcāyatanīśca dīkṣāḥ
smārtīṃ tato mānasikīṃ ca dīkṣām |
yaugīṃ ca dīkṣāṃ tadanantaraṃ dṛg-
dīkṣāṃ tataḥ sparśamayīṃ ca dīkṣām || 1-24 ||

dīkṣāṃ pravakṣyāmi tataśca vacikīṃ
māntrīṃ ca hautrīṃ ca tataḥ samāsataḥ |
śāstrīṃ vadiṣyāmi tathābhiṣekikīṃ
tatraiva saṃkṣiptatarāṃ ca śāmbhavīm || 1-25 ||

tataḥ pravakṣyāmyupadeśadīkṣāṃ
tato'bhidhānaṃ kramadīkṣaṇasya |
pūrṇābhiṣekaṃ ca tato'bhidhāsye
manorgrahaṃ deśikamantareṇa || 1-26 ||

ṣaṣṭhe taraṅge'tha puraskriyāyā
nirūpayiṣyāmi vidhiṃ samagram |
tatrāpi tāvat samayaṃ ca deśān
vakṣyāmi vakṣye tata āsanāni || 1-27 ||

mālāprabhedānapi tatra vakṣye
rudrākṣamāhātmyamapi prasaṅgāt |
tatraiva mālāgrathanaprakāraṃ
vakṣyāmi saṃskāravidhiṃ ca tasyāḥ || 1-28 ||

p. 6) mudrāṇāmapi lakṣaṇāni niyamān heyāni deyāni ca
prāyaścittamanekamuktaniyamabhraṣṭopayuktaṃ naraḥ |
sakṣoṇīgṛhakūrmacakraracanāṃ śāktāni pīṭhāni ca
prātaḥkṛtyamatho'japāvidhiyutaṃ vakṣyāmi tatraiva ca || 1-29 ||

śaucaṃ tatra ca dantadhāvananidhiṃ snānaṃ ca nānāvidhaṃ
tantroktaṃ tilakasya dhāraṇavidhiṃ tadvadvibhūterapi |
sandhyopāsānadevatarpaṇavidhiṃ nityārcanaṃ tādṛśaṃ
homaṃ prastutakarmaṇaśca vidhivadvakṣye tataḥ sakramam || 1-30 ||

yantrasya nirmāṇamatha pramāṇaṃ
tataḥ pratiṣṭhāvidhimāgamoktam |
vakṣye manūnāmatha sūtakitvaṃ
svāpaṃ prabodhaṃ ca śikhāśritaṃ ca || 1-31 ||

vakṣyāmi tatraiva ca kullukāmanuṃ
setuṃ ca nirvāṇamapi kramāgatam |
tatraiva mantraṃ rasanāviśodhanaṃ
tataśca mantrāvayavān hareritān || 1-32 ||

japasya vakṣyāmi tato vidhānaṃ
homavyavasthāmatha tarpaṇaṃ ca |
tato'bhiṣekasya vidhiṃ pravakṣye
tataḥ paraṃ brāhmaṇabhojanaṃ ca || 1-33 ||

tato'bhidhāsyāmi sasiddhilakṣaṇāḥ
sopāyavargāśca puraskriyābhidhāḥ |
sarvāstaraṅge rucire ca saptame
sadūtikāyāgasavīrasādhanāḥ || 1-34 ||

p. 7) tato'ṣṭame cārutame taraṅge
gaṇādhipasyātha raveśca viṣṇoḥ |
matsyasya kūrmasya varāhamūrte-
stadīyaśakteśca tathā dhariṇyāḥ || 1-35 ||

śrīdivyasiṃhasya ca vāmanasya
trivikramasyātha kuṭhārapāṇeḥ |
śrīrāmacandrasya ca bhūsutāyā-
stataḥ pralambāsuravairiṇaśca || 1-36 ||

kṛṣṇasya buddhasya tathaiva kalkina-
stato hayagrīvarathāṅgarūpayoḥ |
anantasaṃjñasya ca gopasundarī-
mūrtestataḥ śrīdharasaṃjñakasya || 1-37 ||

śivasya mṛtyuñjayanīlakaṇṭha-
śrīdakṣiṇāmūrtipurassaraiśca |
sakṣetrapālairbaṭukādirūpai-
rbhedairanekaiḥ sahitasya tadvat || 1-38 ||

murāridehārddhabhṛtaḥ purāre-
stato bhavānyāśca garutmataśca |
sakārtavīryasya samīrasūnoḥ
smarasya vakṣyāmi manūnanekān || 1-39 ||

eṣāṃ ca ṛṣyādipurassarāṇi
dhyānāni vakṣyāmi puraskriyāśca |
pratyekapūrvaṃ vidhimatra naiṣāṃ
vakṣye prabandhasya gurutvabhīteḥ || 1-40 ||

p. 8) svaiḥ svaiḥ prabhedaiḥ sahitāstato mahā-
vidyāstaraṅge navame daśa kramāt |
vakṣyāmi tāsāṃ ca puraskriyāvidhiṃ
nīlakramādīnapi bhāvasaṃyutān || 1-41 ||

tatastrilokārcitapādukāyā
vāgdevatāyā daśame taraṅge |
tatraiva devyāstvaritābhidhāyāḥ
klinnānnapūrṇādisamanvitāyāḥ || 1-42 ||

pratyaṅgirāyā atha kubjikāyāḥ
surāpagāyāśca savāgbhavāyāḥ |
caṇḍādikānāṃ ca tato grahāṇāṃ
grahaprasūnāmatha digyutānām || 1-43 ||

mantrān pravakṣyāmi puraskriyāntā-
nathābhidhāsye jananīṃ śrutīnām |
nyāsānaśeṣān kramataśca tasyā
dhyānaṃ ca yantraṃ yajanakramaṃ ca || 1-44 ||

tasyāḥ saparyāvidhimapyaśeṣaṃ
mudrāśca vakṣyāmi puraskriyāśca |
tārasya pauraścaraṇaṃ vidhānaṃ
mṛtyuñjayaśrautamanostathaiva || 1-45 ||

rudrādhyāyapuraskriyā bahuvidhaiḥ saumyaiḥ samaṃ vakṣyate
sūktānāṃ ca puraskriyāvidhimatha śrautān grahāṇāṃ manūn |
teṣāṃ ca tripurāribhāṣitapuraścaryābhidhānaṃ tato
havyānāmapi ca pramāṇamakhilaṃ vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepataḥ || 1-46 ||

p. 9) taraṅga - ekādaśa - uktarītyā
durgāmanūnāmatha tāntrikāṇām |
vakṣyāmi pauraścaraṇaṃ vidhānaṃ
durgotsavasyāpi vidhiṃ samagram || 1-47 ||

balipradānasya vidhiṃ paśūnāṃ
vastvantarāṇāmapi daivatāni |
tatraiva vakṣyāmi kumārikāyāḥ
pūjāvidhānaṃ ca śivābaliṃ ca || 1-48 ||

tatraiva revantahayebhapūjā-
vidhiṃ prayogān navarātrakṛtyam |
tathaiva vakṣyāmi śivopadiṣṭaṃ
nirājanaṃ khañjanadarśanaṃ ca || 1-49 ||

tatastaraṅge carame manorame
brāhmādimantrān sapuraskriyāvidhīn |
gorakṣanāthasya manūṃstathāvidhān
vakṣye tatastādṛśaśītalāmanum || 1-50 ||

yantrāṇyatho yantrapuraskriyāyā
vidhiṃ tato bhūtalipiṃ ca tasyāḥ |
tatraiva pauraścaraṇaṃ vidhānaṃ
vakṣye lipinyāsapuraskriyāśca || 1-51 ||

vidyotayantīmatha mātṛkāyā-
strilohamudrāmatulaprabhāvām |
tatraiva saṃskāravidhiṃ ca tasyā-
p. 10)  stathaiva vakṣye navaratnamudrām || 1-52 ||

ṣoḍhānyāsasahasranāmakavacastotrāntarāṇāṃ tata-
stadvat saptaśatīstavasya ca puraścaryābhidhānaṃ kramāt |
tatpāṭhasya vidhiṃ phalaiśca sahitaṃ homaprayogānvitam
mantrāṇāṃ ca vibhāgamasya tadanu praśnasya vakṣye vidhim || 1-53 ||

prāṇāyāmapuraskriyā budhavarairvedyā tato vakṣyate
mantrāṇāṃ ca puraskriyāmapi vinā siddhiprakāro'pi vā |
nānābhairavayoginīsuravadhūjvālāmukhīnāṃ kramā-
danyāsāmapi ca kṣiterapi puraścaryānvitaṃ sādhanam || 1-54 ||



	tatra puraścaryāyā mantrasiddhiphalakadevatārādhanaviśeṣa-rūpatvādārādhyadevatāyā abhyarhitatvena ṣaḍāmnāyāntargatadeva-tāstāvannirūpyante | tatra gaṇeśaviśeṣasya mantraniṣṭhaṃ kiṃ cidvailakṣaṇyamādāya ṣaḍāmnāyavṛttitvamuktaṃ

merutantre

ekabījātmakaṃ vakṣye vighnamantraṃ susiddhidam |
tatrādau vaidikānāṃ tu gakāraḥ kevalo mataḥ || 1-55 ||

dakṣiṇānāṃ binduyukto dvibinduḥ pūrvamārgiṇām |
etaddvayayutaḥ paścādūrdhvāmnāye ca gaumiti || 1-56 ||

glaumuttare gomiti ca pātālāmnāya īritam |

	atra dakṣiṇāmnāye lakṣmīgaṇeśaharidrāgaṇeśādayaḥ, urddhvāmnāye mahāgaṇapatyādayaḥ, pūrvāmnāye viriñcigaṇa-nāyakādayaḥ, paścimāmnāye kṣipraprasādanagaṇeśādayaḥ, uttarāmnāye ucchiṣṭagaṇeśādaya, iti bhūyāṃso gaṇeśabhedā merutantre draṣṭavyāḥ |

p.11) sūryasya dvādaśabhedavato'pi dakṣiṇa evāmnāyaḥ || 1-57 ||

taduktaṃ tatraiva

eka evāsya cāmnāyo dakṣiṇaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
yathāyaṃ yavanaṃ vipraṃ samameva karaiḥ spṛśet || 1-58 ||

upāsanā tathaivāsya svadharmācārapūrvikā |
caturyugeṣu pratyakṣo devo'yaṃ sarvasaṃmataḥ || 1-59 ||

sarveṣāmeva devānāṃ netrabhūto jagatpatiḥ |

dvādaśabhedāḥ

varuṇaḥ1 sūrya2vedāṅgau3 bhānu4rindro5 ravi6stathā || 1-60 ||

gabhasti7stu yamaḥ8 svarṇaretā9ścātha divākaraḥ10 |
mitro11viṣṇu12riti proktā ādityā dvādaśa kramāt || 1-61 ||

tapanti māghādārabhya kramādamitatejasaḥ |

iti tatraivoktāḥ

viṣṇoḥ śivasya cāmnāyaprayuktabhedo'nupalabhyamānaḥ | avatāramantradhyānādiprayuktāstvanantā eva bhedāḥ, teṣu kati cidbhedāḥ puraścaraṇaprakārakathanāvasare sphuṭībhaviṣyantīti atra noktāḥ | devībhedānāmanantatvena vaktumaśakyatve'pi vāḍavānalīyatantroktāḥ kati cid bhedā iha pradarśyante |

taduktaṃ vaḍavānalīyatantre

ekaivādyā jagatsūtiḥ saccidānandavigrahā || 1-62 ||

tattadvibhūtibhedena bhinnānekatvamāgatā |
p. 12) pūrṇeśī bhuvaneśānī lalitā cāparājitā || 1-63 ||

lakṣmīḥ sarasvatī vāṇī pārijātapadāṅkitā |
annapūrṇā jayādyāśca pūrvāmnāyasamāśritāḥ || 1-64 ||

niśeśī dakṣiṇākālī bagalā chinnamastakā |
bhadrā tārā ca mātaṅgī dakṣiṇāmnāyadevatāḥ || 1-65 ||

bahuprabhedasaṃyuktā kubjikā ca kulālikā |
mātaṅgyamṛtalakṣmyādyāḥ paścimāmnāyadevatāḥ || 1-66 ||

etena mātaṅgyā āmnāyadvayavṛttitvamāhitam, dakṣiṇāmnāye'pi tasyā abhidhānāt | tathā -

siddhilakṣmīrguhyakālī mahābhīmasarasvatī |
dhūmrā kāmakalā kālī mahākālī kapālinī || 1-67 ||

mahāśmaśānakālī ca kālasaṃkarṣiṇī tathā |
pratyaṅgirā mahārātriryogeśī siddhibhairavī || 1-68 ||

etā bahuvidhāyuktāḥ sarvavidyottamottamāḥ |
uttarāmnāyapīṭhasthāścaturvargaphalapradāḥ || 1-69 ||

	tārācchinnamastayorapi uttarāmnāyavṛttitvamuktaṃ

muṇḍamālāyām

bhedāstvekonapañcāśaduttarāmnāyavartmani |
guhyakālī tataḥ proktā siddhilakṣmīstataḥ param || 1-70 ||

svarṇakoṭīśvarī tatra rājarājeśvarī tathā |
guhyeśvarī tathā proktā tathā tārā trirūpiṇī || 1-71 ||

chinnamastā mahādevī tataḥ proktātibhīṣaṇā |
evaṃvidhāstu saṃketāḥ sadā gopyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 1-72 ||

p.13) vāḍavānalīye

kāmeśī lalitā bālā mahātripurasundarī |
tripurā bhairavī hyetā ūrdhvāmnāyasamāśritāḥ || 1-73 ||

	lalitāyāḥ pūrvāmnāye'pyuktatvādāmnāyadvayavṛttitvaṃ bodhitam | kva cittvariteti pāṭhaḥ |

yoginī vajrapūrvā ca pannagī naiṛteśvarī |
adharāmnāyapīṭhasthā jainamārgaprapūjitāḥ || 1-74 ||

atraiva pradhānatayā bhīmādevī

mahākālasaṃhitāyāmuktā

niśāmayādharāmnāyagocarān devatāmanūn |
yatrādyabhūtā vikhyātā bhīmādevī bhayānakā || iti || 1-75 ||

kulabālā ca durgā ca sarvāmnāyaprapūjitā |

kulabālā, kumārī | tathā -

tayoḥ pūjā tu sarvatra nityatvenābhidhīyate || 1-76 ||

tayordurgākumāryoḥ | etāstu daśa devatā mahāvidyātvena prasiddhāḥ |

taduktaṃ śaktisaṅgame

kālī1 tārā2 chinnamastā3 sundarī4 bagalā5 ramā6 |
mātaṅgī7 bhuvaneśānī8 siddhavidyā ca bhairavī9 || 1-77 ||

dhūmāvatī10 ca daśamī mahāvidyā daśa smṛtāḥ |

tatraiva

	etāsāṃ pratyekaṃ bhairavamāha -

kālikāyā mahākālaḥ sundaryā laliteśvaraḥ || 1-78 ||

p. 14) tārāyāśca tathākṣobhyaśchinnāyā vikarālakaḥ |
bhuvanāyā mahādevo dhūmrāyāḥ kālabhairavaḥ || 1-79 ||

nārāyaṇo mahālakṣmyā bhairavyā baṭukaḥ smṛtaḥ | 
mātaṅgyāstu mataṅgaḥ syādatha vā syāt sadāśivaḥ || 1-80 ||

mṛtyuñjayastu bagalāvidyāyāḥ parikīrtitaḥ |

	laliteśvarastripurabhairavaḥ, vikarālaḥ krodhabhairavaḥ, kālabhairavoghoraḥ | yadyapi rudrayāmale tārācchinnamastādīnāṃ sadyojātakālarudrādaya uktāḥ, tathāpi eteṣāmeva bhairavāṇāṃ nāmāntarāṇi tatroktāni na tu bhairavāntarāṇītyavirodhaḥ |

śaktisaṃgame

krodhabhairavasaṃyogādyakṣiṇyaḥ siddhidā yathā || 1-81 ||

tathā vidyāḥ prasiddhyanti puṃyogādeva pārvati |

rudrayāmale

kevalaṃ yo japecchāktaṃ manuṃ śaivaṃ na yojayet || 1-82 ||

ubhayopāsanaṃ devā bhuktimuktipradāyakam |

śaktyā vinā śive śuṣke nāma dhāma na vidyate || 1-85 ||

p. 15) śivaṃ vinā citkalānāṃ kalātvaṃ na kvacid bhavet |

ṣaḍāmnāyadevatārādhanaphalānyāha

merutantre

dakṣiṇopāsakaḥ kāla ūrdhvaḥ sāyujyamāpnuyāt || 1-87 ||

devatāyāstathā pūrvaḥ sārūpyaṃ labhate param |
samīpyaṃ cottaro loke paścimastvaihikaṃ phalam || 1-88 ||

lābhastyāgaścopakāro hyadhomārge'pyudarka yuk |

	kāla iti, mahākālasvarūpī śiva eva bhavedityarthaḥ | yattu kāle iti saptamyantaṃ padaṃ, tena dakṣiṇopāsaka ūrdhvāmnāyopāsakaśca kāle dehāntasamaye sāyujyamāpnuyādityartha iti kaiściduktaṃ, tadasat |

pañcalakṣaṇasaṃyukto brāhmaṇaḥ kāla eva saḥ || 1-89 ||

iti pūrvagranthavirodhāt |

yatprasādādahaṃ tvaṃ ca jātaḥ kāla ivāparaḥ |

	ityuttaragranthavirodhācca | uttaro loke iti, uttarāmnāyadevatopāsakaḥ sālokyaṃ prāpnuyādityarthaḥ | idamuttarāmnāye guhyeśvaryādidevatopāsakaviṣayam |

tadupāsakānāṃ tu caturvidhamuktiprāptiruktā

p.16) mahākālasaṃhitāyāmanusmṛtiprakaraṇe

sālokyaṃ caiva sāmīpyaṃ sārūpyaṃ ca tṛtīyakam || 1-90 ||

sāyujyamiti deveśi muktiruktā caturvidhā |
tāsvanyopāsakānāṃ syādekaikā muktirīśvari || 1-91 ||

guhyakālyārādhakasya kramānmukticatuṣṭayam |
jāyate nātra sandehastripuraghnānuśāsanāt || 1-92 ||

ṣaḍāmnāyeṣu deveśi bhūyasyaḥ santi devatāḥ |
tāsu kāścit kṛtayuge tretāyāṃ kāścidīritāḥ || 1-93 ||

dvāpare phaladāḥ kāścit kalau kāścit phalapradāḥ |
caturyugeṣu phaladā daśa vidyā mayeritāḥ || 1-94 ||

tāsu tisro viśiṣyante kālī tārā ca sundarī |
tisṛṣvapi śive tāsu kalau kālī viśiṣyate || 1-95 ||

navavidhāsu kālīṣu guhyakālī praśasyate |
anayā sadṛśī vidyā nāsti brahmāṇḍagolake || 1-96 ||

vidyārājñī guhyakālī bhidyate na kadācana |

p. 18) yā mahāvaiṣṇavī māyā sā mahāsundarī matā |

rudrayāmale

devīcakre'rcayet sarvān śivaliṅge'thavā śive || 1-118 ||

p. 20) kāmadhenutantre

kālasaṃkalanāt kālī kālagrāsaṃ karotyataḥ |

sundarīstave

puraṃ śarīraṃ yasyāṃ sā tripureti prakīrtitā |

tārāśabdārthamāha

matsyasūkte

līlayā vākpradā caiva tena nīlasarasvatī || 1-124 ||

āmnāyabhedena mārgabhedanirṇayamāha

vāḍavānalīye

pūrvāmnāye dakṣamārgo vāmaḥ syāt paścime pare |

tatraiva

dakṣahastena vāme'pi viśeṣaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
p. 21) dakṣavāmakriyāyuktaḥ kaulaścobhayamiśritaḥ || 1-129 ||

makārāvaśyakatvācca dakṣatarpaṇapūjanāt |
kva cidvāmena deveśi kaulo mārgastṛtīyakaḥ || 1-130 ||

rudrayāmale

trīṇi bījāni durgāyāstadācārāstrayaḥ smṛtāḥ |
prathamo dakṣiṇācāro vāmācāro dvitīyakaḥ || 1-131 ||

tṛtīyastu kulācāro vidhiṃ teṣāṃ śṛṇu priye |
prabhāte snānasaṃdhyādi madhyāhne japa īśvari || 1-132 ||

aurṇamāsanamātmārthaṃ bhakṣyaṃ pāyasaśarkaram |
mālā rudrākṣasaṃbhūtā pātraṃ pāṣāṇasaṃbhavam || 1-133 ||

bhogaḥ svakīyakāntābhirdakṣiṇācāra ityayam |
dravyeṇa madhunā devi siddhihānikaro mataḥ || 1-134 ||

vāmācāraṃ pravakṣyāmi śrīdurgāsādhanaṃ param |
yaṃ vidhāya kalau śīghraṃ māntrikaḥ siddhibhāg bhavet || 1-135 ||

mālā nṛdantasaṃbhūtā pātraṃ pāṣāṇamuṇḍakam |
āsanaṃ siddhacarmādi kaṅkaṇaṃ strīkacodbhavam || 1-136 ||

dravyamāsavatattvāḍhyaṃ bhakṣyaṃ māṃsādikaṃ śive |
carvaṇaṃ bālamatsyādi mudrā vīṇāravaḥ kathā || 1-137 ||

maithunaṃ parakāntābhiḥ sarvavarṇasamānatā |
vāmācāra iti proktaḥ sarvasiddhipradaḥ śive || 1-138 ||

anyathā siddhihāniḥ syānmantrasyāsya maheśvari |
kulācāraṃ pravakṣyāmi sevyaṃ yogibhiruttamaiḥ || 1-139 ||

p. 22) kulastriyaṃ kulaguruṃ kuladevīṃ maheśvari |
nityaṃ yat pūjayedviśvaṃ sa kulācāra ucyate || 1-140 ||

kulastriyaṃ śive jñātvā nutvā natvā maheśvari |
haṭhādānīya saṃpūjya tato bhogaṃ vidhāya ca || 1-141 ||

tejasā tarpayeddevīṃ śrīdurgāṃ cakranāyikām |
nīlakaṇṭhaṃ ca vidhinā japaṃ kuryādviśeṣataḥ || 1-142 ||

tatpūrvakaṃ careddhomaṃ kulakāntāṃ vibhūṣayet |
pānaiḥ peyaistathā bhakṣyaiḥ saṃtarpya kulayoṣitam || 1-143 ||

pratyahaṃ vai caredevaṃ kulācāra iti smṛtaḥ |

kaulādibhedena vāmamārgavidhirukto,

merutantre

kauliko'ṅguṣṭhatāṃ prāpto vāmaḥ syāttarjanīsamaḥ || 1-144 ||

cīnakramo madhyamaḥ syāt siddhāntīyo'varo bhavet |
kaniṣṭhaḥ śābaro mārga iti vāmastu pañcadhā || 1-145 ||

mārgabhedenādhikāribheda ukto,

vāḍavānalīye

brāhmaṇāh kṣatriyā vaiśyā dakṣamārgādhikāriṇaḥ |
dvijavarjāstrayo varṇā vāmakaulādhikāriṇaḥ || 1-146 ||

dakṣamārgaparibhraṣṭo brāhmaṇo vāmamārgagaḥ |
ihaiva siddhimāpnoti nāpavargaṃ kadācana || 1-147 ||

vedamārgaparityāgī kaivalyecchāvivarjitaḥ |
siddhikāmī vāmamārgī brāhmaṇo nārakī bhavet || 1-148 ||

p. 23) vedamārgaṃ parityajya tantramārgaikatatparāḥ |
brāhmaṇā nirayaṃ bhuktvā bhaveyurbrahmarākṣasāḥ || 1-149 ||

vaidikaṃ tāntrikaṃ mārgaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ brāhmaṇaścaran |
iha siddhīśvarobhūtvā dehānte'mṛtamaśnute || 1-150 ||

	etena vacananicayena dakṣiṇe eva mārge brāhmaṇasya, mārgatraye'pi kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ, vāme kaule ca śūdrasyādhikāra iti vyavasthāpya siddhyādilobhādvāme kaule vā pravartamānasya brāhmaṇasya kevalaṃ bhoga eva na mokṣaḥ, tadubhayaṃ tu śrautamārgasaṃvalitatantrokta-dakṣiṇamārgeṇa samuciteṣṭadevatārādhakasyaiva brāhmaṇasyaiti siddhāntitam | na ca -

vāmācārairdvijaiḥ pūrvaṃ kartavyaṃ kuladīkṣaṇam |
paścāddīkṣā tu sāvitryā vyatyāsāt patito bhavet || 1-151 ||

	iti brāhmaṇānāṃ vāmamārgādhikāritvaprakāśakena merutantravacanena virodha iti vācyam | tatra dvijapadasya brāhmaṇetaradvija-paratvakalpanena virodhābhāvāt | ata eva -

brāhmaṇānāṃ vāmamārgo narakāyaiva jāyate |

	iti tantrāntaravacanāntaramapi saṃgacchate, adhikāriviśeṣānuṣṭhitasya vāmamārgasyāpi bhuktimuktijanakatayā brāhmaṇānāṃ tadadhikāritve tasya tannirayahetutvānupapatteḥ | tathā ca -

yo dākṣiṇyaṃ vinā vipro mahāmāyāṃ prapūjayet || 1-152 ||

sa pāpaḥ svargalokāttu cyuto bhavati rogadhṛk |

	iti kālikāpurāṇavacanāddakṣiṇamārge eva brāhmaṇasyādhikāra iti bodhyam | evaṃ ca kṣatriyavaiśyayorapi dakṣiṇamārga eva praśastaḥ, tayorvāmamārgādhikāritvakathanaṃ tu rāgāt (p. 24) pravṛttayorapi na pātityamityabhiprāyeṇa, brāhmaṇasyaiva tathāvidhasya tacchavaṇāt | vāmamārgastu śūdrāṇāmeva praśastaḥ |

śūdrādiyavanāntānāṃ siddhirvāmapathe sthitā || 1-153 ||

	iti merutantravacanāt | ācāramadhikṛtya -

vāmādinā yajecchūdro dakṣiṇena dvijātayaḥ |

iti mahākālavacanāt | dvijātaya iti, brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyā ityarthaḥ | vāmādinetyādiśabdena kulācāraḥ saṃgṛhyate, so'pi pūrvavacchūdrāṇāmeva praśastaḥ, kṣatriyavaiśyayorna praśasto nāpi niṣiddho, brāhmaṇānāṃ tu niṣiddha eva |

brāhmaṇyahānihetutvāt kulācāraṃ na cācaret || 1-154 ||

iti siddhāntasāravacanāt |

ye viprāḥ śāpanirdagdhā brahmakarmavivarjitāḥ |
teṣāṃ tu kauladharmo'yaṃ bhuktimuktyostu sādhanam || 1-155 ||

ye viprāḥ pāpasaṃjātāḥ śāpanirdagdhaviplutāḥ |
teṣāṃ kulādhikāro'yaṃ mārgasyāsya prajāyate || 1-156 ||

	ityādimerutantravacanena dakṣayajñe pāriṣadaśāpādvedamārga-bahirbhūtānāṃ vinaṣṭabrāhmaṇatvānāmeva viprāṇāṃ tadadhikāritvabodhanācca | yadi ca svatantravakturīśasya vacanānāṃ vaicitryād brāhmaṇānāmapi vāmakaulādhikāritvabodhakavacanāntara-mupalabhyeta tadā pañcasu lakṣaṇeṣu yatkiṃcillakṣaṇopalakṣitā hīnabrāhmaṇāstatra boddhavyāḥ, tādṛśānāṃ tu tadadhikāro'styeva |

p. 25) etacca merutantre eveśvaracaraṇairvyaktīkṛtam | yathā

brahmavīryaṃ1 tathā kṣetraṃ2 saṃskārā brahmasaṃbhavāḥ3 |
brāhmaṇācaraṇā4dbrahmavidyābhi5rbrāhmaṇo bhavet || 1-157 ||

yasya vaśyā vayaṃ yasya vācamartho'nudhāvati |
ekasmi/llakṣaṇe hīne vāgarthamanudhāvati || 1-158 ||

dvihīnalakṣaṇo devi svācārādbrāhmaṇo bhavet |
lakṣaṇatrayahīno yastantramārgeṇa siddhyati || 1-159 ||

caturlakṣaṇahīnānāṃ mārgāḥ syuḥ kaulikādayaḥ |

lakṣaṇahāniśca nirdeśakramaprātilomyena bodhyā |

tatraiva

pūrvoktalakṣaṇeṣvekaṃ yasmin vipre pradṛśyate || 1-160 ||

tapyate copatāpena kiṃcidbrāhmaṇatāṃ gataḥ |
adhikāro na me jātaḥ samastabrahmakarmaṇām || 1-161 ||

iha loke paratrāpi kathaṃ prāpsyāmyahaṃ sukham |
andhaśca yaḥ paradravye parastrīṣu napuṃsakaḥ || 1-162 ||

parāpavāde yo mūko nityamitthaṃ vicārayet |
tasyaiva brāhmaṇasyātra vāme syādadhikāritā || 1-163 ||

ekamiti, brahmavīryotpannatvam |

tathā

pañcalakṣaṇasaṃyukto brāhmaṇaḥ kāla eva saḥ |
ahaṃ brahmā tathā viṣṇustadājñāvaśavartinaḥ || 1-164 ||

sa cedvāmapathaṃ prāptaḥ punaḥ śvā cābhijāyate |
p. 26) caturlakṣaṇasaṃyukto vāmastasya phalapradaḥ || 1-165 ||

tābhyāṃ datto bhāgyavaśyāttāntrikaḥ ko'pi cenmanuḥ |
sadyaḥ phalati cādyasya kriyayā cāparasya tu || 1-166 ||

lakṣaṇatrayayukto yastasya vaidikatāntrike |
yathoktācaraṇādeva phaladastena vāmakaḥ || 1-167 ||

lakṣaṇadvayasaṃyuktastasya vāmaśca dakṣiṇaḥ |
samau tulyaphalau syātāṃ na kadācana vaidikaḥ || 1-168 ||

ekalakṣaṇasaṃyukto vāmastasya phalapradaḥ |
śūdrādiyavanāntānāṃ siddhirvāmapathe sthitā || 1-169 ||

vāmamārgasthito viprastulasīṃ na kvacit spṛśet |
na spṛśedvaiṣṇavaṃ vipraṃ praṇamenna ca vaidikam || 1-170 ||

nindantu bāndhavāḥ sarve tyajantu strīsutādayaḥ |
janā hasantu māṃ dṛṣṭvā rājāno daṇḍayantu vā || 1-171 ||

rogadāridryaduḥkhādyaiḥ pīḍito'pyaniśaṃ tvaham |
lakṣmīstiṣṭhatu vā yātu na muñcāmi padaṃ tvidam || 1-172 ||
evaṃ yasya dṛḍhā bhaktiḥ sa vāme siddhimāpnuyāt || 1-172ef ||

rudrayāmale

vāme candramukhī mukhe ca madirā pātraṃ karāmbhoruhe
mūrddhni śrīgurucintanaṃ bhagavatīdhyānāspadaṃ mānasam |
jihvāyāṃ japasādhanaṃ pariṇatiḥ kaulakramābhyāgame 
yeṣāṃ vai niyataṃ pibantu surasaṃ te bhuktimuktī gatāḥ || 1-173 ||

vāme rāmā ramaṇakuśalā dakṣiṇe cālipātram
agre mudrāścaṇakavaṭakāḥ śūraṇaścauṣṭhaśuddhiḥ |
p. 27) tantrīvīṇā sarasamadhurā sadguruḥ satkathāyāṃ
vāmācāraḥ paramagahano yogināmapyagamyaḥ || 1-174 ||

merutantre

peyaṃ madyaṃ palaṃ khādyaṃ samālokyaṃ priyāmukham |
ityevācaraṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ pāmarāḥ pravadanti ca || 1-175 ||

madyapānena manujo yadi siddhiṃ labheta cet |
madyapānaratāḥ sarve kiṃ siddhā na bhavantyume || 1-176 ||

māṃsabhakṣaṇamātreṇa yadi puṇyā gatirbhavet |
kravyādānāṃ tadā mokṣo māṃsādanyo na yujyate || 1-177 ||

strīsaṃbhogena bho devā yadi mokṣo'bhijāyate |
tadā sarve'pi muktāḥ syuḥ kasya janma bhaviṣyati || 1-178 ||

kṛpāṇadhārāgamanād vyāghrakarṇāvalambanāt |
bhujaṅgāvaraṇānnūnamaśakyaṃ kulavartmani || 1-179 ||

atha vaiṣṇavabhedāḥ |

taduktaṃ śaktisaṃgame

vaikhānaso1 bhavedādau śrīrādhāvallabha2stathā |
gaukuleśo3 maheśāni tathā vṛndāvanī4 bhavet || 1-180 ||

pāñcarātraḥ5 pañcamaḥ syāt ṣaṣṭhaḥ śrīvīravaiṣṇavaḥ6 |
rāmānandī7 harivyāsī8 nimbārka9śca maheśvari || 1-181 ||

tato bhāgavato10 devi daśa bhedāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
vaikhānasādidīkṣādyairbhūṣitaḥ smārttavaiṣṇavaḥ || 1-182 ||

vaiṣṇavācāranirato viṣṇumantraikapāragaḥ |
p. 28) ananyacetāḥ śāntātmā viṣṇucihnaparāyaṇaḥ || 1-183 ||

śrīrādhāvallabho devi gokuleśaṃ śṛṇu priye |
nānābharaṇasaṃpanno nānāsaugandhyabhūṣitaḥ || 1-184 ||

gavāṃ kulaṃ prīṇayataḥ kalikṛṣṇasya rūpadhṛk |
śarīramarthaṃ prāṇāṃśca tannivedanakārakaḥ || 1-185 ||

antaḥ śaktiparo devi bahirvaiṣṇavarūpadhṛk |
gandharvācāranirato latāveṣṭita tatparaḥ || 1-186 ||

saṃpradāyo gaukuleśaḥ sarvasiddhikaro bhuvi |
vigatāśaḥ prasannātmā viṣṇubhaktiparāyaṇaḥ || 1-187 ||

kāminīsaṅgacapalo vanakrīḍāvinodadhṛk |
saugandhyabhūṣitatanuḥ strīdhyānaikaparāyaṇaḥ || 1-188 ||

viṣṇusārūpyatattvajñaḥ prokto vṛndāvanī śive |
pañcarātravrataṃ prāptāḥ pāñcarātrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 1-189 ||

dinapañcakaparyantaṃ śaivā nāma trilocanāḥ |

gopālapaddhatau

kāyena manasā vācā bhajate viṣṇumeva yaḥ || 1-190 ||

sa vīravaiṣṇavaḥ prokto devatāntaranindakaḥ |
carācaramidaṃ viśvaṃ jānan nārāyaṇātmakam || 1-191 ||

kaṃ cinna dveṣṭi yaḥ so'yamuttamo vīravaiṣṇavaḥ |

tatraiva

hṛdayāmbhojamadhyasthe śrīrāme paramātmani || 1-192 ||

ānandaṃ bhajate yastu rāmānandī sa ucyate |

p. 29) śaktisaṃgame

pāpasaṃharaṇāsakto viṣṇubhakto jitendriyaḥ || 1-193 ||

yamādiniyamairyukto yuktācāraparāyaṇaḥ |
svopayuktaparigrāhī parakāryaparāyaṇaḥ || 1-194 ||

harivyāsī maheśāni śivaśaktisvarūpadhṛk |
nityārcanakramāśaktaḥ svatantraikaparāyaṇaḥ || 1-195 ||

bāhyapūjādinirato'nanyabhaktaḥ prasannadhīḥ |
āryapakṣānvitaḥ svacchaḥ svacchandācāratatparaḥ || 1-196 ||

svatantraḥ smārttavidveṣī nimbārko bhagavān hariḥ |
viṣṇubhaktaikanipuṇo vijitātmā prasannadhīḥ || 1-197 ||

smārttagarvānvito devi tadanyācāratatparaḥ |
śaivadveṣī tasya saṅgāt punaḥ snānaparāyaṇaḥ || 1-198 ||

kevalaṃ viṣṇubhaktijñaḥ prokto bhāgavataḥ śive |

paramabhāgavatalakṣaṇaṃ tu

ekādaśaskandhe

sarvabhūteṣu yaḥ paśyed bhagavadbhāvamātmanaḥ || 1-199 ||

bhūtāni bhagavatyātmanyeṣa bhāgavatottamaḥ |

anyacca tatraiva

gṛhītvāpīndriyairarthān yo na dveṣṭi na hṛṣyati || 1-200 ||
viṣṇormāyāmidaṃ paśyet sa vai bhāgavatottamaḥ || 1-200ef ||

tathā

tribhuvanavibhavahetave'pyakuṇṭha
smṛtirajitātmasurādibhirvimṛgyāt |
p. 30) na calati bhagavatpadāravindā-
llavanimiṣārddhamasau tu vaiṣṇavāgryaḥ || 1-201 ||

madhyamalakṣaṇaṃ tatraiva

īśvare tadadhīneṣu bāliśeṣu dviṣatsu ca |
premamaitrīkṛpopekṣā yaḥ karoti sa madhyamaḥ || 1-202 ||

prākṛtabhāgavatalakṣaṇaṃ tatraiva

na tadbhakteṣu cānyeṣu sa bhaktaḥ prākṛtaḥ smṛtaḥ || 1-203 ||

atha śaivabhedāḥ |

śaktisaṃgame

śikhī1 muṇḍī2 jaṭī3 caiva dvi4triḍaṇḍī5 krameṇa ca |
ekadaṇḍī6 maheśāni vīraśaiva7stathaiva ca || 1-204 ||

sapta pāśupatāḥ proktā daśadhā vaiṣṇavāstathā |
atha pāśupatānāṃ ca vāsanāṃ śṛṇu pārvati || 1-205 ||

śikhī pāśupato devi śikhāsūtradharaḥ śubhaḥ |
samādhitatparo devi samādhau śivapīṭhadhṛk || 1-206 ||

rudrākṣaṃ ca tathā bhasma liṅgaṃ śaivābhidhaṃ śive |
prasannātmā śāntacitto brahmacārī prakīrtitaḥ || 1-207 ||

mataṃ śrībindumālinyāḥ samāśritya pratiṣṭhitaḥ |
muṇḍī triṃśadvidho devi tārāsūkte prakāśitaḥ || 1-208 ||

tatra pāśupatācārasaṃpanno muṇḍito bhavet |
trijaṭaikajaṭādhārī tathā vakrajaṭādharaḥ || 1-209 ||

p. 31) nagnastapasvī śāntātmā dehakleśaparāyaṇaḥ |
karmatritayasaṃpanno jaṭī pāśupataḥ smṛtaḥ || 1-210 ||

dvidaṇḍī tu maheśāni vāṅmanodaṇḍasaṃyutaḥ |
kāyavāṅmanasāṃ daṇḍī tridaṇḍī parikīrtitaḥ || 1-211 ||

manomātrasya daṇḍī ya ekadaṇḍī prakīrtitaḥ |
kevalaṃ śivasaṃpannaḥ svasya devasya nindakaḥ || 1-212 ||

vīraśaiva iti khyātaḥ, iti |

	anyatra vaiṣṇavatvena prasiddhāstridaṇḍyādayaḥ śaivā api saṃbhavantīti darśitāḥ |

śrīśiva uvāca |

anyadharmasya cādveṣāt kṛtaṃ śuddhaṃ tu tāmasam |

athavā māraṇādyarthaṃ galitaṃ tattu tāmasam |

dīkṣāmāhātmyamāha

kulārṇave

upapātakalakṣāṇi mahāpātakakoṭikāḥ |
kṣaṇāddahati deveśi dīkṣā hi vidhinā kṛtā || 1-287 ||

rudrayāmale

dīkṣito brāhmaṇo yāti brahmalokaṃ sudhāmayam |
p. 40) aindraṃ lokaṃ kṣatriyo'pi prājāpatyaṃ viśastathā || 1-288 ||

yāti gandharvanagaraṃ śūdro dīkṣāprasādataḥ |
dīkṣāṃ vinā na mokṣaḥ syāt prāṇināṃ śivaśāsanāt || 1-289 ||

sā ca na syādvinācāryamityācāryaparamparā |

vaiśampāyanasaṃhitāyām

sarvasiddhikarī dīkṣā sarvasaṃpatpradāyinī || 1-290 ||

pratimāsvaravarṇāni kāntipuṣṭidhanāni ca |
mohanastambhanākṛṣṭimāraṇoccāṭanāni ca || 1-291 ||

vaśīkāraṃ ca kavitāṃ buddhiṃ vai dhāraṇāyutām |
tāraṇaṃ ca samudrasya vahneḥ śaityaṃ raṇe jayam || 1-292 ||

vipine cābhayaṃ caiva śokaduḥkhāpahaṃ tathā |
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṃśca dadātyeṣā na saṃśayaḥ || 1-293 ||

dīkṣāśabdārtha ukto

rudrayāmale

divyajñānaṃ yato dadyāt kuryāt pāpasya saṃkṣayam |
tena dīkṣeti loke'smin kīrtitā mantrapāragaiḥ || 1-294 ||

merutantre

dīyate jñānavijñānaṃ kṣīyante pāparāśayaḥ |
tena dīkṣeti hi proktā prāptā cet sadgurormukhāt || 1-295 ||

asadguruḥ svayaṃ naṣṭaḥ kathaṃ tārayate param |

āgamasāre

gurormukhānmahāvidyāṃ gṛhṇīyāt pāpanāśinīm || 1-296 ||

tasmādyatnād guruṃ kṛtvā mantrasādhanamācaret |

p. 41) (cāmuṇḍātantre)

upavidyāsu sarvāsu ṣaṭkarmādiprasādhane || 1-297 ||

nātra dīkṣādyapekṣāsti nātrāṅgādiprapūjanam |

	upavidyā yakṣiṇyādyāḥ | ṣaṭkarmādisādhanamantrāśca mahāmantrātiriktāḥ sādhanamantrāḥ | atra dīkṣāpadaṃ kriyāvatyādidīkṣāparam | upadeśadīkṣā tu sarvatrāvaśyakī |

pustake likhito mantro yena sundari japyate || 1-298 ||

na tasya jāyate siddhirhānireva pade pade |
gurumukhyāḥ kriyāḥ sarvā bhuktimuktiphalapradāḥ || 1-299 ||

gurvanuktāḥ kriyāḥ sarvā niṣphalāḥ syuryato dhruvam |

ityādivacanāt || na ca

śāstrādvā guruvaktrādvā jñātvā mantraṃ prasādhayet || 1-300 ||

	iti vacanāt pustakādapi mantrajñānaṃ siddhijanakamiti vācyam | gurorgṛhītamantraḥ sādhako mantrasādhanaprakāraṃ śāstrādguru-mukhādvā jñātvā mantraṃ sādhayedityasyaiva tadarthatvāt ||

mantrapadārthamāha (piṅgalāmate)

mananādviśvavijñānaṃ trāṇaṃ saṃsārabandhanāt |
yataḥ karoti saṃsiddhiṃ mantra ityucyate tataḥ || 1-301 ||

mantramāhātmyamāha (merutantre)

mantra eveśvaraḥ sākṣānmantra eva mahauṣadham |
nahi mantrāt paraṃ kaścit sarvasiddhipradāyakaḥ || 1-302 ||

tatraiva-

sādhakānāṃ phalaṃ dātuṃ tattadrūpaṃ dhṛtaṃ suraiḥ |
mukhyasvarūpaṃ teṣāṃ tu mantrā eva na cetarat || 1-303 ||

p. 42) (sārasaṃgrahe)

yathā deve tathā mantre yathā mantre tathā gurau |
yathā gurau tathā svātmanyevaṃ bhaktikramaḥ smṛtaḥ || 1-304 ||

gurumāhātmyamāha (rudrayāmale)

gurureva paro mantro gurureva paro japaḥ |
gurureva parā vidyā nāsti kiṃcidguruṃ vinā || 1-305 ||

yasya tuṣṭā gururdevi tasya tuṣṭā maheśvarī |
yena saṃtoṣito devi guruḥ sa hi sadāśivaḥ || 1-306 ||

tasmādguruṃ bhajedbhaktyā toṣayet satataṃ gurum |
guroryatra parīvādo nindā yatra pravarttate || 1-307 ||

karṇau tatra pidhātavyau yadvā dūraṃ palāyanam |

(brahmavaivartte)

yaiḥ śiṣyaiḥ śaśvadārādhyā guravo hyavamānitāḥ || 1-308 ||

putramitrakalatrādisaṃpadbhyaḥ pracyutā hi te |
ye gurudrohiṇo mūḍhāḥ satataṃ pāpakāriṇaḥ || 1-309 ||

teṣāṃ tu yāvat sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ syānna saṃśayaḥ |
prātikūlyaṃ gurornityaṃ manasā vandanaṃ caret || 1-310 ||

sadāśivadhiyā nityamekagrāmanivāsinam |
guruṃ vandeta ceddūre manasā vandanaṃ caret || 1-311 ||

brahmaviṣṇuśivādīnāṃ pūjane yat phalaṃ bhavet |
tat phalaṃ samavāpnoti pratyahaṃ vandane guroḥ || 1-312 ||

avidyo vā savidyo vā gurureva tu daivatam |
p. 43) amārgastho'tha mārgastho gurureva parā gatiḥ || 1-313 ||

yathā siddharasasparśāt tāmraṃ bhavati kāñcanam |
sannidhāne gurorevaṃ śiṣyaḥ śivamayo bhavet || 1-314 ||

tathā-

utpādakabrahmadātrorgarīyān brahmadaḥ pitā |
tasmānmanyet satataṃ piturapyadhikaṃ gurum || 1-315 ||

(vasantalalitabhairavyām)

divyajñānopadeṣṭāraṃ daiśikaṃ parameśvaram |
pūjayet parayā bhaktyā tasyājñāṃ naiva laṅghayet || 1-316 ||

yathā gurustathaiveśo yathaiveśastathā guruḥ |
pūjanīyāvubhau bhaktyā na bhedo vidyate tayoḥ || 1-317 ||

no dvaitavādaṃ kurvīta guruṇā saha kutracit |
advaitaṃ bhāvayedbhaktyā gurumīśaḥ sa jāyate || 1-318 ||

anyatrāpi

śive ruṣṭe gurustrātā gurau ruṣṭe na kaścana |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena gurumeva prasādayet || 1-319 ||

(merutantre)

ghṛṇā1 śaṅkā2 bhayaṃ3 lajjā4 jugupsā5 ceti pañcamī |
kulaṃ6 śīlaṃ7 tathā śakti8raṣṭau pāśāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 1-320 ||

pāśabaddhaḥ paśuḥ proktaḥ pāśamuktaḥ sadāśivaḥ |
tasmāt pāśaharo yo'tra sa gururnānya ucyate || 1-321 ||

tathā-

śaṅkayā bhakṣitaṃ sarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram |
sā śaṅkā bhakṣitā yena sa gururdurlabhaḥ kṣitau || 1-322 ||

p. 44) (kularatnāvalyām)

atrinetraṃ śivaḥ sākṣādacaturbāhuracyutaḥ |
acaturvadano brahmā śrīguruḥ kathitaḥ priye || 1-323 ||

sadāśivasya devasya śrīgurorapi pārvati |
ubhayorantaraṃ nāsti yaḥ karoti sa pātakī || 1-324 ||

yathā ghaṭaśca kalaśaḥ kumbhaścaikārthavācakāḥ |
tathā devaśca mantraśca guruścaikārtha ucyate || 1-325 ||

na guroḥ sadṛśaṃ vastu na devaḥ śaṅkaropamaḥ |
na ca kaulāt paro yogo na vidyā kālikāsamā || 1-326 ||

(merutantre)

gurubhaktivihīnasya tapo vidyā vrataṃ kulam |
vyarthaṃ sarvaṃ śavasyeva nānālaṅkārabhūṣaṇam || 1-327 ||

guruśabdārthamāha (āgamasāre)

gakāraḥ siddhidaḥ prokto rephaḥ pāpasya dāhakaḥ |
ukāraḥ śiva ityuktastritayātmā guruḥ paraḥ || 1-328 ||

dṛḍhavrataṃ sadācāraṃ śraddhābhaktisamanvitam |
kṛtajñaṃ pāpabhītaṃ ca sādhusajjanasaṃgamam || 1-329 ||

āstikaṃ dānaśīlaṃ ca sarvabhūtahite ratam |

gurulakṣaṇam (kulārṇave)

śrīguruḥ parameśāni śuddhavarṇo manoharaḥ || 1-330 ||

sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpannaḥ sarvāvayavaśobhitaḥ |
sarvāgamārthatattvajñaḥ sarvamantrapradhānavit || 1-331 ||

lokasaṃmohanākāro devavatpriyadarśanaḥ |
p. 45) iṅgatākāraceṣṭābhi rdūrataḥ kṛtadurjanaḥ || 1-332 ||

antarmukho bahirdṛṣṭiḥ sarvajño deśakālavit |
ājñāsiddhastrikālajño nigrahānugrahakṣamaḥ || 1-333 ||

vedavedāṅgavicchāntaḥ sarvajīvadayāparaḥ |
svādhīnendriyasaṃcāraḥ ṣaḍvargavijayakṣamaḥ || 1-334 ||

agragaṇyo'tigambhīraḥ pātrāpātraviśeṣavit |
nirmalo nityasaṃtuṣṭo nirdvandvo nityaśaktimān || 1-335 ||

sadbhaktavatsalo dhīraḥ kṛpāluḥ smitapūrvavāk |
bhaktipriyaḥ sarvasamo dayāluḥ śiṣyaśāsitā || 1-336 ||

sveṣṭadevaguruḥ prājño vanitāpūjanotsukaḥ |
nitye naimittike kāmye rataḥ karmaṇyanindite || 1-337 ||

rāgadveṣabhayakrodhadambhāhaṅkāravarjitaḥ |
svavidyānuṣṭhānatapovidyānāṃ ca prakāśakaḥ || 1-338 ||

yadṛcchālābhasaṃtuṣṭo guṇadoṣavibhedakaḥ |
strīdhanādiṣvanāsakto duḥsaṅgavyasanoñjhitaḥ || 1-339 ||

alolupo'hiṃsrakaścāpakṣapātī vicakṣaṇaḥ |
vittavidyādibhirmantrayantratantrādyavikrayī || 1-340 ||

niḥsaṃkalpo nirvikalpo nirṇītātmātidhārmikaḥ |
tulyanindāstutirmaunī nirapekṣo niyāmakaḥ || 1-341 ||

ityādilakṣaṇopetaḥ kathitaḥ śrīguruḥ priye |

atha niṣiddhagurulakṣaṇam (tattvasāre)

bahvāśī dīrghasūtrī ca viṣayādiṣu lolupaḥ || 1-342 ||

hetuvādarato duṣṭo vāgvādī gurunindakaḥ |
p. 46) aromā bahuromā ca ninditāśramasevakaḥ || 1-343 ||

kāladanto'sitoṣṭhaśca durgandhiśvāsavāhakaḥ |
duṣṭalakṣaṇasaṃpanno yadyapi svayamīśvaraḥ || 1-344 ||

bahupratigrahāsakta ācāryaḥ śrīkṣayāvahaḥ |

atha śiṣyalakṣaṇam (kulārṇave)

sacchiṣyaṃ taṃ kuleśāni sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutam || 1-345 ||

śamādisādhanopetaṃ guṇaśīlasamanvitam |
śuddhadehānvavāyajñaṃ dhārmikaṃ śuddhamānasam || 1-346 ||

viśvāsavinayopetaṃ vittaśāṭhyavivarjitam |
asādhyasādhakaṃ śūraṃ balakāntisamanvitam || 1-347 ||

anukūlakriyāyuktamapramattaṃ vicakṣaṇam |
hitasatyamitasnigdhabhāṣiṇaṃ muktadūṣaṇam || 1-348 ||

sakṛduktagṛhītārthaṃ caturaṃ buddhivistaram |
gṛhamatyāsavotsaṅga nirvikāramasevakam || 1-349 ||

vimṛśyakāriṇaṃ vīraṃ manodāridryavarjitam |
sarvakāryātikuśalaṃ dhīraṃ sarvopakārakam || 1-350 ||

atyarthaṃ paranindāyāṃ vimukhaṃ sumukhaṃ priye |
jitendriyaṃ susaṃtuṣṭaṃ dhīmantaṃ brahmacāriṇam || 1-351 ||

tyaktādhivyādhicāpalyaṃ duḥśaṅkātaṅkavarjitam |
gurudhyānastutikathāsevanābhajanotsukam || 1-352 ||

gurudaivatabhaktaṃ ca kāminī bhajanotsukam |
gurvājñāpālakaṃ devi gurukīrtipraśaṃsakam || 1-353 ||

p. 47) guruvākyapramāṇajñaṃ guruśuśruṣaṇe ratam |
cittānuvartinaṃ prekṣyakāriṇaṃ kulanāyike || 1-354 ||

lajjābhimānagarvādivarjitaṃ gurusannidhau |
nirapekṣaṃ gurudravye tatprasādābhikāṅkṣiṇam || 1-355 ||

gurudhyānādinirataṃ mokṣamārgābhikāṅkṣiṇam |
ityādilakṣaṇopetaṃ guruḥ śiṣyaṃ parigrahet || 1-356 ||

kutsitaśiṣyalakṣaṇaṃ tatraiva -

duṣṭānvavāyajaṃ duṣṭaṃ guṇahīnaṃ virūpitam |
paraśiṣyaṃ ca pākhaṇḍaṃ dhūrtaṃ paṇḍitamāninam || 1-357 ||

hīnādhikavikārāṅgaṃ vikalāvayavānvitam |
paṅgumandhaṃ ca vadhiraṃ malinaṃ vyādhipīḍitam || 1-358 ||

ucchiṣṭaṃ durmukhaṃ cāpi svecchāveśadharaṃ viṭam |
durbhidagdhaṃ kuceṣṭaṃ ca kuṭilaṃ bhīmavīkṣaṇam || 1-359 ||

nidrālasyayutaṃ tandrādyūtādivyasanānvitam |
kapāṭakuḍyastambhādau tirohitatanuṃ sadā || 1-360 ||

śūnyamuktakaraṃ kṣudraṃ śraddhābhaktivivarjitam |
kimekavādinaṃ stabdhaṃ dūṣakaṃ capalaṃ śaṭham || 1-361 ||

dhanastrīśuddhirahitaṃ niṣedhavidhivarjitam |
rahasyabhedakaṃ cāpi vedakāryārthaghātakam || 1-362 ||

mārjārabakavṛttiṃ ca randhrānveṣaṇatatparam |
māyānvitaṃ kṛtaghnaṃ ca pracchannāntaradāyakam || 1-363 ||

p. 48) viśvāsaghātaktaṃ devadrohiṇaṃ pāpakāriṇam |
aviśvāsamavidyāṅgamanarthasiddhikāṅkṣiṇam || 1-364 ||

ātatāyinamāditsuṃ kutsitaṃ kūṭasākṣiṇam |
sarvatra yācakaṃ devi sarvākṛṣṭābhigāminam || 1-365 ||

asatyaniṣṭhaṃ cāsaktaṃ grāmyādibahubhāṣiṇam |
durvicāravitarkādikārakaṃ kalahapriyam || 1-366 ||

vṛthākṣepakaraṃ bhrāntaṃ bhrāmakaṃ vāgviḍambakam |
parokṣe dūṣaṇakaraṃ pratyakṣe priyavādinam || 1-367 ||

vāgrukṣavādinaṃ vidyācauramātmapraśaṃsakam |
guṇāsahiṣṇuṃ sadbhītamatikrodhasamanvitam || 1-368 ||

cārvākaṃ durjanasakhaṃ sarvalokavigarhitam |
piśunaṃ parasaṃtāpaṃ sarvaprāṇibhayaṃkaram || 1-369 ||

akleśavādinaṃ mitradrohiṇaṃ bhrātṛvañcakam |
jihvopasthaparaṃ devi taskaraṃ paśuceṣṭhitam || 1-370 ||

akāraṇadveṣahāsyakleśakrodhādikāriṇam |
atihāsyasya kartāraṃ bhramāntaḥparihāsakam || 1-371 ||

kāmukaṃ cāṭinirlajjaṃ mithyāduśceṣṭasūcakam |
asūyāmadamātsaryadambhāhaṃkārasaṃyutam || 1-372 ||

īrṣyāpaiśunyapāruṣyakārpaṇyakrodhamāninam |
adhīraṃ duḥkhitaṃ dveṣyamaśaktaṃ tattvavarjitam || 1-373 ||

asiddhakumatiṃ mūḍhaṃ cintākulitamānasam |
tṛṣṇālobhayutaṃ dīnamatuṣṭaṃ sarvayācakam || 1-374 ||

p. 49) bahvāśinaṃ kapaṭinaṃ bhrāmakaṃ kuṭilaṃ priye |
bhaktiśraddhādayāśāntidharmācāravivarjitam || 1-375 ||

mātāpitṛguruprājñamahatāṃ hāsyakārakam |
kuladravyādivībhatsaṃ gurusevābhimāninam || 1-376 ||

strīduṣṭaṃ samayabhraṣṭaṃ guruśaptaṃ maheśvari |
ityādiviguṇopetaṃ guruḥ śiṣyaṃ parityajet || 1-377 ||

snehādvā lobhato vāpi yadi gṛhṇāti dīkṣayet |
tasmin gurau saśiṣye ca devatāśāpa āpatet || 1-377 ||

tasmādevaṃ vidhaṃ śiṣyaṃ na gṛhṇīyāt kadācana |
yadi gṛhṇāti mohena tatpāpaiḥ prāpyate guruḥ || 1-378 ||

mantripāpaṃ ca rājānaṃ patiṃ jāyākṛtaṃ yathā |
tathā śiṣyakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ prāyo gurumapi spṛśet || 1-379 ||

jñānena kṛpayā vāpi guruḥ śiṣyaṃ parīkṣayet |
saṃvatsaraṃ tadarddhaṃ vā tadarddhaṃ vā tadarddhataḥ || 1-380 ||

uttamānadhame kārye nīcānuttamakarmaṇi |
prāṇadravyapradānādyairādeśaiśca samāgamaiḥ || 1-381 ||

tanmarmasūcakairvākyairmāyābhiḥ krūraceṣṭitaiḥ |
pakṣapātairudāsīnairanekaiśca muhurmuhuḥ || 1-382 ||

āhṛṣṭaḥ pīḍitaścāpi yo viṣādaṃ na yāti ca |
guruḥ kṛpāṃ karotīti mudā saṃvedayet sadā || 1-383 ||

śrīgurusmaraṇe vāpi kīrttane cāpi vandane |
paricaryāyāmāhvāne preṣaṇe'nveṣaṇe priye || 1-384 ||

p. 50) ānandakamparomāñcasvaranetrādivikriyā |
yeṣāṃ syāt te na yogyāḥ syurmantrasiddhirapīśvari || 1-385 ||

(mantramuktāvalyām)

tayorvatsaramātreṇa jñātānyonyaṃ bhaviṣyati |
gurutā śiṣyatā vāpi nānyatheti viniścayaḥ || 1-386 ||

(merutantre)

dhanārthinastu guravaḥ śiṣyāḥ siddhyarthinaḥ kalau |
tasmānna cobhayoḥ siddhirdevatānāṃ viḍambanam || 1-387 ||

(liṅgāgame)

śiṣyaśca sadgurormantraṃ hṛhītvā taṃ parityajet |
gurutyāgakaraḥ śiṣyaḥ prāyaścittī bhaveddhruvam || 1-388 ||

(rudrayāmale)

gurutyāgādbhavenmṛtyurmantratyāgāddaridratā |
gurumantraparityāgādrauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet || 1-389 ||

kaulikena paśormantragrahaṇe tu tatparityāgo'pi devyāgame uktaḥ |

yadi daivāt paśorvidyā labhyate kulajairnaraiḥ |
dvijaṃ tu kaulikaṃ prāpya punarvidyāmupālabhet || 1-390 ||

yo gṛhṇāti paśordīkṣāṃ tasyādhirgātrapīḍanam |
bhavatyeva na saṃdeho bandhanaṃ caiva vā bhavet || 1-391 ||

vaṭapatre likhitvā ca tanmantraṃ śrotasi kṣipet |
sadguruṃ vā samāśritya gṛhṇīyāt saṃskṛtaṃ manum || 1-392 ||

(kulārṇave)

śrīguruṃ lakṣaṇopetaṃ saṃśayacchedakārakam |
p. 51) labdhvā jñānapradaṃ devi na gurvantaramāśrayet || 1-393 ||

anabhijñaṃ guruṃ prāpya saṃśayācchedakārakam |
śiṣyo gurvantaraṃ gatvā taddoṣairnānulipyate || 1-394 ||

(śaivāgame)

guravo bahavaḥ santi kumantrauṣadhavedinaḥ |
nigamāgamaśāstroktamantrajño durlabho bhuvi || 1-395 ||

suvijñaścoddharenmūrkhaṃ na mūrkho mūrkhamuddharet |
śilāṃ saṃtārayennaurhi kiṃ śilāṃ tārayecchilā || 1-396 ||

madhulubdho yathā bhṛṅgaḥ puṣpāt puṣpāntaraṃ vrajet |
jñānalubdhastathā śiṣyo gurorgurvantaraṃ vrajet || 1-397 ||

(vārāhītantre)

svanāmnā na guruḥ kāryo bhāryā ca mātṛnāmikā |


athoktalakṣaṇalakṣitasyāpi gurorviśeṣamāha |  (rudrayāmale)

vaiṣṇave vaiṣṇavo grāhyaḥ śaive śaivaśca śāktike || 1-398 ||

śaivaḥ śākto'pi sarvatra dīkṣāsvāmī na saṃśayaḥ |
vyutkrameṇa maheśāni mantrasiddhirbhavenna hi || 1-399 ||

na patnīṃ dīkṣayedbharttā na pitā dīkṣayet sutām |
na putraṃ ca tathā bhrātā bhrātaraṃ ca na dīkṣayet || 1-400 ||

na patnīṃ dīkṣayedbharteti paśukalpe | vīrakalpe tu -

vīrāṇāṃ kaulikānāṃ ca bhartā dīkṣaṇamācaret |
bharturdīkṣāpradāne tu na doṣaḥ kaulikāgame || 1-401 ||

śivena dīkṣā pūrvaṃ tu gauryai dattā śubhāvahā- ityuktam |

tathā-

piturmantraṃ na gṛhṇīyāt tathā mātāmahādapi || 1-402 ||

p. 52) sodarācca kaniṣṭhādvā vairipakṣāśritāt tathā |

(gaṇeśavimarṣiṇyām)

piturdīkṣā yaterdīkṣā dīkṣā ca vanavāsinaḥ || 1-403 ||

viviktāśramiṇo dīkṣā sā na kalyāṇadāyinī |

(yoginītantre)

nirvīryaṃ hi piturmantraṃ tathā mātāmahasya ca || 1-404 ||

sodarasya kaniṣṭhasya vairipakṣāśritasya ca |
pramādādvā tathājñānāt pitṛdīkṣāṃ yadācaret || 1-405 ||

prāyaścittaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā punardīkṣāṃ samācaret |

	atra piturityanādare ṣaṣṭhī tena pitrādiṣvabhaktirūpo'nādaro'tipari-cayādinā yadi bhavati tadā putrādinā mantro na grāhya ityarthaḥ | iti sudhārṇavakṛtaḥ |

matsyasūkte tu -

nirvīryaṃ hi piturmantraṃ śaive śākte na duṣyati - ityuktam || 1-406 ||

(tārākalpe)

kathamapi manumetaṃ prāpya śiṣyāya kasmai
nijakulatilakāya jyeṣṭhaputrāya dadyāt - idaṃ tārāviṣayakam | śrī vidyāviṣaye'pi na pitṛdīkṣāniṣedhaḥ | taduktaṃ (śrīkrame)

manurvimṛśya dātavyo jyeṣṭhaputrāya dhīmate |


svīyamantragrahaṇe tu guruvicāro na kartavyaḥ |


svīyamantropadeśe tu na kuryādgurucintanam || 1-407 || ||

iti bhairavatrantravacanāt |

tathā ca -

brahmaṇā kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ vaśiṣṭhāya mahātmane |
vasiṣṭho'pi svaputrāya matpitre dattavān svayam || 1-408 ||

p. 53) prasannahṛdayaḥ svacchaḥ pitā me karuṇānidhiḥ |
kurukṣetre mahātīrthe sūryaparvaṇi dattavān || 1-409 ||

	iti viṣṇumantramadhikṛtya vaiśampāyanasaṃhitāyāṃ śaunakaṃ prati vyāsavākyādviṣṇumantre'pi na pitṛdīkṣāniṣedhaḥ |

sa tūpadeśyaḥ putraśca vyatyayī ca sutastathā |

	iti nārāyaṇīyavacanādabhaktasyaiva putrasya pitṛdīkṣāniṣedhaḥ | iti saṃpradāyavidaḥ | vyatyayī mantrāntaraṃ datvā mantrāntaragrāhakaḥ |

(yoginītantre)

striyo dīkṣā śubhā proktā mātuścāṣṭaguṇā smṛtā || 1-410 ||

sādhvī caiva sadācārā gurubhaktā jitendriyā |
sarvamantrārthatattvajñā suśīlā pūjane ratā || 1-411 ||

guruyogyā bhavet sā hi vidhavā parivarjitā |

(vaiśampāyanasaṃhitāyām)

svapnalabdhaṃ striyā dattaṃ saṃskāreṇaiva siddhyati || 1-412 ||

saṃskāraśca tatraiva -

svapnalabdhe ca kalaśe guroḥ prāṇān niveśayet |
vaṭapatre kuṅkumena likhitvā grahaṇaṃ kṛtam || 1-413 ||

tataḥ siddhimavāpnoti viphalaṃ tvanyathā bhavet |

siddhamantragrahaṇe tu na kaścidvicāraḥ | taduktaṃ (śaktiyāmale)

siddhamantro yadi bhavet tadā patnīṃ sudīkṣayet || 1-414 ||

śaktitvena varārohe na ca sā putrikā bhavet |
pitā tathāvidhaḥ putryai tathā dīkṣāṃ samācaret || 1-415 ||

bhrātā siddhamanurbhūyāt gururbhrātustu śaktitaḥ |
siddhamantro naraḥ sarvamayogyaṃ yogyatāṃ nayet || 1-416 ||

p. 54) (mahākapilapañcarātre)

na mantre cādhikāro'sti śūdrāṇāṃ niyamaḥ paraḥ |
mantrābhāvādamantreṇa bhāṣitaṃ sarvakarma hi || 1-417 ||

(sātātapasaṃhitāyām)

yāvantyarṇāni mantrāṇāṃ strīśūdrādeḥ pradāpayet |
tāvatyo brahmahatyāḥ syuḥ svayamāha prajāpatiḥ || 1-418 ||

iti vaidikādimantraparam ||

(bhaviṣyottare)

brāhmaṇā kṣatriyā vaiśyāḥ śūdrā ye śucayo'malāḥ |
teṣāṃ mantrāḥ pradeyā vai na tu saṃkīrṇavarṇinām || 1-419 ||

(devīyāmale)

svāhāpraṇavasaṃyuktaṃ mantraṃ śūdre dadaddvijaḥ |
śūdro nirayagāmī syādvipraḥ śūdratvamāpnuyāt || 1-420 ||

(brahmapurāṇe)

vaidiko miśrito vāpi viprādīnāṃ vidhīyate |
tāntriko viprabhaktasya śūdrasyāpi prakīrtitaḥ || 1-421 ||

(bhāgavate 11 skandhe)

vaidikastāntriko miśra iti me trividho makhaḥ |
trayāṇāmīpsitenaiva vidhinā māṃ samarccayet || 1-422 ||

(sāmbapurāṇe)

śrutibhraṣṭaḥ śrutiproktaprāyaścitte bhayaṃ gataḥ |
krameṇa śrutisiddhyarthaṃ brāhmaṇastaṃ samāśrayet || 1-423 ||

pañcarātraṃ bhāgavataṃ tantraṃ vaikhānasābhidham |
vedabhraṣṭān samuddiśya kamalāpatiruktavān || 1-424 ||

(ācārakusumāvalyām)

brahmavīryasamutpanno brāhmaṇīgarbhasaṃbhavaḥ |
p. 55) vedoktāśeṣasaṃskārasaṃskṛtaḥ sarvadā śuciḥ || 1-425 ||

adhītavedavedāṅgo vedāntārthavicakṣaṇaḥ |
trikālavihitasnānasaṃdhyopāsanatarpaṇaḥ || 1-426 ||

nityaṃ sahasragāyatrījāpako vaiśvadevakṛt |
agnihotrī sāvadhāno daive paitre ca karmaṇi || 1-427 ||

svakarmacyutibhītaśca bhītaḥ kālavyatikramāt |
prāyaścitteṣvabhītaśca sadācāreṣu sādaraḥ || 1-428 ||

garhitācāravimukhaḥ śānto dāntaḥ prasannadhīḥ |
ityādilakṣaṇopetaḥ kathito vaidiko budhaiḥ || 1-429 ||

etadanyabrāhmaṇā ye vedabhraṣṭāśca te matāḥ |
vedabhraṣṭadvijātīnāṃ miśro'dhvā siddhidaḥ kalau || 1-430 ||

vedokta eva mārgaḥ syādvaidikasya dvijasya ca |
parityaktaśrautamārgaḥ patitaḥ kathyate dvijaḥ || 1-431 ||

(nāradapañcarātre)

strīśūdrebhyo manuṃ dadyāt svāhāpraṇavavarjitam |

(kulārṇave)

strīśūdrāṇāmayaṃ mantro namo'ntaśca sukhāvahaḥ || 1-432 ||

etajjñātvā maheśāni cāṇḍālānapi dīkṣayet |

(yājñavalkyasaṃhitāyām)

na vaidikaṃ japecchūdraḥ striyaścaiva kadācana || 1-433 ||

namo'ntaṃ śaivamantraṃ vā vaiṣṇavaṃ ceṣyate budhaiḥ |

(merutantre)

mohādvā kāmataḥ śūdraḥ purāṇaṃ saṃhitāṃ smṛtim || 1-434 ||

paṭhannarakamāpnoti pitṛbhiḥ saha pāpakṛt |
p. 56) yaḥ śūdro buddhipūrvaṃ tu śṛṇuyācchrāvayeddvijaḥ || 1-435 ||

vedaṃ tau duḥkhitau sto'smin bhave'nyatra tu nārakau |
adhikāramadādyastu dhanasya madato'pi vā || 1-436 ||

śūdrastu śṛṇuyādvedaṃ sa nirvaṃśaśca nirdhanaḥ |
ajñānāccecchuto vedaḥ śrāvito vā dvijanmanā || 1-437 ||

prājāpatyatrayaṃ kṛtvā tau dvau śuddhau bhaviṣyataḥ |


bhūtaśuddhitantroktāni tantracintāmaṇilikhitavacanāni |


praṇavoccāraṇāddhomācchālagrāmaśilārcanāt || 1-438 ||

brāhmaṇīgamanāccaiva śūdraścāṇḍālatāṃ vrajet |
śūdrasya praṇavoccāraṃ purāṇāsaṃmataṃ priye || 1-439 ||

tasmādyatnena kartavyaṃ tantroktaṃ śūdrajātinā |
mahāvidyāprabhāveṇa śūdro vaiśyatvamāpnuyāt || 1-440 ||

praṇavādyaṃ maheśāni gṛhṇīyuḥ śūdrajātayaḥ |
etaddīkṣāparaṃ tattvaṃ sugopyaṃ parameśvari || 1-441 ||

(rudrayāmale)

pravṛtte bhairavītantre sarve varṇā dvijātayaḥ |
nivṛtte bhairavī tantre sarve varṇāḥ pṛthak pṛthak || 1-442 ||

atra dvijātaya iti varṇadvayajātāḥ | kuṇḍādaya iti nāgojibhaṭṭāḥ |

(sautrāmaṇitantre)

māyābījaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ syācchrībījaṃ kṣatriyaḥ smṛtaḥ |
kāmabījaṃ bhavedvaiśyo vāgbhavaṃ śūdra īritaḥ || 1-443 ||

caturbījaparityakto mantraḥ paulastyasaṃjñakaḥ |
caturbījaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ tribījakam || 1-444 ||

bījadvayaṃ tu vaiśyānāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ tvekabījakam |

p. 57) (brahmapurāṇe)

oṃkārādisamāyuktaṃ namaskārāntamīritam |
svanāma sarvasattvānāṃ mantra ityabhidhīyate || 1-445 ||

(yāmale)

svatārārāśikoṣṭhānāmanukūlān bhajenmanūn |
śiṣyānukūlaṃ cakrebhyo jñātvā dadyānmanuṃ guruḥ || 1-446 ||

atha cakravicāraḥ | taduktam (bhuvaneśvarī tantre)

tārāśuddhirvaiṣṇavānāṃ kośaśuddhiḥ śivasya ca |
rāśiśuddhistraipure ca gopāle'kaḍamaḥ smṛtaḥ || 1-447 ||

akaḍamo vāmanasya gaṇeśe dantacakrakam |
dantacakraṃ varāhasya mahālakṣmyāḥ kulākule || 1-448 ||

nāmādicakre sarveṣāṃ bhūtacakre tathaiva ca |
doṣān saṃśodhya gṛhṇīyādguruvākyena jalpakaḥ || 1-449 ||

(vārāhasaṃhitāyām)

tārācakraṃ rāśicakraṃ nāmacakraṃ tathaiva ca |
atra cet saguṇo mantro nānyacakraṃ vicārayet || 1-450 ||

atha vakṣye vicāro'yaṃ cakrāṇāṃ siddhihetave |
tatastu prathamaṃ vakṣye svakulākulacakrakam || 1-451 ||

kāraṇāt pañcabhūtānāmudbhūtā mātṛkā yataḥ |
ato bhūtātmakā varṇāḥ pañcapañcavibhāgataḥ || 1-452 ||

vāyvagnibhūjalākāśāḥ pañcāśallipayaḥ kramāt |
pañca hrasvāḥ pañca dīrghā vindvantāḥ saṃdhisaṃbhavāḥ || 1-453 ||

pañcaśaḥ kādayaḥ ṣakṣalasahāntāḥ samīritāḥ |

	pañcapañceti daśavarṇā ityarthaḥ | vāyvagnibhūjalākāśā iti | a-ā-ekacaṭatapayaṣā mārutāḥ | i-ī-r-ekhachaṭhathapharakṣā āgneyāḥ | u-ū-ogajaḍadabalalāḥ bhaumāḥ |

p. 58) ṛ-ṝ aughajhaḍhadhabhavasā jalīyāḥ | ḷ ḹ aṃṅañaṇanamaśahā nābhasāḥ | pañcahrasvā iti a i u ṛ ḷ | pañcadīrghā iti ā ī ū ṝ ḹ | vindvantā iti vindurante yasya tena aṃ ityarthaḥ | saṃdhisaṃbhavā iti e ai o au |

svanāmādyakṣarāṇāṃ tu mitraṃ vā svakulaṃ varam || 1-454 ||

tadyathā -

sādhakasyākṣaraṃ pūrvaṃ mantrasyāpi tathākṣaram |
yadyekabhūtadaivatyaṃ jānīyāt svakulaṃ hi tat || 1-455 ||


mitramiti parasparamaitram | taduktam (dīpikāyām)


pārthivaṃ vāruṇaṃ maitramāgneyasyāpi mārutam |
pārthivānāmakṣarāṇāṃ śatrutve mārutaṃ sthitam || 1-456 ||

āgneyasyāmbhasaṃ śatrurambhasastaijasaṃ smṛtam |
eteṣāmeva sarveṣāmavirodhi tu nābhasam || 1-457 ||

parasparaviruddhānāṃ na kuryānmelanaṃ sudhīḥ |
uktaṃ kulākulaṃ cakraṃ samastapuruṣārthakam || 1-458 ||

p. 59) vakṣye akathahaṃ cakraṃ prāglikheccaturasrakam |
madhye rekhādvayaṃ dadyāt prāgagramudagagragam || 1-459 ||

caturasraṃ bhavet tatra catuṣkoṣṭhasamanvitam |
pūrvāparāyataṃ dadyānmadhye rekhādvayaṃ tataḥ || 1-460 ||

udagdakṣiṇadīrghaṃ ca dadyādrekhādvayaṃ tataḥ |
tataḥ ṣoḍaśakoṣṭhāḍhyaṃ jāyate cakramuttamam || 1-461 ||

ādikṣāntā/llikhedvarṇān krameṇānena mantravit |

ādau catuścatuṣkoṣṭhe siddhādīn parikalpayet |
pradakṣiṇakramādevaṃ svanāmākṣaramādyataḥ || 1-463 ||

siddhasādhyasusiddhārikrameṇa gaṇayedbudhaḥ |
siddhādīn siddhidaḥ siddho japāt sādhyo hutādibhiḥ || 1-464 ||

susiddhaḥ prāptimātreṇa sādhakaṃ bhakṣayedariḥ |
siddhārṇā bāndhavā jñeyā sādhyāste sevakā matāḥ || 1-465 ||

susiddhāḥ poṣakāścaivaṃ śatravo ghātakāḥ smṛtāḥ |

(piṅgalāmate)

siddhaḥ siddhyati kālena sādhyastu japahomataḥ || 1-466 ||

susiddho grahaṇādeva śatrurmūlāni kṛntati |

(merutantre)

ādau jñeyaṃ catuṣkeṣu siddhādyaṃ vidiśāsu ca || 1-467 ||

p. 60) tatsthānasamakoṣṭhāt tu punargaṇyaṃ gurūktitaḥ |

(piṅgalāmate)

siddhādīn śodhayenmantrī punaḥ siddhādibhiḥ smṛtaḥ || 1-468 ||

siddhisiddho japāt siddhirdviguṇāt siddhisādhyakaḥ |
siddhaḥ susiddho'rdhajapāt siddhārirhanti bāndhavān || 1-469 ||

sādhyasiddho dviguṇakaḥ sādhyasādhyo nirarthakaḥ |
sādhyasusiddho'rdhajapāt sādhyārirhanti gotrajān || 1-470 ||

susiddhasiddho'rdhajapāt phalaṃ dadyādyathepsitam |
susiddhasādhyaṃ satataṃ japāt siddhirna cānyathā || 1-471 ||

susiddhasiddho yo mantraḥ pūrvajanmakṛtaśramaḥ |
tasmāt taṃ sarvasiddhīnāṃ sādhane yojayenmanum || 1-472 ||

susiddhārirviśeṣeṇa svakulaṃ nāśayeddhruvam |
arisiddhaḥ sutaṃ hanti arisādhyastu kanyakām || 1-473 ||

patnīmapi susiddhastu aryarirmūlaghātakaḥ |

p. 61) atha ṛṇidhanicakram | tatra mantrāṇāṃ sādhakānāṃ ṛṇitve dhanitve ca hetumāha (tantrarāje)

pūrvajanmakṛtābhyāsāt pāpādasyāphalāptikṛt || 1-474 ||

pāpe naṣṭe phalāvāptiḥ kāle dehakṣayādṛṇī |
mantrasaṃprāptimātreṇa prāk pūtaḥ siddhaye bhavet || 1-475 ||

siddhamantrān gurorlabdhvā mantrī yatsiddhibhāgnaraḥ |
lakṣmīvān sa sadā pūjyo mantrabhogamavāpnuyāt || 1-476 ||

sa mantro'sya ṛṇī jñeyo bhajanaṃ tasya pūrvagam |
tasmādṛṇaviśuddhistu kāryā sarvaistu sarvataḥ || 1-477 ||

taduktam (sundarīrahasyavṛttau)

pūrvāparāyataṃ lekhyaṃ rekhādvādaśakaṃ kramāt |
krameṇaivātra jāyante koṣṭhā ekādaśa kramāt || 1-478 |


teṣu varṇā/llikhenmantrī svarādikramayogataḥ |

p. 62) sādhyāṅkān sādhakāṅkā/śca pūrayedgṛhasaṃkhyayā |
nāmākṣarādakathahādgajabhaktaśeṣaṃ

mantro yadyadhikāṅkaḥ syāt tadā mantraṃ japet sudhīḥ |
same'pi ca japenmantrī na japet tadṛṇādhike || 1-482 ||

(merutantre)

ṛṇī tu sādhako jñeyaḥ sādhakāṅko yadādhikaḥ |
yāvat syādadhikastāvatsaṃkhyākāni yadācaret || 1-483 ||

tadaṅkaṃ kathayāmyatra ekādaśagṛhasthitam || 1-485 ||

iti ṛṇidhanicakram |

atha vargacakram | taduktam (tantrarāje)

mantrāṇāmariśodhārthaṃ nāmādyakṣarapūrvakam |
aṣṭau koṣṭhān samālikhya vargānaṣṭau likhet tataḥ || 1-487 ||

pakṣimārjārapañcāsyaśvānaḥ sarpākhudantinaḥ |
meṣa kramādakārādivasuvargayutā ime || 1-488 ||

pakṣimārjārayorvairaṃ vairaṃ mārjārapakṣiṇoḥ |

(vārāhītantre)

apavādamāha (yāmale)

yadi mantrā na śobhante vicāreṇa kathaṃcana |
tadā niṣedhamāśritya gṛhṇīyānmantramuttamam || 1-535 ||

nṛsiṃhārkavarāhāṇāṃ prāsādapraṇavasya ca |

ekākṣaradvyakṣarāṇāṃ siddhādīn naiva śodhayet || 1-537 ||

(agnipurāṇe)

haṃsasyāṣṭākṣarasyāpi tathā pañcākṣarasya ca |
napuṃsakasya mantrasya siddhādīnnaiva śodhayet || 1-538 ||

napuṃsako namo'ntaḥ |

p. 73) taduktam (śāradāyām)

mātṛkāvarṇabhedebhyaḥ sarve mantrāḥ prajajñire |
mantravidyāvibhedena dvividhā mantrajātayaḥ || 1-539 ||

mantrāḥ puṃdevatā jñeyā vidyāḥ strī devatāḥ smṛtāḥ |
puṃstrīnapuṃsakātmāno mantrāḥ sarve samīritāḥ || 1-540 ||

puṃmantrā hū/phaḍantāḥ syurdviṭhāntāḥ syuḥ striyo matāḥ |
napuṃsakā namo'ntāḥ syurityuktā manavastridhā || 1-541 ||

(sanatkumārasaṃhitāyām)

siddhaḥ sādhyaḥ susiddhaśca ariścaiva ca nārada |
gopāleṣu na boddhavyaḥ svaprakāśo yataḥ svayam || 1-542 ||

(gaṇeśavimarṣiṇyām)

ekākṣare tathā kūṭe mālāmantre tribījake |
svapnalabdhe striyā datte siddhādīnnaiva śodhayet || 1-543 ||

mālāmantralakṣaṇaṃ ca (nārāyaṇī tantre)

viṃśatyarṇādhikā mantrā mālāmantrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |

(devī pureṇe)

kālikāyāṃ ca tārāyāṃ kullukāyāṃ trikūṭake || 1-544 ||

tathaikākṣarakūṭe ca śivapañcākṣare'pi ca |
ṣaḍakṣare tu tasyaiva viṣṇoraṣṭākṣare'pi vā || 1-545 ||

viṣṇordvādaśavarṇe ca rāmamantre'tha tārake |
vaiṣṇaveṣu ca bhairavyāṃ sundarī mantravaryake || 1-546 ||

sārasvate ca bālāyāṃ siddhādīnnaiva śodhayet |

p. 74) (gautamīye)

sarveṣu mantravarṇeṣu śreṣṭho vaiṣṇava ucyate || 1-547 ||

vaiṣṇaveṣu ca sarveṣu kṛṣṇamantrāḥ phalādhikāḥ |
viśeṣato daśārṇo'yaṃ japamātreṇa siddhyati || 1-548 ||

striyā dīkṣā śubhā proktā mātuścāṣṭaguṇādhikā |
svapnalabdhā ca yā vidyā tatra nāsti vicāraṇā || 1-549 ||

(yoginī tantre)

gāruḍādiṣu saureṣu vaiṣṇave bauddhajainayoḥ |
mahāmantreṣu kūṭeṣu naiva siddhādiśodhanam || 1-550 ||

bibhratyadbhutabhūtibhūtapavacovaicitryacitrāmṛta-
srotaḥśālini sannibandhanasaritsaṃbhedamabhyarhitam |
sadyuktisphuṭamauktikotkararucottuṅgastaraṅgo gataḥ
ādyaḥ śrīnaradevanirmitapuraścaryārṇave prasphuran || 1-551 ||

	iti śrīmanmahārājādhirājapratāpasiṃhasāhaviracite puraścaryārṇave ṣaḍāmnāyadevatātadācārādinirṇayapūrvakacakravivecanaṃ nāma prathamastaraṅgaḥ |

atha dīkṣākālanirṇayaḥ |

tatrādau māsaphalam | taduktam (yāmale)

vaiśākhe tu śivā dīkṣā vipulāṃ śriyamādiśet |
jyeṣṭhe mṛtyupradā caiva āṣāḍhe sukhasampadau || 2-1 ||

śrāvaṇe ratnalābhaḥ syādbhādre duḥkhapradā matā |
āśvine sarvasiddhiḥ syāt kārtike jñānasiddhidā || 2-2 ||

śubhakṛnmārgaśīrṣe ca pauṣe jñānavināśinī |
medhāvṛddhikarī māghe phālgune sarvavaśyakṛt || 2-3 ||

caitre ca pīḍājananī malamāsaṃ vivarjayet |

(siddhāntaśekhare)

śaratkāle ca vaiśākhe dīkṣā śreṣṭhaphalapradā || 2-4 ||

phālgune mārgaśīrṣe ca jyeṣṭhe dīkṣā ca madhyamā |
āṣāḍhe māghamāse ca kaniṣṭhā sā prakīrtitā || 2-5 ||

ninditaścaitramāsastu pauṣo bhādrapadastathā |


gopālamantradīkṣāyāṃ tu caitro'pi praśastaḥ | taduktaṃ gautamena |


mantrārambhastu caitre syāt samastapuruṣārthadaḥ || 2-6 ||

vaiśākhe ratnalābhaḥ syāt jyeṣṭhe tu maraṇaṃ dhruvam |
āṣāḍhe bandhunāśaḥ syāt pūrṇāyuḥ śrāvaṇe bhavet || 2-7 ||

prajānāśo bhavedbhādre āśvine ratnasaṃcayaḥ |
kārtike mantrasiddhiḥ syāt mārgaśīrṣe tathā bhavet || 2-8 ||

pauṣe tu śatrupīḍā syānmāghe medhāvivardhanam |
phālgune sarvakāmāḥ syurmalamāsaṃ vivarjayet || 2-9 ||

p. 76) atha pakṣaphalam | taduktam (rudrayāmale)

śuklapakṣe śubhā dīkṣā kṛṣṇe'pyāpañcamīdinam |
kṣīṇendau vratadīkṣāṃ ca na kuryānmantravittamaḥ || 2-10 ||

kṛṣṇāṣṭamīmārabhya śuklacaturdaśīṃ yāvat kṣīṇacandraḥ |
atha tithiphalam | taduktam (yāmale)

pratipatsaṃskṛtā dīkṣā jñānanāśakarī matā |
dvitīyāyāṃ bhavejjñānaṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ sukhī bhavet || 2-11 ||

caturthyāṃ dhananāśaḥ syāt pañcamyāṃ buddhivardhanam |
ṣaṣṭhyāṃ jñānakṣayaṃ saukhyaṃ labhate saptamīdine || 2-12 ||

aṣṭamyāṃ buddhināśaḥ syānnavamyāṃ vapuṣaḥ kṣayaḥ |
daśamyāṃ rājasaubhāgyamekādaśyāṃ śucirbhavet || 2-13 ||

dvādaśyāṃ sarvasiddhiḥ syāt trayodaśyāṃ daridratā |
tiryagyoniścaturdaśyāṃ kṛmirmāsāvasānake || 2-14 ||

pakṣānte sarvasiddhiḥ syādasvādhyāyaṃ vivarjayet |
sandhyāgarjananirghoṣe bhūkampolkānipātane || 2-15 ||

trirātraṃ varjayenmantrī śrutyuktānapi varjayet |

(tattvasārasaṃhitāyām)

tāṃ tāṃ tithiṃ samālocya tadbhaktā/statra dīkṣayet || 2-16 ||

brahmaṇaḥ pūrṇamāsyuktā dvādaśī cakradhāriṇaḥ |
caturdaśī śivasyoktā vācaḥ proktā trayodaśī || 2-17 ||

dvitīyā ca śriyāḥ proktā pārvatyāśca tṛtīyakā |
caturthī gaṇanāthasya bhānoḥ proktā ca saptamī || 2-18 ||

nityāmārgeṣu pārvatyā aṣṭamī ca caturdaśī |

	atha māsāntapakṣāntāvityuktatvānmāsāntaṃ saṃkrāntergatadinaṃ pakṣāntaṃ pūrṇamāsyavāsye | taduktam (gautamīye)

p. 77) sauramāsi śubhā dīkṣā na cāndre na ca tārake || 2-19 ||

iti vacanānmāsāntaṃ sauramāsāntaṃ jñeyam |
atha vāraphalam |

ravivāre bhavedvittaṃ some śāntirbhavet kila |
āyuraṅgārake hanti tatra dīkṣāṃ vivarjayet || 2-20 ||

budhe saundaryamāpnoti jñānaṃ syāt tu bṛhaspatau |
śukre saubhāgyamāpnoti yaśohāniḥ śanaiścare || 2-21 ||

atha nakṣatraphalam | taduktam (tripurārṇave)

aśvinyāṃ sukhamāpnoti bharaṇyāṃ maraṇaṃ dhruvam |
kṛttikāyāṃ bhavedduḥkhī rohiṇyāṃ vākpatirbhavet || 2-22 ||

mṛgaśīrṣe sukhāvāptirārdrāyāṃ bandhunāśanam |
punarvasau dhanāḍhyaḥ syāt puṣye śatruvināśanam || 2-23 ||

āśleṣāyāṃ bhavenmṛtyurmaghāyāṃ duḥkhamocanam |
saundaryaṃ pūrvaphālgunyāṃ prāpnotīti na saṃśayaḥ || 2-24 ||

jñānaṃ cottaraphālgunyāṃ hastāyāṃ ca balī bhavet |
citrāyāṃ jñānasiddhiḥ syāt svātyāṃ śatruvināśanam || 2-25 ||

viśākhāyāṃ bhavet saukhyamanurādhā ca buddhidā |
jyeṣṭhāyāṃ sutahāniḥ syānmūlāyāṃ kīrtivardhanam || 2-26 ||

pūrvāṣāḍhottarāṣāḍhe bhavetāṃ kīrtidāyike |
śravaṇāyāṃ bhavedduḥkhī dhaniṣṭhāyāṃ daridratā || 2-27 ||

buddhiḥ śatabhiṣāyāṃ syāt pūrvabhādre sukhī bhavet |
saukhyaṃ cottarabhādre syādrevatyāṃ kīrtivardhanam || 2-28 ||

(ratnāvalyām)

pratipatpūrvāṣāḍhā ca pañcamīkṛttikā tathā |
p. 78) pūrvabhādrapadā ṣaṣṭhyāṃ daśamīrohiṇī tathā || 2-29 ||

dvādaśyāṃ sārpanakṣatramaryamṇā ca trayodaśī |
nakṣatraluptā ityete devānāmapi nāśadā || 2-30 ||

atha lagnaphalam (rājamārtaṇḍe)

(rudrayāmale)

vṛṣe siṃhe ca kanyāyāṃ dhanurmīnākhyalagnake |

(merutantre)

sadā duḥkhaṃ sadā hānirvāmināṃ mithune śubham |

(smṛtisaṃhitāyām)

(gautamīye saubhāgyaratnākare ca)

triṃśatkalāḥ śataṃ vedakāṣṭhāvarṇāstu yatra tu || 2-127 ||

sakṛddhi ete kālena śaptā duḥkhāni kurvate |
navārṇa o/kārayutaścāmuṇḍāśāpato manuḥ || 2-128 ||

nistriṃśaḥ sādhakaṃ chindyānnistriṃśena na saṃśayaḥ |
p. 88) ante namo vā svāhā vā nāsti vā yasya madhyataḥ || 2-129 ||

vaṣaṭ vauṣaṭ sahū/phaṭ ca etadvā nāstyasau manuḥ |
nirbīja iti saṃśapto bhuvaneśyāsya sādhakaḥ || 2-130 ||

sevāṃ kuryāllabhedvaṃśanāśaṃ vaṃśatrayāditaḥ |
ādimadhyāvasāneṣu phaṭphaṭkārastu yasya tu || 2-131 ||

kaumāryastu sa śāpena siddhihīno na siddhikṛt |
daśākṣaro yastu mantraḥ sa gaṇeśasya śāpataḥ || 2-132 ||

mando japtastu mūrkhatvaṃ kuryājjanmani janmani |
ādivyañjanasaṃyoga eka eva svaro bhavet || 2-133 ||

tat kūṭaṃ kūṭato hānirmātaṅgyāḥ śāpataḥ smṛtaḥ |
dvivarṇaḥ sattvahīnaḥ syādāśvineyasya śāpataḥ || 2-134 ||

nāśayet sādhakabalaṃ rājñaḥ sainyaṃ vināśayet |
caturvarṇaḥ kekarastu jātaḥ sūryātiśāpataḥ || 2-135 ||

japitvā kekaraṃ mantraṃ cakṣuṣā naiva paśyati |
ṣaḍakṣarāṇi bījahīnā jātā rāvaṇaśāpataḥ || 2-136 ||

nāśayet sādhakabalaṃ rājñaḥ sainyaṃ vināśayet |
vaṃśavṛddhirna tajjāpādanyathā strīsukhaṃ na hi || 2-137 ||

sārdhatrisaptasūryārṇā dhūmāvatyāśca śāpataḥ |
dhūmatāvyākulaṃ cittaṃ jāyate sādhakasya tu || 2-138 ||

viṃśat tathaikaviṃśacca triṃśadvarṇastu tārayā |
śapta āliṅgitaścāyaṃ japāt svapnarate mṛtiḥ || 2-139 ||

dvāviṃśatyakṣaro mantraḥ sarasvatyāstu śāpataḥ |
mohitaṃ sādhakaṃ kuryāt pānādivyasanākulam || 2-140 ||

p. 89) caturviṃśativarṇo yaḥ saptaviṃśativarṇakaḥ |
kṣudhārto rājamātaṅgyāḥ śāpāddāridryakārakaḥ || 2-141 ||

trayoviṃśāstrayo rudrā varṇāḥ kaulikaśāpataḥ |
mantrā dṛptāstajjapena chāndaso jāyate janaḥ || 2-142 ||

ṣadviṃśadekonatriṃśat triṃśat ṣaṭtriṃśadakṣaraḥ |
pūṣṇaḥ śāpādaṅgahīnaḥ kāmanāpūrako na hi || 2-143 ||

aṣṭāviṃśadekatriṃśadvarṇau nandīśaśāpataḥ |
atikrūraḥ sādhyanāśaṃ kṛtvā naśyecca sādhakaḥ || 2-144 ||

aṣṭatriṃśākṣaro mantro mātṛkāṇāṃ tu śāpataḥ |
atikruddhastajjapena svakṛtaḥ svasya saṃkṣayaḥ || 2-145 ||

catvāriṃśat samārabhya triṣaṣṭiṃ yāvadakṣaram |
savrīḍā mālikāśāpāt savrīḍāstasya sādhakāḥ || 2-146 ||

pañcaṣaṣṭyakṣarā ye syurmantrāste śāntamānasāḥ |
kāmaśāpena tajjāpādudyogasya phalaṃ na hi || 2-147 ||

ṣaṭṣaṣṭito navanavatiparyantaṃ kulasya tu |
sthānabhraṣṭāstu śāpena jātāstādṛk ca sādhakaḥ || 2-148 ||

kvātidhṛtirvyatidhṛtiḥ kvaṅkayugmaṃ dvigokarāḥ |
śatatrayaṃ yatra varṇāḥ śāpitāśchinnamastayā || 2-149 ||

yūyaṃ bhavata niḥsnehāḥ sādhakānāṃ bhayāpahāḥ |
niṣkāmamatibhirjaptāḥ saṃsārasnehanāśakāḥ || 2-150 ||

catuḥśatānyathārabhya yāvadvarṇasahasrakam |
ativṛddhā yajñaśāpāt pauṣṭikādiṣu varjitāḥ || 2-151 ||

sahasrārṇādhikā mantrā daṇḍapāṇestu śāpataḥ |
p. 90) daṇḍakākhyāpīḍitāśca tajjapāt kaṣṭamāpnuyāt || 2-152 ||

dvisahasrādhikā mantrā bhārgavasya tu śāpataḥ |
khaṇḍīkṛtāḥ saptadhā te siddhikhaṇḍakarāḥ smṛtāḥ || 2-153 ||

anyachṛṇvantu bho devā munīnāṃ japatāṃ yadā |
siddhirna jātā tu tadā dattaḥ śāpaḥ sudāruṇaḥ || 2-154 ||

āśvāsanaṃ devatānāṃ jātaṃ siddherakāraṇāt |
upadeśe guruṃ kuryāt tasmāt tanmantraśodhanam || 2-155 ||

mantraśodhanaprakāramāha |

tatprakāraṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇvantu sakalāḥ surāḥ |
guruḥ siddhāsane saṃstho gudameḍhrāntaraṃ dṛḍham || 2-156 ||

vāmena pārṣṇināpīḍya tasyopari tu vinyaset |
pādaṃ tu dakṣiṇaṃ gulphagranthyoranyonyamardanam || 2-157 ||

yathā syāt tat prakartavyaṃ tato dakṣiṇapārṣṇinā |
liṅgamūlaṃ viniṣpīḍya sthirakāya upāviśet || 2-158 ||

gudamākuñcya pavanamapānākhyaṃ tathā guruḥ |
utthāpayet tataḥ prāṇavāyuṃ samyaṅnirodhayet || 2-159 ||

apānaprāṇayoḥ kuryāt saṃghaṭṭaṃ caikadṛṅmanāḥ |
mūlādhāre citsvarūpakuṇḍalyāṃ paramātmani || 2-160 ||

jātadoṣaṃ svasya mantraṃ cintayet tatra sadguruḥ |
tasya mantrasyākṣarāṇi kramādekaikaśastataḥ || 2-161 ||

suṣumṇāyāstu mārgeṇa mūlādhāre praveśayet |
svādhiṣṭhāne tataścakre maṇipūre hyanāhate || 2-162 ||

p. 91) viśuddha ājñācakre ca bhittvābhittvā praveśayet |
evaṃ nītvā brahmarandhraṃ tatratyāt somamaṇḍalāt || 2-163 ||

nirgatāmṛtasaṃsiktān mantrārṇān suvibhāvayet |
punaḥ suṣumṇāmārgeṇa ājñācakre'vatārayet || 2-164 ||

viśuddhe'nāhate vāpi maṇipūre ca cakrake |
svādhiṣṭhāne tato bhedānmūlādhāre praveśayet || 2-165 ||

tatastanmantramṛṣyādinyāsapūrvaṃ japet punaḥ |
aṣṭottarasahasraṃ tu tena śuddho bhavenmanuḥ || 2-166 ||

anuktaśāpānnirmuktaṃ paścādupadiśecca tam || 2-166ef ||

	anuktaśāpāditi mānasaśāpādityarthaḥ | vācikaśāpānāṃ tu vakṣyamāṇadaśasaṃskaranāśyatvāditi bhāvaḥ | chinnatvādidoṣāṇāmuktayonibandhanāśyatvaṃ nāgabhaṭṭairapyuktam |

chinnā ruddhāstambhitāḥ kīlakā ye
suptā mantrā mūrcchitā hīnavīryāḥ |
dagdhāstrastāḥ śatrupakṣe sthitā ye
bālā vṛddhā garvitā yauvanena || 2-167 ||

ye nirjīvā ye ca sattvena hīnāḥ 
ṣaṇḍhībhūtāścāṅgamantrairvihīnāḥ |
ete mudrābandhanenaiva yonyā
mantrāḥ sarve vīryavanto bhavanti || 2-168 ||

p. 92) (śāradāyām)

doṣānimānavijñāya yo mantrān bhajate jaḍaḥ |
siddhirna jāyate tasya kalpakoṭiśatairapi || 2-169 ||

mantrāṇāṃ daśasaṃskārāstatraiva |

jananaṃ1 jīvanaṃ2 paścāt tāḍanaṃ3 bodhanaṃ4 tathā |
tathābhiṣeko5 vimalīkaraṇā6'pyāyane7 punaḥ || 2-170 ||

tarpaṇaṃ8 dīpanaṃ9 gupti10rdaśaitā mantrasaṃskriyāḥ |

jananādiprakārāstu (merutantre)

vācikānāṃ tu śāpānāṃ nāśādyarthamathocyate || 2-171 ||

śrīparṇyā vā candanasya pīṭhe cūtādikasya vā |
kastūrīkuṅkumājyaiśca vilikhet pāñcabhautikam || 2-172 ||

cakramāvāhayet tatra mātṛkākhyāṃ sarasvatīm |
yathoktavidhinā pūjāṃ kṛtvā cāṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 2-173 ||

japitvā mātṛkāṃ tasyā madhyāt svābhīṣṭamantrakam |
svaravyañjanavindū/śca sargayogākṣarāṇi ca || 2-174 ||

pṛthak pṛthagathoddhṛtya caikīkṛtya gurūktitaḥ |
gṛhītaṃ janayenmantraṃ proktaṃ saṃjananaṃ tvidam || 2-175 ||

ādyantaṃ praṇavaṃ kṛtvā praṇavāntaritaṃ japet |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ mantraṃ varṇānetat tu jīvanam || 2-176 ||

rocanākuṅkumābhyāṃ ca bhūrjapatre likhenmanum |
pratyakṣaraṃ śataṃ vāraṃ tāḍayeccandanāmbhasā || 2-177 ||

samuccaran vāyubījametat tāḍanamucyate |
p. 93) kuṇṭhitāḥ śābarā mantrāstāḍitāḥ siddhikārakāḥ || 2-178 ||

pūrvoktapīṭhe saṃlikhya tanmantrākṣarasaṃkhyayā |
ādāya karavīrasya puṣpāṇyatha samuccaran || 2-179 ||

pratyakṣaraṃ ca taiḥ puṣpaiḥ śatavāraṃ hunediti |
uccaran vahnibījaṃ ca bodhanaṃ tvetaducyate || 2-180 ||


(śāradāyāṃ) tu hunedityukte hanyādityuktam |


vilikhenmantrayanmantrī prasūnaiḥ karavīrajaiḥ |


tanmantrākṣarasaṃkhyātairhanyādrāntena bodhitam- iti |
rāntena vahnibījena (mairau)

prāgvat pīṭhe ca tairdravyaiḥ padmamaṣṭadalaṃ likhet || 2-181 ||

tatkarṇikāyāṃ mālatyā likhet kalikayaikayā |
ekaikārṇaṃ tu mantrasya tanmantrākṣarasaṃkhyayā || 2-182 ||

bodhidrupallavān gṛhya tamevaṃ mantramuccaran |
abhiṣiñcāmyamuṃ mantraṃ nama ityabhiṣecayet || 2-183 ||

nānāsugandhitoyena pallavaikena cākṣaram |
ekamaṣṭottaraśatamabhiṣiñcedayaṃ smṛtaḥ || 2-184 ||

abhiṣeko mantraśuddhiṃ kuryānnṝṇāṃ yathā jalam |
jyotirmantraścintanīyo mūlādhāre'gnimaṇḍale || 2-185 ||

saṃskarttavyaṃ manuṃ tasya ūrdhvamevaṃ vibhāvayet |
āṇavākhyaḥ sāhajika āgantuḥ kārmaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ || 2-186 ||

yogākhyo māyikaḥ proktaḥ svamantre tu malatrayam |
p. 94) jyotirmantreṇa nirdagdhamekamekaṃ vicintayet || 2-187 ||

evaṃ tu vimalo jātaḥ svamantra iti cintayet |
jyotirmantrastu vedādihraumiti dvyakṣaro mataḥ || 2-188 ||

vimalīkaraṇaṃ hyetat prayogānte'pi kārayet |
prāgvat pīṭhe kuṅkumādidravyairmantraṃ vilekhayet || 2-189 ||

tāmrādipātraṃ karpūravāsitena jalena ca |
śuddhenāpūrya likhitamantreṇāṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 2-190 ||

abhimantrya jalaṃ tena mantreṇa kuśavindubhiḥ |
pratyakṣaraṃ prokṣayet triretadāpyāyanaṃ matam || 2-191 ||

puraścaryākleśitānāṃ samāvarttanavat tvidam |
kastūryādyaistāmrapātramadhye mantraṃ vilekhayet || 2-192 ||

pātrāntare ca karpūrādikavāsitasajjalam |
gṛhītvā cārghapātreṇa paṭhanmantramimaṃ manum || 2-193 ||

tarpayāmi namaśceti tarpayediti tarpaṇam |
oṃ hrī/ śrī/ pūrvakaṃ mantraṃ japedaṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 2-194 ||

japedetaddīpanaṃ syāt sarvamantreṣvapīṣyate |
jappamānasya mantrasya gopanaṃ tvaprakāśanam || 2-195 ||

saṃskārā daśa saṃproktāḥ sarvatantreṣu gopitāḥ |

yān kṛtvā saṃpradāyena mantrī vāñchitamaśnute |
atha mantrāṇāṃ jāgṛtistatraiva |

athādau sarvamantrāṇāṃ jāgṛtiḥ procyate mayā || 2-196 ||

guruṇā sā prakartavyā śiṣyasya siddhihetave |
p. 95) tārāntyāgni viyadyuk ca yasminnāgneyakaḥ sa tu || 2-197 ||

anyaḥ saumyaḥ svāpakāla iḍāyāmagnirūpiṇaḥ |
saumyasya piṅgalāyāṃ syāt tayoranyatra jāgṛtiḥ || 2-198 ||

prabodhakāle saṃjapto jāgrato jāyate manuḥ |
atha vā saṃpuṭīkṛtya lāntānādyān sa bindukān || 2-199 ||

punaśca savisargāntān kṣakāraṃ kevalaṃ paṭhet |
evaṃ japto yadīṣṭaścet prabuddhaḥ śīghrasiddhidaḥ || 2-200 ||

śābaramantreṣu viśeṣa uktastatraiva |

atha śābaramantrāṇāṃ jāgṛtiṃ śṛṇu pārvati |
kāṃsyabhājanamānīya śuddhaṃ bhasmādibhiḥ kṛtam || 2-201 ||

p. 96) prajapedravirātrau tu yāme yāme śatāṣṭakam |
khādiryā tāḍayedyaṣṭyā kāṃsyapātraṃ vadediti || 2-202 ||

jāgrato bhava mantrasya bhāṣayātha turīyake |
yāme dadyādvaliṃ kālyā mantrajaṃ bhairavasya tu || 2-203 ||

kukkuṭaṃ'cānyadeveṣu sphoṭayennārikerakam |

mantrasya bhāṣayeti | yaddeśīyabhāṣātmakaḥ śābaramantrastaddeśīya-bhāṣayaiva mantraṃ saṃbodhya tvaṃ jāgrato bhaveti vadedityarthaḥ |

	atha dīkṣopayuktamapaṇḍapārthaṃ bhūmiparīkṣā |

plavasthānaṃ ca gambhīraṃ dīrghatoyāśayāśrayaḥ || 2-204 ||

agniśālāgnihotṝṇāṃ puṇyasthānaṃ pariśrutam |
dvijānāṃ savratānāṃ ca āśrayaḥ svāśramo'thavā || 2-205 ||

dīkṣāyāmatiśastāni sthānānyetāni pārvati |
na ca dīkṣāṃ prakurvīta nikuñje gahane vane || 2-206 ||

dvisīmni ca mahāraṇye mātsaryamṛgasevite |

(tantrāntare)

mahānadīnāṃ tīrāṇi gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgamaḥ || 2-207 ||

praśastāni ca tīrthāni kṣetrāṇi cāśramā api |
araṇyāni ca sarvāṇi parvatāḥ sakalā api || 2-208 ||

sanirjharāpyapagiri devatāyatanānyapi |

(mahākapilapañcarātraṃ)

tatra bhūmiṃ parīkṣeta vāstujñānaviśāradaḥ || 2-209 ||

sphuṭitā ca saśalyā ca valmīkārohiṇī tathā |
dūrataḥ parivarjyā bhūḥ karturāyurdhanāpahā || 2-210 ||

sphuṭitā maraṇaṃ kuryādūṣarā dhananāśino |
saśalyā kleśadā nityaṃ viṣamā śatruto bhayam || 2-211 ||

p. 97) īśakoṇaplavā sā ca kartuḥ śrīdāśu niścitam |
pūrvaplavā vṛddhikarī varadā tūttaraplavā || 2-212 ||

dakṣiṇaplavā yā bhūmirnityaṃ mṛtyubhayapradā |
gṛhakṣayakarī sā ca bhūmiryānairṛtaplavā || 2-213 ||

dhanahānikarī pṛthvī kīrtitā varuṇaplavā |
vāyuplavā tathā bhūmirnityamudvegakāriṇī || 2-214 ||

agnikoṇaplavā yā ca putrāyurdhananāśinī |

(gautamīye)

bhūmeḥ parigrahaṃ kuryādyāvadāyatanaṃ bhavet || 2-215 ||

śuklamṛtsnā ca yā bhūmirbrāhmī sā parikīrtitā |
kṣatriyā raktamṛtsnā ca haridvaiśyā prakīrtitā || 2-216 ||

kṛṣṇā bhūmirbhavechūdrā caturdhā parikīrtitā |

(mahākapilapañcarātre)

brāhmaṇī bhūḥ kuśopetā kṣatriyā syāccharākulā || 2-217 ||

kuśakāśākulā vaiśyā śūdrā sarvatṛṇākulā |

(gautamīyatantre)

brāhmī sarvārthasiddhyaiḥ syāt kṣatriyā rājyadā tathā || 2-218 ||

dhanadhānyakarī vaiśyā śūdrā tu ninditā mune |

(vāsiṣṭhyām)

khanyamāne yadā kuṇḍe pāṣāṇāḥ prāpyate bhuvi || 2-219 ||

tadāpamṛtyave cāsthikeśāṅgārairdhanakṣayaḥ |
bhasmanāgnibhayaṃ proktaṃ tuṣaiḥ proktā daridratā || 2-220 ||

śalyajñānārthamahibalacakramāha (ādiyāmale)

ahicakraṃ pravakṣyāmi yathā sarvajñabhāṣitam |
dravyaṃ śalyaṃ tathā śūnyaṃ yena jānanti sādhakāḥ || 2-221 ||

p. 98) nidhirnivartanaikasthaḥ saṃbhrānto yatra bhūtale |
tatra cakramidaṃ sthāpyaṃ sthānadvāramukhasthitam || 2-222 ||

ahigatyā ca nakṣatraṃ tatkāle yatra candramāḥ |
dravyaṃ śalyaṃ bhavet tatra candrabhāskarasādajam || 2-223 ||

ūrdhvarekhāṣṭakaṃ lekhyaṃ tiryak pañca tathaiva ca |
ahicakre bhavantmatyevamaṣṭāviṃśatikoṣṭhakāḥ || 2-224 ||

tatra pauṣṇāśvinīyāmyakṛttikāmaghabhāgyakam |
uttarāphālgunī lekhyā paṅktau tatsaptake dhruvam || 2-225 ||

ahirbudhnyājapādarkṣaṃ śatabhaṃ bhrāhmyasārpabham |
puṣyaṃ hastaṃ samālekhyaṃ dvitīyapaṅktisaṃsthitam || 2-226 ||

abhijidviṣṇudhaniṣṭhāḥ saumyaṃ raudraṃ punarvasum |
citrabhaṃ ca tṛtīyāyāṃ paṅtau dhiṣṇyādisaptake || 2-227 ||

viśvarkṣaṃ toyabhaṃ mūlaṃ jyeṣṭhāṃ maitraṃ viśākhabham |
svātīṃ paṅktau caturthyāṃ tu kṛtvā cakraṃ vilokayet || 2-228 ||

p. 99) revatyaśvinībharaṇīkṛtikāmaghāpūrvottarāḥ prathamapaṅktau | uttarabhādrapadapūrvabhādrapadaśatabhiṣārohiṇyāśleṣāpuṣyahastā dvitīyapaṅktau | abhijitśravaṇadhaniṣṭhāmṛgaśira-ārdrāpunarvasucitrās-tṛtīyapaṅktau | tata uttarāṣāḍhāpūrvāṣāḍhāmūlajyeṣṭhānurā-dhāviśākhāsvātyaścaturthapaṅktau |

evaṃ prajāyate cakre prastāraḥ pannagākṛtiḥ |
dvāraśākhe maghāyāmye dvārasthā kṛttikā matā || 2-229 ||

aśvīśapūrvaṣāḍhāditrikapañcacatuṣṭayam |
revatīpūrvabhādrendorbhāni śeṣāṇi bhāsvataḥ || 2-230 ||

udayādigatā nāḍyo bhaghnāḥ ṣaṣṭyāptaśeṣake |
dinendubhuktayukto'sau bhavet tatkālacandramāḥ || 2-231 ||

ṣaṣṭighna taṃ niśānāthaṃ śaravedāptakaṃ punaḥ |
yugaiḥ śeṣaṃ bhavedyat tat prāgādicakravakragam || 2-232 ||

candravat sādhayet sūryamṛkṣasthaṃ ceṣṭakālikam |
paścādvilokayet tau ca sva ṛkṣe'thānyabhe sthitau || 2-233 ||

candra ṛkṣe yadārkendū tatra syānniścito nidhiḥ |
bhānu ṛkṣe sthitau tau cet tadā śalyaṃ na cānyathā || 2-234 ||

svasvabhe dvitayaṃ jñeyaṃ nāsti kiṃ cidviparyaye |
