aryamā vidā-ī |

(merutantre)

brahmasthānaṃ raktavarṇairdiśaḥ pītairvidik sitaiḥ |
tato diśastu haritairvidiśaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇakaiḥ || 2-334 ||

aruṇaḥ paridhiḥ kāryaḥ sundaraṃ maṇḍalaṃ bhavet |
madhye padānāṃ navakaṃ mārjayitvā prapūrayet || 2-335 ||

sitena rajasā bhūyastaddikṣu catasṛṣvapi |
ṣaṭkaṃ saṃmārjya rajasā raktena paripūrayet || 2-336 ||

tayormadhye caturdikṣu koṣṭhaṃ dvādaśasaṃkhyakam |
p. 111) mārjayitvā haridvarṇairyarthāśobhaṃ prapūrayet || 2-337 ||

koṇe koṇe'ṣṭayugmaṃ pītavarṇena pūrayet |
śiṣṭāni ca padānyatra yathāśobhaṃ prakalpayet || 2-338 ||

vicitrāṇi tato madhye brahmāṇaṃ saṃprapūjayet |

(gautamīyatantre)

uktānāmapi devānāṃ padānyāpūrya pañcabhiḥ || 2-339 ||

rajobhisteṣvatho tebhyaḥ pāyasānnairvaliṃ haret |
pāyasairmadhuraiḥ sarvān saṃyajenmadhurānvitaiḥ || 2-340 ||

tattaddravyairvā matimān pūjayeddoṣaśāntaye |

(merutantre)

vrīhimudgayavaiḥ kṣaudraghṛtadugdhaplutaiḥ kramāt || 2-341 ||

aṣṭottaraśataṃ vāṣṭottaraviṃśatimeva vā |
pratyekaṃ tairhuneddravyairmantraistadvacca pāyasaiḥ || 2-342 ||

valiṃ ca teṣāṃ kurvīta pāyasairvyañjanānvitaiḥ |
sasitairdugdhakadalīphalāpūpādisaṃyutaiḥ || 2-343 ||

evaṃ siṃhagate bhānau pūrṇāyāṃ prativatsare |
svagehe vāstupūjāyāṃ maṇḍapeṣu samācaret || 2-344 ||

na bālamaraṇaṃ vyādhirbhūtapretādikāni ca |
na sarpapīḍā nānyonyakalahādyaśubhāni ca || 2-345 ||

putrapautradhanārogyaṃ paśudāsīsamṛddhibhāk |
arogī vijayakhyātaściraṃ jīvati tadgṛhe || 2-346 ||

rājaveśmasu sarvatra tathā ca mahiṣīgṛhe |
sacivāmātyasenānībhavaneṣu pare tathā || 2-347 ||

vidadhyāt prativarṣaṃ tu proktasiddhyai tu daiśikaḥ |
p. 112) na ceduktānyathārūpaphalaiḥ kleśo'niśaṃ bhavet || 2-348 ||

bhairavakṣetrapālānāṃ yoginīnāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
vāmācārairvaliḥ kāryo mahiṣairvāpi kukkuṭaiḥ || 2-349 ||

ārādhitāstu vāmena jāyante'tibubhukṣitāḥ |
devatā balimicchanti taddhīnaṃ ghnanti sādhakam || 2-350 ||

vāstudhyānam (somaśambhutantre)

ākuñcitakaraṃ vāstumuttānamasurākṛtim |
smaret pūjāsu kuḍyādiniveśe tvadharānanam || 2-351 ||

janunīkūrparāśakte diśi vātahutāśayoḥ |
paitryāṃ pādapuṭau raudryāṃ śiro'sya hṛdaye'ñjaliḥ || 2-352 ||

paitryāmiti | prakṛtimaṇḍale pitṛdevatādhiṣṭhitāyāṃ rakṣodiśi na tu dakṣiṇasyāmiti dhyeyam |

(mahākapilapañcarātre'pi)

pūrvamāsīnmahānugraḥ sarvabhūtabhayaṅkaraḥ |
sa devairnihato bhūmau sa vāstupuruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ || 2-353 ||

yāvadbhūmiḥ sthirā loke tāvadvāstvasuraḥ sthitaḥ |
saṃhāre tu layaṃ yāti devaiḥ sarvagrahādibhiḥ || 2-354 ||

vistāro'sya samantācca śatakoṭistu yojanam |
saṃsthito'sau dharāṃ vyāpya prottānaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇakaḥ || 2-355 ||

	atrāyaṃ kramaḥ | ādau yajamāna ācāryaṃ brūyāt | tata ācāryaḥ proktalakṣaṇāṃ bhūmiṃ parigṛhya tasyāḥ śuddhiṃ vidhāya pañcagavyaiḥ saṃsicya tanmadhye śuklaḍorakaṃ prasārya kanakaśalākayā ratnena rajataśalākayā vā tadabhāve puṣpaphaladhānyānāmanyatamena vā |

śāntā1 yaśovatī2 kāntā3vilāsā4 prāṇavāhinī5 |
p. 113) satī6 vasumatī7 nandā8 subhadrā9 navamī matā || 2-356 ||

iti nava rekhāḥ prāgaparāyatā dakṣiṇopakramā udagapavargā vilikhya

hiraṇyā1 suvratā2 lakṣmī3rvibhūti4rvimalā5 priyā6 |
jayā7 kālā8 viśokā9 ca navamī saṃskṛtā budhaiḥ || 2-357 ||

	iti nava rekhā dakṣiṇottarāyatāḥ paścimopakramāḥ pūrvāpavargā vilikhyaivaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭipadaṃ vāstumaṇḍalaṃ nirmāya pañcarajobhiḥ padāni paripūrya madhye'ṣṭadalaṃ vakṣyamāṇarītyā nirmāya tanmadhye brahmāṇaṃ dhyātvā āvāhya tattatpadeṣu tattaddevatāścāvāhya praṇavādinamo'ntaistannāmamantraiḥ pūjayet | maṇḍalādbahiḥkoṣṭheṣu koṣṭhacatuṣṭayamatiraktaṃ nirmāya tatra gurugaṇeśadurgākṣetrapālān pūjayet | aiśānyāṃ kalaśaṃ saṃsthāpya varuṇaṃ yathāvidhi pūjayediti rāghavabhaṭṭāḥ |

eṣāṃ dhyānāni tatrāntare -

uktānāṃ sarvadevānāṃ svarūpaṃ ca nigadyate |
akṣamālāṃ śruvaṃ dakṣe vāme daṇḍakamaṇḍalum || 2-358 ||

dadhānamaṣṭanayanaṃ yajenmadhye'mbujāsanam |
sarve caturbhujā devā vāstudehe vyavasthitāḥ || 2-359 ||

kṛtāñjalipuṭā ūrddhaṃ khaḍgakheṭakapāṇayaḥ |
brahmāṇaṃ saṃnnirīkṣante tadvakrābhimukhāśca te || 2-360 ||

svasvasthānasthitāścaiva sādhāraṇamudāhṛtam |
marīciḥ śvetavarṇaḥ syādvivasvānabhravarṇakaḥ || 2-361 ||

śātakumbhanibho mitraḥ kṛṣṇavaktrastu bhūdharaḥ |
savitā nīlavarṇābhaḥ sāvitro dhūmravigrahaḥ || 2-362 ||

induścāruṇavarṇābhaḥ śuklaścendrajayastathā |
rudraḥ pravālasadṛśaḥ pīto rudrajayastathā || 2-363 ||

āpo gokṣīradhavala āpavatso japādyutiḥ |
īśānaḥ kṣīradhavalaḥ parjanyo'ñjanasaṃnibhaḥ || 2-364 ||

jayanto'ñjanasaṃkāśo mahendraḥ śyāmaladyutiḥ |
p. 114) ādityo raktavarṇaḥ syāt satyakaścitravarṇakaḥ || 2-365 ||

vṛṣo bandhūkapuṣpābhaḥ kundābhaścāntarikṣakaḥ |
udyaddinakarābho'gniḥ pūṣā ratnābhasannibhaḥ || 2-366 ||

vitathaścendracāpābho vidyudvarṇo gṛharakṣakaḥ |
nirṛtiḥ pāvakābhaśca pīto dauvārikaḥ smṛtaḥ || 2-367 ||

sugrīvo nīlakaṇṭhābhaścandrābhaḥ puṣpadantakaḥ |
varuṇaḥ sphaṭikābhāṅgo bhṛṅgābhaścāsuro mataḥ || 2-368 ||

yamaścāñjanasaṅkāśo gandharvaḥ padmarāgavat |
bhṛṅgarājaśca bhṛṃṅgābho mṛgo jīmūtasaṃnibhaḥ || 2-369 ||

śoṣaścotpalasaṃkāśo rogaścaivendranīlavat |
vāyuḥ kṛṣṇābhavarṇaḥ syānnāgaḥ śeṣendusaṃnibhaḥ || 2-370 ||

mukhyo mauktikasaṃkāśo bhallāṭaḥ śvetapadmavat |
somaḥ sphaṭikasaṃkāśo'rgalo raktotpaladyutiḥ || 2-371 ||

ditiḥ kundendudhavalā kapilā cāditiḥ smṛtā |
carakī śaṅkhasadṛśī vidārī pāvakadyutiḥ || 2-372 ||

pūtanā himasaṃkāśā meghābhā pilapicchikā |
khaḍgaṃ ca pānapātraṃ ca churikāṃ kartarīṃ tathā || 2-373 ||

dadhānā bhīmarūpāstā rākṣasyaḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
sito raktaśca pītaśca kṛṣṇaḥ skandādikā grahāḥ || 2-374 ||

vajraṃ śaktiṃ ca khaḍgaṃ ca pāśaṃ ca vikṛtānanāḥ |
dadhānā bhīṣaṇāḥ proktā grahāḥ skandādikāśca te || 2-375 ||

	evaṃ dhyātvā pañcopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya vāstupuruṣamapi yathoktaṃ dhyātvā vaidikamate uktavaidikamantreṇa tāntrikamate tu o/vāstoṣpataye namaḥ iti mantreṇa pañcopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya tatpūrvadiśi tatpaścimadiśi vā hastamātraṃ trimekhalaṃ kuṇḍaṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ vā nirmāya tatra vakṣyamāṇakrameṇāgnimupasamādhāya maṇḍalādbrahmādidevatāstatrāvāhya

p. 115) tebhyaḥ pūrvoktasaṃkhyayā āhutīrhutvā pūrṇāhutiṃ vidhāya punarmaṇḍale devānānīya tebhyaḥ pratyekaṃ baliṃ dadyāt |

balimantrā dravyabhedāśca (gautamīyamahākapilapañcarātrayoḥ)

pāyasaudanalājaiśca yuktaṃ dhūpaprasūnakaiḥ |
annādibhiśca saṃyuktaṃ māṣabhaktādimaṇḍitam || 2-376 ||

gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ brahman vāstudoṣaṃ praṇāśaya |
gandhādiśarkarārūpaṃ pāyasoparisaṃyutam || 2-377 ||

āryakākhya gṛhāṇemaṃ sarvadoṣaṃ praṇāśaya |
candanādyarcitaṃ nātha karpūrāgurumaṇḍitam || 2-378 ||

vivasvan vai gṛhāṇemaṃ sarvadoṣaṃ praṇāśaya |
saguḍaṃ pāyasaṃ nātha puṣpādisusamanvitam || 2-379 ||

gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ hṛdyaṃ mitra śāntiṃ prayaccha me |
māṣaudanaṃ samāṃsaṃ ca gandhādikṣīrasaṃyutam || 2-380 ||

gṛhāṇemaṃ mahībhṛt tvaṃ sarvadoṣaṃ praṇāśaya |
evaṃ madhye tu saṃpūjya īśānādi valiṃ haret || 2-381 ||

etena pūjāyāmāgneyādikrame'pi valāvīśānādikramaḥ |
anyatrāpi |

īśānāddakṣiṇāvarto valiḥ sāmānyabhāṣitaḥ |
sarveṣāṃ khalu vastūnāṃ viśeṣaḥ padanirṇaye || 2-382 ||

kvacit tu pūjāpi tathaivoktā |

īśakoṇādiṣu surān pūjayecca vidhānataḥ |
īśānādicatuṣkoṇasaṃsthitān pūjayedbudhaḥ- iti || 2-383 ||

tathā |

kṣīrakhaṇḍasamāyuktaṃ puṣpādibhiralaṃkṛtam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ gṛhyamāpa śāntiṃ prayaccha me || 2-384 ||

p. 116) dadhīdaṃ guḍasaṃmiśraṃ gandhādibhiḥ susaṃyutam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ vatsa vighnamatra praṇāśaya || 2-385 ||

puṣpādikuśapānīyaṃ śarkarāguruvāsitam |
sāvitra vai gṛhāṇemaṃ śāntimatra prayaccha me || 2-386 ||

piṣṭakaṃ saguḍaṃ nātha raktagandhādiśobhitam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ sūrya vighnamatra praṇāśaya || 2-387 ||

śītamannaṃ tathā puṣpaṃ kuṅkumādisumaṇḍitam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ gṛhyaṃ śakradeva namo'stu te || 2-388 ||

odanaṃ ghṛtasaṃyuktaṃ vastragandhādimaṇḍitam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ gṛhyamindrajaya namo'stu te || 2-389 ||

pakvāpakvamidaṃ māṃsaṃ vastrapuṣpādisaṃyutam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ gṛhyaṃ rudradeva namo'stu te || 2-390 ||

samāṃsaṃ ghṛtasaṃpakvaṃ gandhapuṣpādisaṃyutam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ rudrajaya svasti namo'stu te || 2-391 ||

raktapuṣpaṃ samāṃsaṃ vai raktavastrādisaṃyutam |
vidāri vai gṛhāṇemaṃ rakṣovighnaṃ vināśaya || 2-392 ||

pittaraktāsthisaṃyuktaṃ raktagandhādimaṇḍitam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ pūtane tvaṃ rakṣovighnaṃ vināśaya || 2-393 ||

saghṛtaṃ māṣabhaktaṃ ca raktagandhādisaṃyutam |
valiṃ gṛhāṇa śarva tvaṃ rakṣovighnaṃ vināśaya || 2-394 ||

māṃsapuṣpādisaṃyuktaṃ māṣabhaktopaśobhitam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ skanda rakṣovighnaṃ vināśaya || 2-395 ||

śvamāṃsaṃ piṣṭakairyuktaṃ pakvamāṃsodakānvitam |
aryaman vai gṛhāṇemaṃ sarvavighnaṃ vināśaya || 2-396 ||

p. 117) raktamāṃsaudanaṃ matsyaṃ gandhadhūpasamanvitam |
jṛmbhaka tvaṃ gṛhāṇemaṃ rakṣovighnaṃ vināśaya || 2-397 ||

chāgakarṇānvitaṃ māṃsaṃ vastragandhādisaṃyutam |
pilapicche gṛhāṇemaṃ rakṣovighnaṃ vināśaya || 2-398 ||

ghṛtena sādhitaṃ māṃsaṃ vastragandhādisaṃyutam |
caraki tvaṃ gṛhāṇemaṃ rakṣovighnaṃ vināśaya || 2-399 ||

saghṛtaṃ sākṣataṃ bhaktaṃ vastragandhādyalaṃkṛtam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ tvīśa vāstudoṣāpahārakam || 2-400 ||

utpalaṃ pāyasairyuktaṃ vastrādikasamanvitam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ gṛhyaṃ megharāja namo'stu te || 2-401 ||

pañcahastaṃ supītaṃ ca dhvajaṃ bhaktādimaṇḍitam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ gṛhyaṃ jiṣṇusuta namo'stu te || 2-402 ||

odanaṃ ghṛtasaṃpūrṇaṃ pañcaratnādimaṇḍitam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ gṛhyaṃ devarāja namo'stu te || 2-403 ||

raktapuṣpayutaṃ bhaktaṃ raktagandhādibhiryutam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ hṛdyaṃ bhāskarāya namo'stu te || 2-404 ||

vitānaṃ dhūmravarṇābhaṃ gandhādikasuśobhitam |
raktapuṣpaṃ gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ satya namo'stu te || 2-405 ||

idaṃ tu māṃsabhaktaṃ vai vastragandhādipūjanam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ vṛṣa valiṃ vāstudoṣaṃ vināśaya || 2-406 ||

idaṃ tu śādvalaṃ māṃsaṃ naivedyādisaṃyutam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ hṛdyaṃ vyoma śāntiṃ prayaccha me || 2-407 ||

suvarṇaṃ piṣṭakaṃ cātha vastragandhādibhiryutam |
p. 118) ghṛtānvitaṃ gṛhāṇemaṃ saptajihva namo'stu te || 2-408 ||

kṣīraṃ lājāsamāyuktaṃ gandhapuṣpādisaṃyutaṃ |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ hṛdyaṃ pūṣadeva namo'stu te || 2-409 ||

māṃsaudanaṃ gandhamālyayutaṃ vastrādibhūṣitam |
vitatheśa valiṃ gṛhṇn vāstudoṣaṃ vināśaya || 2-410 ||

matsyamāṃsayutaṃ bhaktaṃ gandhapuṣpādyalaṃkṛtam |
yamarāja gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ śāntiṃ prayaccha me || 2-411 ||

pakvamāṃsaudananavanītaṃ vastrādimaṇḍitam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ prītikaraṃ gṛharakṣa namo'stu te || 2-412 ||

nānāgandhasamāyuktaṃ raktagandhādibhiryutam |
valiṃ gṛhāṇa gandharva sarvadoṣaṃ praṇāśaya || 2-413 ||

imāṃ tu śākunīṃ jihvāṃ māṣabhaktoparisthitām |
gṛhāṇemaṃ bhṛṅgarāja valiṃ śāntiṃ prayaccha me || 2-414 ||

evaṃ tilaghṛtopetaṃ gandhapuṣpādibhiryutam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ baliṃ hṛdyaṃ mṛgadeva namo'stu te || 2-415 ||

śarkarāmaṇḍitaṃ khaṇḍaṃ vastragandhādyalaṃkṛtam |
prīto valiṃ gṛhāṇemaṃ rakṣorāja namo'stu te || 2-416 ||

candanāgurukākhaṇḍaṃ vastragandhādibhiryutam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ hṛdyaṃ dauvārika namo'stu te || 2-417 ||

idaṃ tu pāyasaṃ nātha gandhapuṣpādimiśritam |
sugrīva vai gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ śānti prayaccha me || 2-418 ||

yavāgvalaṃkṛtaṃ dugdhaṃ bhaktoparisamanvitam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ hṛdyaṃ jaladeva namo'stu te || 2-419 ||

p. 119) māṣabhaktaṃ kuśastambaṃ ghṛtagandhādisaṃyutam |
puṣpadanta gṛhāṇemaṃ vāstudoṣaṃ praṇāśaya || 2-420 ||

madhunā sādhitaṃ piṣṭaṃ gandhādyairupaśobhitam |
valiṃ gṛhāṇāsurendra sarvadoṣaṃ praṇāśaya || 2-421 ||

ghṛtaṃ cānnasamāyuktaṃ karpūrādisamāyutam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ śoṣa sarvaśāntiṃ prayaccha me || 2-422 ||

yavajaṃ taṇḍulaṃ nātha gandhapuṣpādiśobhitam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ roga sarvadoṣaṃ praṇāśaya || 2-423 ||

saghṛtaṃ modakaṃ cedamannādyairupaśobhitam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ hṛdyaṃ mṛgavāha namo'stu te || 2-424 ||

idaṃ tu kṛsarānnaṃ me dugdhagandhādisādhitam |
pātāleśa gṛhāṇemaṃ vighnamatra praṇāśaya || 2-425 ||

nārikelodakaṃ bhaktaṃ pītavastrādisaṃyutam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ mukhya vāstudoṣaṃ praṇāśaya || 2-426 ||

dadhigandhādibhiryuktaṃ pītapuṣpasamanvitam |
valiḥ pragṛhyatāṃ soma sarvadoṣaṃ praṇāśaya || 2-427 ||

odanaṃ ghṛtasaṃmiśraṃ gandhapuṣpasamanvitam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ gṛhyaṃ bhallāṭaka namo'stu te || 2-428 ||

māṣānnaṃ tu ghṛtābhyaktaṃ gandhapuṣpādimaṇḍitam |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ hṛdyamargalākhya namo'stu te || 2-429 ||

kṣīrakhaṇḍasamāyuktaṃ nānāpuṣpopaśaobhitam |
daityamātargṛhāṇemaṃ sarvadoṣaṃ praṇāśaya || 2-430 ||

p. 120) paurikāṃ madhusaṃmiśrāṃ vastragandhādisaṃyutām |
gṛhāṇemaṃ valiṃ gṛhyaṃ devamātarnamo'stu te || 2-431 ||

svargapātālamartyeṣu devatā vāstudevatāḥ |
gṛhṇantvimaṃ valiṃ hṛdyaṃ tuṣṭā yāntu svamandiram || 2-432 ||

mātaro bhūtavetālā ye cānye valikāṅkṣiṇaḥ |
viṣṇoḥ pāriṣadā ye ca te'pi gṛhṇantvimaṃ valim || 2-433 ||

pitṛbhyaḥ kṣetrapālebhyo valiṃ datvā prakāmataḥ |
abhāvāduktamārgasya kuśapuṣpādibhiryajet || 2-434 ||

(prayogasāre)

tatpārṣadebhyaśca tataḥ sarvebhya iti saṃyutaḥ |

dviṣatpiśācavetālarakṣorakṣārthināmiha |
p. 121) diśāṃ valirviśeṣeṇa sarvasaṃpatsamṛddhidaḥ || 2-441 ||

	iti baliṃ dattvā punastān samabhyarcya yathāśakti japtvā stutvā praṇamya visarjayet |

trasareṇavaste tvaṣṭau bālāgraṃ tat smṛtaṃ budhaiḥ || 2-522 ||

bālāgrāṇyaṣṭa likṣā tu yūkā likṣāṣṭakaṃ smṛtam |
 aṣṭau yūkā yavaṃ prāhuraṅgulaṃ tu yavāṣṭakam || 2-523 ||

hastaścaturviṃśatibhiraṅgulaiḥ samudāhṛtam |

dvihastādau taddviguṇādivṛddhiprakārastu ekahastakuṇḍakoṇasūtreṇa dvihastakuṇḍamadhyasūtramevaṃ tatkoṇasūtreṇa caturhastakuṇḍamadhya-sūtramavagantavyam | evamagre'pi |
taduktam (gautamīyatantre)

karṇasūtrapramāṇena dvihastaṃ kuṇḍamuddharet || 2-524 ||

sarvakuṇḍeṣu sarvatra vardhayedvidhināmunā |

 atra karṇasūtrapadaṃ koṇasūtraparam |
taduktam tatraiva |

pūrvapūrvasya kuṇḍasya koṇasūtraṃ tu yadbhavet || 2-525 ||
uttarottarakuṇḍānāṃ mānaṃ tadvadudīritam || 2-525ef ||

	etena dvihastakuṇḍasya catustriṃśadaṅgulāni vyāsaḥ | ekacatvāriṃ-śadaṅgulāni pañca yavāśca trihastasya | aṣṭacatvāriṃśadaṅgulāni caturhastasya | tripañcāśadaṅgulāni ṣaḍyavāśca pañcahastasya | aṣṭapañcāśadaṅgulāni sapta yavāśca ṣaḍḍhastasya | triṣaṣṭiraṅgulāni catvāro yavāśca saptahastasya | saptaṣaṣṭiraṅgulāni sapta yavāścāṣṭahastasya | dvāsaptatiraṅgulāni navahastasya | pañcasaptatiraṅgulāni sapta yavāśca daśahastasya | ekahastakuṇḍasya kṣetraphalaṃ tu ṣaṭsaptatyadhikapañcaśatāṅgulāni | evaṃ dvihastādāvapi boddhavyam | yadyapi hastamātrakuṇḍasyaiva dīkṣopayuktatvena dvihastadikuṇḍāni dīkṣāyāmanupayuktāni tathā ca vakṣyamāṇapuraścaraṇāṅgahome upayujyanta iti pradarśitāni |
	khātamānaṃ mekhalālakṣaṇaṃ ca (tripurāsārasamuccaye)

p. 132) yāvanmānaṃ kuṇḍavistāra uktaṃ
tāvat khātasyāpi mānaṃ pradiṣṭam |
kuṇḍākāraṃ paṇḍito yādṛśaṃ tat
tādṛgrūpaṃ mekhalāyāṃ vidadhyāt || 2-526 ||

	etāvatkuṇḍakaraṇāśaktastu pañcakuṇḍāni kuryāt | tatrāpyaśaktastu ekamevakuṇḍaṃ kuryāt |

nava pañcātha caikaṃ vā kartavyaṃ lakṣaṇānvitam |

ityāmnāyarahasyavacanāt |

aṣṭāśaktau tu catvāri dikṣu kuṇḍāni tāntrike || 2-527 ||

tadaśaktāvekameva caturasraṃ prakalpayet || iti (merutantravacanācca) pañcakuṇḍapakṣe tanniveśanam (jñānaratnāvalyāmuktam)

dikṣu vedāsravṛttāni pañcamaṃ tvīśagocaram || 2-528 ||

ityekakuṇḍapakṣe tanniveśanamuktamācāryaiḥ |

athavā diśi kuṇḍamuttarasyāṃ
pravidhyāccaturasramekameva- iti |

(piṅgalāmate)

kuṇḍamekakaraṃ vṛttaṃ mekhalākaṇṭhanābhimat |
nitye karmaṇi dīkṣāyāṃ śāntau puṣṭau mataṃ śubham || 2-529 ||

kaṇṭhamānamāha (mahākapilapañcarātre)

caturviṃśatibhāgena kaṇṭho vai parikīrtitaḥ |
khātādbāhye'ṅgulaḥ kaṇṭhaḥ sarvakuṇḍeṣvayaṃ vidhiḥ || 2-530 ||
caturviṃśatimaṃ bhāgamaṅgulaṃ parikalpayet || 530ef ||

tisraḥ kuṇḍe mekhalā mekhale dve
yadvā caikāṃ mekhalāṃ vai prakuryāt |
kuryādūrdhvaṃ mekhalāyā gajoṣṭhā-
kārāṃ yoniṃ cāpi hoturdiśīha || 2-531 ||

p. 133) etena yoneḥ kuṇḍāṅgatvamuktvā tasyāḥ sthānasvarūpadiṅniyamāścoktāḥ | (kriyāsāre)

nābhiyonisamāyuktaṃ kuṇḍaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ trimekhalam |
kuṇḍaṃ dvimekhalaṃ madhye nīcaṃ syādekamekhalam || 2-532 ||

(somaśambhau)

trimekhalaṃ dvije kuṇḍaṃ kṣatriyasya dvimekhalam |
mekhalaikā tu vaiśyasya --- --- --- --- --- --- --- --- iti || 2-533 ||

kuṇḍatāratamyato mekhalātāratamyamuktam | (tripurāsārasamuccaye)

muṣṭimātre tu tāṃ mekhalāṃ dvyaṅgulaikā-
ṅgulārdhāṅgulotsedhatārāḥ kramāt syuḥ |
ratnimātre tu tāstryaṅgulārdhāṅgulaikā-
ṅgulotsedhatārā bhaveyuḥ purastāt || 2-534 ||

yugāppittanetrāṅgulotsedhatārāḥ
syuretāḥ kramāddhastamātre tu kuṇḍe |
karadvandvamātre rasāmbhodhivahnya-
ṅgulotsedhatārā bhaveyuḥ purastāt || 2-535 ||

vedakare'smin sindhuratarkvā
dvyaṅgulatārotsedhayutāḥ syuḥ |
ṣaṭkaragātre'smin daśakarya-
ṅgāṅgulatārotsedhasametāḥ || 2-536 ||

bhāskarapaṅktigajāṅgulatāro-
tsedhayutāḥ syurimā vasuhaste |
nābhimihāmbujameva vidadhyā-
daṣṭadalaṃ sahakarṇikayāntaḥ || 2-537 ||

p. 134) nābhimiti | kuṇḍamadhye sakarṇikāṣṭadalakamalarūpāṃ nābhiṃ kuryādityarthaḥ | tatraiva yonyādeḥ kuṇḍaparimāṇānusāraṃ parimāṇamuktam |

muṣṭimātre rasāmbhodhinetrāṅgulāyāmatāronnatiryoniruktā budhiḥ |
netravedāṅgulotsedhatārānvitā nābhirapyatra yonyoṣṭhamekāṅgulam || 2-538 ||

yonyā lakṣaṇametadgaditamaratnimātrakuṇḍasya |
kuṇḍasyaiva mayoktaṃ tadeva khalu hastamātrasya || 2-539 ||

dvihastādikuṇḍeṣu yonyādivṛddhiprakārastatraiva |

ekaikenāṅgulenāmalaniśitamatirvardhayedanyakuṇḍe-
ṣvenāṃ yoniṃ ca nābhiṃ khalu yavayugaleneha yonyāstathauṣṭham |
kuryānnābhiṃ ca vṛttākṛtimapi yadi vā kuṇḍarūpānyarūpāṃ
yonyākāre'tra yoniṃ sarasijasadṛśe no vidadhyācca nābhim || 2-540 ||

kuryādityanena nābhyākāravikalpa uktaḥ | yonyākāra iti | yonikuṇḍe yoniṃ padmakuṇḍe nābhiṃ na kuryādityarthaḥ |
matāntaramāha tatraiva |

kuṇḍasyeke hastamātrasya yoniṃ
prāhurbhūtadvyaṅgulāyāmatārām |
vyāsatryaṃśaikāṃśakotsedhayuktāṃ
kuryādbodhikṣmājapatrānurūpām || 2-541 ||

tatpakṣe nābhivṛddhiṃ tadracanāprakāraṃ cāha |

netrabhūtāṅgulotsedhatārānvitāṃ
nābhimaṃśadvaye taddalānyaṣṭa ca |
vyāsavahnyaṃśatoṃśena tatkarṇikāṃ
madhyabhāge vidadhyādbudhaḥ kuṇḍataḥ || 2-542 ||

nemiparimāṇamāha tatraiva |

kuṇḍasyaikakarasya cāru parito nemirbhavedaṅgule-
naikenaiva tato vilocanakarādārabhya saṃvardhayet |
p. 135) kuṇḍe dikkarakuṇḍakāvadhi budhaḥ pratyekamardhāṅgulaṃ
tadbāhye khalu mekhalātritayakaṃ kuryādyathoktakramāt || 2-543 ||

vaidikamate tu nābhikaṇṭhayonyo na kāryāḥ |

khyātānyetāni kuṇḍāni tāntrike smārttakarmaṇi |
 anābhikānyakaṇṭhāni viyonīti śruteriti || 2-544 ||

iti merutantravacanāt |
nālamāha | (śāradāpañcarātrayoḥ)

sthalādārabhya nālaṃ syādyonyā madhye sarandhrakam |
sthūlamūlaṃ tu sūkṣmāgraṃ bāhyamekhalayā samam- iti || 2-545 ||

(jñānārṇave)

mekhalānāṃ bahiḥsthānaṃ sthalamityabhidhīyate |
caturasrasthalāllabdhaṃ nālaṃ madhye sarandhrakam || 2-546 ||

 āhutitāratamyataḥ kuṇḍānāṃ tāratamyamuktam |
(tripurāsārasamuccaye)

śatārdhahome khalu muṣṭimātra-
maratnimātraṃ śatahomakuṇḍam |
hastaṃ sahasre tvayute dvihastaṃ
lakṣe caturhastamidaṃ pradiṣṭam || 2-547 ||

daśalakṣe rasahastaṃ vasuhastaṃ koṭihome syāt |
koṭyardheṣvaṣṭakaraṃ home'ṣṭāgamavido bruvate || 2-548 ||

matāntaramāha |

keciddhastaṃ lakṣahome dvihastaṃ
lakṣadvandve vahnilakṣe trihastam |
home kuṇḍaṃ vedahaste'bdhilakṣaṃ
prāhurdoṣṇāṃ pañcakaṃ pañcalakṣe || 2-549 ||

rasahastaṃ rasalakṣe saptakaraṃ saptalakṣe syāt |
p. 136) vasuhastaṃ vasulakṣe navalakṣe navakaraṃ kuṇḍam || 2-550 ||

daśahastaṃ daśalakṣe daśakarameveha koṭihome'pi |
daśahastānna hi kuṇḍaṃ paramasti mahītale'muṣmin || 2-551 ||

kuṇḍānāṃ pṛthak pṛthak phalaṃ tatraiva |

sarvasiddhikaramambudhikoṇaṃ putradaṃ manasijāvasathāntam |
 ardhacandrasadṛśaṃ śubhadaṃ syādagnikoṇamarivardhanahetuḥ || 2-552 ||

śāntikarmaṇi bhavet suvartulaṃ chedamāraṇakaraṃ ṣaḍasrakam |
padmakuṇḍamiha vṛṣṭikāraṇaṃ rogaśāntikaramaṣṭakoṇakam || 2-553 ||

varṇabhedena kuṇḍabhedamāha |

caturasraṃ viprāṇāṃ rājñāmiha vartulaṃ kuṇḍam |
vaṇijāmardhaśaśāṅkākāraṃ tryasraṃ tu śūdrāṇām || 2-554 ||

caturasraṃ sarveṣāṃ praśastamiti ke cidāhurācāryāḥ |
vāñchitaphaladaṃ yat tat kuṇḍaṃ bhuvi bhuktimuktikaram || 2-555 ||

ṣaṭkoṇamardhacandrākāraṃ cāpi prajākaraṃ kuṇḍam |
muktikaraṃ pañcāsraṃ vasukoṇaṃ rājyadā yoniḥ || 2-556 ||

tathā |

nitye naimittike ca praviralamatayaḥ prāhureke'bdhikoṇaṃ
kuṇḍaṃ hastapramāṇaṃ smaragṛhasadṛśaṃ cātha vṛddhaṃ praśastam |
ṣaṭkoṇaṃ cārdhadoṣākarasadṛśayutaṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ ratnimātrā-
yāmaṃ svāṅguṣṭhapūrvonnatamapi susamaṃ nirmitaṃ bālukābhiḥ || 2-557 ||

(merutantre)

 anekadoṣaṃ vai kuṇḍaṃ mānaṃ nyūnādhikaṃ yadi |
tasmāt samyak parīkṣyedaṃ kartavyaṃ śubhamicchatā || 2-558 ||

khātādhike bhavedrogī hīne dhenudhanakṣayaḥ |
vakrakuṇḍe tu santāpo maraṇaṃ cchinnamekhale || 2-559 ||

p. 137) mekhalārahite śoko'bhyadhike vittasaṃśayaḥ |
bhāryāvināśakaṃ kuṇḍaṃ proktaṃ yonyā vinā kṛtam || 2-560 ||

 apatyadhvaṃsanaṃ proktaṃ kuṇḍaṃ yat kaṇṭhavarjitam |
śṛṅgārarahitaṃ yacca kuṇḍaṃ jarjaramekhalam || 2-561 ||

yajamānavināśāya proccāṭaḥ sphuṭito bhavet |
sūtrādhike suhṛddveṣaḥ sūtrahīne daridratā || 2-562 ||
 anāle jaṭhare rogaśchinnakuṇḍe tvavācyatā || 562ef ||

sthaṇḍilalakṣaṇam (śāradāyām)

p. 139) kuṇḍamekavidhaṃ kaṣṭaṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ tu samācaret |
caturasraṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ tu praśastaṃ sarvakarmasu || 2-563 ||

(candrapīṭhe tu)

trirasraṃ caturasraṃ ca vitastyucchrayasaṃmitam |
sikatādyairvidhātavyaṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ hastamātrakam- ityuktam || 2-564 ||

atha sruglakṣaṇam (śāradāyām)

prakalpayet srucaṃ yāge vakṣyamāṇena vartmanā |
śrīparṇīśiṃśapākṣīraśākhiṣvekatamaṃ guruḥ || 2-565 ||

gṛhītvā vibhajeddhastamātraṃ ṣaṭtriṃśatā punaḥ |
viṃśatyaṃśairbhaveddaṇḍo vedistairaṣṭabhirbhavet || 2-566 ||

ekāṃśena mitaḥ kaṇṭhaḥ saptabhāgamitaṃ mukham |
vedītryaṃśena vistāraḥ kaṇṭhasya parikīrtitaḥ || 2-567 ||

 agraṃ kaṇṭhasamānaṃ syānmukhe mārgaṃ prakalpayet |
kaniṣṭhāṅgulimānena sarpiṣo nirgamāya ca || 2-568 ||

vedimadhye vidhātavyā bhāgenaikena karṇikā |
vidadhīta bahistasyā ekāṃśenābhito'vaṭam || 2-569 ||

tasya khātaṃ tribhirbhāgairvṛttamardhāṃśato bahiḥ |
 aṃśenaikena parito dalāni parikalpayet || 2-570 ||

mekhalāmukhavedyoḥ syānnemirardhāṃśamānataḥ |
daṇḍaśūlāgrayoḥ kumbho guṇavedāṅgulaiḥ kramāt || 2-571 ||

gaṇḍīyugaṃ yamāṃśaiḥ syāddaṇḍasyānāha īritaḥ |
ṣaḍbhiraṃśaḥ pṛṣṭhabhāgo vedyāḥ kūrmākṛtirbhavet || 2-572 ||

p. 140) haṃsasya hastino vāpi potriṇo vā likhenmukham |
mukhasya pṛṣṭhabhāge tu saṃproktaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ srucaḥ || 2-573 ||

śrīparṇī gambhārīti prasiddhā | kṣīraśākhino'śvatthodumbaravaṭapla-kṣāḥ | hastamātramiti bāhumātramityarthaḥ |

srucaṃ bāhupramāṇena homārthaṃ vidadhīta vai |

ityagastyasaṃhitāvacanāt | gaṇḍī kalaśaḥ |
(phetkāriṇītantre)

kāraskareṇāmalakīpalāśakhadirairapi || 2-574 ||

suvarṇarūpyatāmrairvā ayobhirapi kārayet |

(vāyavīsaṃhitāyām)

sruksruvau taijasau grāhyau na kāṃsyāyasasaisakau || 2-575 ||

yajñadārumayau vāpi tāntrikau śilpisaṃmatau |
parṇairvā brahmavṛkṣāderacchidre madhya ucchrite || 2-576 ||

 atrāyase vidhiniṣadhau krūrasaumyaviṣayau |
taduktaṃ tantrāntare |

sauvarṇau sruksruvau syātāṃ saumyakarmaṇi sarvataḥ |
bhavetāmāyasāvetau stambhanādiṣu karmasu || 2-577 ||

(vasiṣṭhasaṃhitāyām)

palāśapatre niśchidre rucire sruksruvau matau |
vidadhyādvāśvatthapatre saṃkṣipte homakarmaṇi || 2-578 ||

(mantramuktāvalyām)

kaṇṭhādhaḥ kārayenmārge vidvānājyasya durgame |
vedhaṃ ca mukhataḥ kuryāt taptalohaśalākayā || 2-579 ||

atha sruvalakṣaṇam (śāradāyām)

srucaścaturviṃśatibhirbhāgairāracayeddhruvam |
dvāviṃśatyā daṇḍamānamaṃśairetasya kīrtitam || 2-580 ||

caturbhiraṃśairānāhaḥ karṣājyagrāhitacchiraḥ |
p. 141) aṃśadvayena nikhanet paṅkairmṛgapadākṛtiḥ || 2-581 ||

daṇḍamūlāgrayorgaṇḍī bhavet kaṅkaṇabhūṣitā |
sruvasya vidhirākhyāta --- --- --- --- --- --- --- --- --- iti || 2-582 ||

athāṅkurāparṇam (śāradāyām)

prāgeva dīkṣādivasāt saptabhirvidhivaddinaiḥ |
sarvamaṅgalasaṃpattyai vidadhyādaṅkurārpaṇam || 2-583 ||

(siddhāntaśekhare)

pratiṣṭhāyāṃ ca dīkṣāyāṃ snāpane cotsave tathā |
saṃprokṣaṇe ca śāntyarthaṃ vivāhe mauñjibandhane || 2-584 ||

sarvamaṅgalakāryeṣu vidadhyādaṅkurārpaṇam |

(merutantre)

saptabhirnavabhirvāpi dinairdīkṣādināt purā || 2-585 ||

bījāropaṇakarmedaṃ kartavyaṃ sarvakāmadam |
maṇḍapottaradigbhāge susaṃvṛttaparaṃ pare || 2-586 ||

bhavane maṇḍalaṃ kuryāddākṣyāṃ prāgvaruṇāyate |
yāmyodīcyāyate vāmadīkṣāyāṃ saṃpraśasyate || 2-587 ||

bhavana iti maṇḍapāntare tacca dakṣiṇaikadvārayuktaṃ jñeyam |
tathā |

gomayenopaliptāyāṃ bhūmau dakṣe'tha vāmake |
satailataṇḍulenaiva satailavijayādravaiḥ || 2-588 ||

tatra rekhāḥ pañcakaradīrghākārāstu pañca ca |
vitastyantarato rudrarekhāḥ kāryāstathāyatāḥ || 2-589 ||

catvāriṃśatkoṣṭhakāḥ syuścaturviṃśatikoṣṭhakaiḥ |
bāhyagaistatra vīthī syāt pūrayedraktacūrṇakaiḥ || 2-590 ||

p. 142) paścāt koṣṭhacatuṣkaṃ yadbrahmasthānaṃ tadīritam |
 āgneyakoṣṭhamārabhya īśānāntaṃ prapūrayet || 2-591 ||

pītaraktaśvetakṛṣṇarajobhiḥ śreṣṭhamārgataḥ |
koṣṭhadvayaṃ tato vīthī raktenaiva prapūrayet || 2-592 ||

viṣṇusthānaṃ tadagre tu jñeyaṃ koṣṭhacatuṣṭayam |
prāgvadrajobhirāpūrya tadagre'pi ca vīthikā || 2-593 ||

prāksthaṃ koṣṭhacatuṣkaṃ ca rudrasthānaṃ tadīritam |
tatpūrvaṃ pūrvavadraktairāraktaiḥ sarvarekhikāḥ || 2-594 ||

vistāronnatidairghyeṇa hastamātrāstu pālikāḥ |
kāryā ḍamarukākārāścatasro viṣṇudevatāḥ || 2-595 ||

dūrvāmrapallavāśvatthadalopetāśca veṣṭitāḥ |
sūtreṇa viṣṇusthānasya catuṣkoṣṭhe niveśayet || 2-596 ||

vairiñcyaḥ pañcamukhyastāḥ koṣṭhajāḥ ṣoḍaśāṅgulaiḥ |
 āyāmocchrāyadairghādyairyuktāḥ paścānniveśayet || 2-597 ||

brahmasthāne pallavādisūtraiḥ prāgvacca yojitāḥ |
śaivāḥ śarāvāścatvāro bhānvaṅgulasamucchrayāḥ || 2-598 ||

prakṣālya sūtraiḥ saṃveṣṭya prāgvadanyādiṣu nyaset |
śālipañjeṣu gandhādidarbhakūrcayugeṣu ca || 2-599 ||

ḍamarukākṛtayo'tigambhīrāḥ śarāvā eva pālikāḥ | mukhapañcakayuk-tāstādṛśā eva pātraviśeṣāḥ pañcamukhyaḥ |
taduktaṃ tantrāntare |

dikṣu catvāri vaktrāṇi mukhamekaṃ tadūrdhvagam- iti |

(merau)

pūrayet tāni pātrāṇi vālukāmṛtkarīṣakaiḥ || 2-600 ||

p. 143) mṛttikāharaṇe viśeṣa uktaḥ (siddhāntaśekhare)

gandhādibhiśca kuddālaṃ pūjayitvā dināntare |
gītanṛtyasamāyuktaṃ gajavājisamanvitam || 2-601 ||

gurvādayo rathārūḍhā gajārūḍhāstathāpare |
gatvā tīraṃ taḍāgasya nadyāḥ puṣpavanasya vā || 2-602 ||

tatra śuddhaṃ bhuvo bhāgaṃ darbhaiḥ saṃmṛjya cāstrataḥ |
 abhyukṣya cārghatoyena tattanmantramanusmaret || 2-603 ||

hṛdā bhūmiṃ samāvāhya gandhapuṣpaiḥ samarcayet |

puraṃ vā nilayaṃ vāpi sarvamaṅgalaniḥsvanaiḥ |
gurupradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā maṇḍapaṃ tvānayet tataḥ || 2-606 ||

etat karma divākāle kuryādrātrau na buddhimān |

bījāropaṇaṃ tu rātrāveva |

 iti tatratyādeva vacanāt |
tathā |

p. 144) śyāmākā bhāṣayā sāmā iti prasiddhā | āḍhakī bhāṣayā rahara iti prasiddhā | niṣpāvaḥ simbiḥ | khalvāḥ kulitthāḥ |
tathā |

brahmapātre tu vikiret prathamaṃ vaiṣṇave tataḥ |

evaṃ dvitīyadivase pitṛbhyastilataṇḍulaiḥ |

nāgebhyaḥ saktavasturye nārikelajalaplutāḥ |

vāmamārge tu viśeṣa uktastatraiva |

p. 145) gauḍī mādhvī ca paiṣṭī ca surāstatra kramānmatāḥ |

madhye madhye yathā matsyā bhaveyuḥ pātayet tathā || 3-86 ||

pātayet teṣu matsyeṣu samaṃ sūtracatuṣṭayam || 3-87 ||

pūrvavatkoṇakoṣṭheṣu karṇasūtrāṇi pātayet |
tadudbhūteṣu matsyeṣu dadyāt sūtracatuṣṭayam || 3-88 ||

tataḥ koṣṭheṣu matsyāḥ syusteṣu sūtrāṇi pātayet |
ṣaṭtriṃśatā padairmadhye likhet padmaṃ sulakṣaṇam || 3-89 ||

bahiḥ paṅktyā bhavet pīṭhaṃ paṅktiyugmena vīthikāḥ |
dvāraśobhopaśobhāstāḥ śiṣṭābhyāṃ parikalpayet || 3-90 ||

śāstroktavidhinā mantrī tataḥ padmaṃ samālikhet |
padmakṣetrasya saṃtyajya dvādaśāṃśaṃ bahiḥ sudhīḥ || 3-91 ||

tanmadhyaṃ vibhajedvṛttaistribhiḥ samavibhāgataḥ |
ādyaṃ syāt karṇikāsthānaṃ keśarāṇāṃ dvitīyakam || 3-92 ||

tṛtīyaṃ tatra patrāṇāmuktāṃśena dalāgrakam |
bāhyavṛttāntarālasya mānaṃ yadvidhinā sudhīḥ || 3-93 ||

nidhāya keśarāgreṣu parito'rdhaniśākarān |
likhitvā sandhisaṃsthāni tatra sūtrāṇi pātayet || 3-94 ||

dalāgrāṇāṃ ca yanmānaṃ tanmānaṃ vṛttamālikhet |
tadantarāle tanmadhye sūtrasyobhayataḥ sudhīḥ || 3-95 ||

alikhedbāhyahastena dalāgreṇa samantataḥ |
p. 156) dalamūleṣu yugaśaḥ keśarāṇi prakalpayet || 3-96 ||

etat sādhāraṇaṃ proktaṃ paṅkajaṃ tantravedibhiḥ |
padāni trīṇi pādārthaṃ pīṭhakoṇeṣu mārjayet || 3-97 ||

avaśiṣṭaiḥ padairvidvān pīṭhagātrāṇi kalpayet |
padāni vīthisaṃsthāni mārjayet paṅktyabhedataḥ || 3-98 ||

dikṣu dvārāṇi racayeddvicatuṣkoṣṭhakaistataḥ |
padaistribhirathaikena śobhāḥ syurdvārapārśvayoḥ || 3-99 ||

upaśobhāḥ syurekena tribhiḥ koṣṭhairanantaram |
avaśiṣṭaiḥ padaiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ koṇānāṃ syāccatuṣṭayam || 3-100 ||

rañjayet pañcabhirvarṇairmaṇḍalaṃ tanmanoharam |
pītaṃ haridrācūrṇaṃ syāt sitaṃ taṇḍulasaṃbhavam || 3-101 ||

kusumbhacūrṇamaruṇaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ dagdhapulākajam |
vilvādipatrajaṃ śyāmamityuktaṃ varṇapañcakam || 3-102 ||

aṅgulotsedhavistārāḥ sīmārekhāḥ sitāḥ śubhāḥ |
karṇikā pītavarṇena keśarāṇyaruṇena ca || 3-103 ||

śubhravarṇena patrāṇi tatsandhīn śyāmalena ca |
rajasā rañjayenyantrī yadvā pītaiva karṇikā || 3-104 ||

keśarāḥ pītaraktāḥ syuraruṇāni dalānyapi |
sandhayaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇāḥ syuḥ pītenāpyasitena vā || 3-105 ||

rañjayet pīṭhagarbhāṇi pādāḥ syuraruṇaprabhāḥ |
gātrāṇi tasya śuklāni vīthīṣu catasṛṣvapi || 3-106 ||

ālikhet kalpalatikā dalapuṣpaphalānvitāḥ |
p. 157) varṇairnānāvidhaiścitrāḥ śobhā raktāḥ samīritāḥ || 3-107 ||

upaśobhāḥ pītavarṇāḥ koṇānyasitabhāni ca |
tisro rekhā bahiḥ kuryāt sitaraktāsitāḥ kramāt || 3-108 ||

maṇḍalaṃ sarvatobhadrametat sādhāraṇaṃ smṛtam |
caturasrāṃ bhuvaṃ bhitvā digbhyo dvādaśadhā sudhīḥ || 3-109 ||

pātayet tatra sūtrāṇi koṣṭhānāṃ dṛśyate śatam |
catuścatvāriṃśadāḍhyaṃ paścāt ṣaṭtriṃśatāmbujam || 3-110 ||

koṣṭhaiḥ prakalpayet pīṭhaṃ paṅktyā naivātra vīthikā |
dvāraśobhe yathāpūrvamupaśobhā na dṛśyate || 3-111 ||

avaśiṣṭaiḥ padaiḥ kuryāt ṣaḍbhiḥ koṇāni tantravit |
vidadhyāt pūrvavaccheṣamevaṃ vā maṇḍalaṃ smṛtam || 3-112 ||

caturasre catuḥṣaṣṭipadānyāracayet sudhīḥ |
padmaiścaturbhiḥ padmaṃ syānmadhye tatparitaḥ punaḥ || 3-113 ||

vithīścatasraḥ kurvīta maṇḍalāntāvasānikāḥ |
diggateṣu catuṣkeṣu paṅkajāni samālikhet || 3-114 ||

vidiggatacatuṣkāṇi bhittvā ṣoṣaśadhā sudhīḥ |
mārjayet svastikākāraṃ śvetapītāruṇāsitaiḥ || 3-115 ||

rajobhiḥ pūrayet tāni svastikāni śivāditaḥ |
prākproktenaiva mārgeṇa śeṣamanyat samācaret || 3-116 ||

navanābhamidaṃ proktaṃ maṇḍalaṃ sarvasiddhidam |
pañcābjaṃ maṇḍalaṃ proktametat svastikavarjitam || 3-117 ||

dīkṣāyāṃ devapūjārthaṃ maṇḍalānāṃ catuṣṭayam |
sarvatantrānusāreṇa proktaṃ sarvasamṛddhidam || 3-118 ||

p. 158) (yāmale)

āraktavarṇaṃ tadvarṇaṃ śaktidvādaśasaṃyutam |
saṃpūjya dīpanāthaṃ ca bhairavaṃ prārthayediti || 3-119 ||

atitīkṣṇa mahākāya kalpāntadahanopama |
bhairavāya namastubhyamanujñāṃ dāturmahasi || 3-120 ||

kāntaṃ cāstraṃ samuccārya pūjāvedīṃ tato viśet |

(vaiṣṇavīkalpe)

saṃskṛtya cāsanasthānamāstīrya vihitāsanam || 3-121 ||
jalamānīya savyena saṃskuryāt tadyathāvidhi |

jalasaṃskārastatraivoktam |

vāyubījena saṃśodhya vahnibījena nirdahet || 3-122 ||

plāvayejjalabījena gāyatryā prokṣayet tataḥ |
tato dhenvāmṛtīkṛtya praṇavenābhimantrayet || 3-123 ||

tathā |

ātmavidyāśivaistattvaiḥ praṇavādiśiro'ntakaiḥ |
tajjalena trirācamya āsanaṃ śodhayet tataḥ || 3-124 ||


śiro'gnijāyā | śodhanaṃ cātrāstramantreṇa jalamekarūpamiti sāṃpradāyikāḥ |
tathā |


merupṛṣṭha ṛṣiḥ proktaḥ sutalaṃ chanda īritam |
kūrmo'tra devatā proktā āsanopaniveśane || 3-125 ||

viniyogaśca kathitaḥ paṭheddhṛtvāsanaṃ tataḥ |
pṛthvi tvayā dhṛtā lokā devi tvaṃ viṣṇunā dhṛtā || 3-126 ||

tvaṃ ca dhāraya māṃ nityaṃ pavitraṃ cāsanaṃ kuru |
māyābījaṃ samuccārya ādhāraśaktaye tataḥ || 3-127 ||

kamalāsanamābhāṣya ṅenamo'ntaṃ prapūjayet |
ātmamantreṇopaviśedāsane deśikottamaḥ || 3-128 ||

p. 159) duḥśilpiracitatvādinānādoṣopaśāntaye |

ātmamantrastu |

svanāmādyakṣaraṃ puṃsaḥ somasāmisamanvitam || 3-129 ||
ātmamantraṃ vijānīyāt .......................................|| 3-129ef ||

iti siddhāntasaṃgrahe proktaḥ | somasāmīti candrārdhamityarthaḥ |
(merutantre)

vedikāyāmāsane sa prāṅmukhaḥ saṃviśedatha |
baddhapadmāsano maunī ṛjukāyo jitendriyaḥ || 3-130 ||

deśikendrastu saṃsthāpya pūjopakaraṇāni ca |
dakṣiṇe vāmabhāge tu sthāpayet kalaśaṃ śubham || 3-131 ||

sugandhijalasaṃpūrṇaṃ hastaprakṣālanāya ca |
paścāt pātraṃ nidhāyātha dīpān prajvālayecchubhān || 3-132 ||

vyañjanaṃ mukuraṃ chatraṃ cāmaraṃ dikṣu mantravit |

sthāpayediti śeṣaḥ | prāṅmukha iti baddhapadmāsana iti copalakṣaṇam |

udaṅmukhaḥ prāṅmukho vā svastikāsanamāsthitaḥ || 3-133 ||

iti bhairavokteḥ |
(nibandhe)

tataḥ saṃkalpya vidhivat sūryāyārghaṃ nivedayet |

sūryārghadānamantra ukto (merutantre)

hrī/hrī/saḥ sagraharāśinakṣatrapadamuccaret || 3-134 ||

tataśca yogakaraṇaparivṛttāsaneti ca |

catustriṃśadakṣaro'yaṃ sūryasyārghanivedane |

tatraiva pūjāsaṃbhārānupakramya |

yathāyathaṃ tu saṃsthāpya saṃskuryāt tāni cātmavān || 3-136 ||

puṣpanaivedyagandhādīn hrā/hu/phaḍiti mantrataḥ |
p. 160) nārācamudrayā dṛṣṭyā samayā ca vilokayet || 3-137 ||

dṛṣṭaṃ vācātmani jñātaṃ samyak puṣpādidūṣaṇam |
aspṛśyasparśanaṃ cāpi yadanyāyārjitaṃ ca vā || 3-138 ||

tathā nirmālyasaṃsṛṣṭaṃ kīṭādyārohaṇaṃ ca yat |
tatsarvaṃ nāśamāyāti naivedyādyavalokanāt || 3-139 ||

tato ramiti mantreṇa śikhāṃ dīpasya saṃspṛśet |
sa tu syācchubhado dīpo niṣkravyādaḥ sukhapradaḥ || 3-140 ||

pataṅgakīṭakeśādidāhāt kravyādisaṃhate |
vasāmajjādisaṃbhūtairghṛtādyairupayojitam || 3-141 ||

ajñānarūpaṃ tatsarvaṃ doṣaṃ sparśādvinaśyati |
kṣrau/ bījamuccaran mantrī devatīrthena saṃspṛśet || 3-142 ||

pānīyaṃ ghaṭamadhyasthaṃ dṛṣṭvā samyak ca sādhakaḥ |
vāmena pāṇinā dhṛtvā vāmapārśvasthitaṃ ghaṭam || 3-143 ||

pātrādhāreṇa mantreṇa saṃskurvan saṃspṛśejjalam |
svasaṃjñādyakṣaraṃ vinducandrārdhapariyojitam || 3-144 ||

tatpātranāma ṅe'ntaṃ ca hṛdantaḥ pātramantrakaḥ |
yadajñānādamedhyādisaṃsṛṣṭiriha saṃgatā || 3-145 ||

yadanyadūṣaṇaṃ pātre toye vājñānato bhavet |
jalāśayaśavasparśājjalasthānācca saṃgatam || 3-146 ||

ityādidūṣaṇaṃ naśyet tat priye devapūjane |
tataḥ kṛtāñjalipuṭo vāmadakṣiṇapārśvayoḥ || 3-147 ||

guruṃ gaṇapatiṃ natvā purataḥ sveṣṭadevatām |

(yāmale)

akhaṇḍamaṇḍalākāraṃ vyāptaṃ yena carācaram || 3-148 ||

tatpadaṃ darśitaṃ yena tasmai śrīgurave namaḥ |
iti natvā guruṃ mantrī karaśuddhyādikaṃ caret || 3-149 ||

karaśuddhiprakārastu (merutantre)

sugandhipuṣpāṇyādāya candanāktāni mantravit |
hsau/ mantreṇa karayorastramantreṇa mardayet || 3-150 ||

tatpuṣpaṃ vāmahastena samādāya ca mastakam |
bhrāmayet paritastāraṃ japannāghrāya tat punaḥ || 3-151 ||

hau/ oṃ pūrvaṃ paṭhecchlokamimaṃ sa ca nigadyate |

mṛtyurogabhayakleśāḥ patantu ripumastake |

karaśuddhau tato'streṇa kuryāt tālatrayaṃ tataḥ |
ūrdhvordhvamastramantreṇa digbandhamapi deśikaḥ || 3-154 ||

tena saṃjanitaṃ tejo vahniprākāramudrayā |

prāṇāyāmalakṣaṇamuktam (sārasamuccaye)

prāṇāyāma iti prokto dvividhaḥ kathyate hi saḥ || 3-156 ||

sagarbho mantrajāpena nigarbho mātrayā bhavet |

mātrākāralakṣaṇaṃ ca (agastyasaṃhitāyām)

nalinīpatrasaṃhatyāṃ sūkṣmasūcyagrabhedane || 3-157 ||

dale dale ca yaḥ kālaḥ sa kālo lavavācakaḥ |
lavaistruṭistu triṃśadbhiḥ kalāṃ tāvattruṭiṃ vidhuḥ || 3-158 ||

kāṣṭhā tāvatkālā jñeyā tāvatkāṣṭhā nimeṣakaḥ |
svāṅgulisphoṭatulyaśca mātrāṣṭādaśabhiḥ smṛtā || 3-159 ||

p. 162) (merutantre)

madhyāmūlādyāvadagramaṅguṣṭhena nipīḍyate |

p. 166) suṣumṇāvartmanā brahmabījamantreṇa mastake |
sahasradalamadhyasthe saṃyojya paramātmani || 3-199 ||

(siddhāntasaṃgrahe)

tataḥ samastatattvāni tatraiva pravilāpayet |
pṛthvīmapsu ca tā vahnau vahniṃ vāyau samīraṇam || 3-200 ||

pravilāpya tathākāśe ākāśaṃ prakṛtau tataḥ |
aparabrahmarūpāṃ tāṃ māyāśaktiṃ parātmani || 3-201 ||

evaṃ samastadehādiprapañcaṃ paramātmani |
pravilāpya parabrahmarūpe tiṣṭhet kiyat kṣaṇam || 3-202 ||

(merutantre)

śarīre vāmakukṣau tu cintayet pāpapūruṣam |
vāmakukṣisthitaṃ pāpapuruṣaṃ kajjalaprabham || 3-203 ||

brahmahatyāśiraskaṃ ca svarṇasteyabhujadvayam |
surāpānahṛdā yuktaṃ gurutalpakaṭidvayam || 3-204 ||

tatsaṃsargapadadvandvamaṅgapratyaṅgapātakam |
upapātakaromāṇaṃ raktaśmaśruvilocanam || 3-205 ||

khaḍgacarmadharaṃ krūramaṅguṣṭhaparimāṇakam |
adhomukhaṃ mahābhīmaṃ rūkṣaṃ pāpaṃ vicintayet || 3-206 ||

(mahākālasaṃhitāyām)

vāmanāsāpuṭenātha pūrayitvā samīraṇam |
savinduvāyubījaṃ ca dhūmravarṇaṃ vibhāvya ca || 3-207 ||

tadevabījaṃ deveśi pañcāśadvāramīrayet |
tadutpannena vātena śuṣkaṃ dehaṃ vicintya ca || 3-208 ||

p. 167) sahaiva recayedvāyuṃ tato nāsāpuṭena ca |
vāmena vāyumuttolya sahasradalamadhyagam || 3-209 ||

vibhāvya paramātmānaṃ candrarūpaṃ varānane |
sānusvāraṃ vāyubījaṃ pañcāśadvāyumuccaran || 3-210 ||

tasmāccandrāt sudhāvṛṣṭyā dehamāplāvya sundari |
bhūbījena sanādena śuddhaṃ saṃyojya vigraham || 3-211 ||

līnīkṛtāni yānīha pañcabhūtāni vai purā |
yathāsthānaṃ sthāpayitvā brahmabījaṃ punargṛṇan || 3-212 ||

ahaṃkārādibhistattvaiḥ sahaiva paramātmani |
jīvātmānaṃ samākṛṣya sthāpayitvā hṛdambuje || 3-213 ||

devīrūpamathātmānaṃ cintayet kṣaṇamūrjitaḥ |


atra vāyubījādeḥ pañcāśadvārajapena pūrakādikamuktam |
(yāmalādau tu)

nābhideśe ca ya/ kāraṃ dhūmraṃ vāyuṃ vicintayet || 3-214 ||

tenaiva śodhayelliṅgatanuṃ ṣoḍaśamātrayā |
karmātmakaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭyā kumbhayitvā samācaret || 3-215 ||

dvātriṃśanmātrayā dakṣe recanaṃ tena vā punaḥ |

iti ṣoṣaśādimātrābhiḥ pūrakādikamuktamiti saṃkhyāvikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ |

(siddhāntasaṃgrahe)

punarutpātayeddehaṃ pavitraṃ paramātmanaḥ || 3-216 ||

parabrahmātmikā vidyā prakṛtirmātṛkā parā |
ajāyata jaganmāturākāśaṃ nabhaso'nilaḥ || 3-217 ||

samīraṇādabhūdvahnirvahnerāpastato mahī |

devatārādhane yogyamutpannamiti bhāvayet |
p. 168) bhūtaśuddhiriyaṃ proktā mahāpāpaughanāśinī || 3-219 ||


saṃkṣiptaprakāramāha (uḍḍīśe)


atha vā kuṇḍalīṃ devīṃ pañcabhūtādinā saha |

dhyānayogena manasā so'haṃso'haṃ vibhāvayet |


kvacicchaktiviśeṣe tu haṃsa iti bījādikaṃ paraśive saṃyojya so'hamitimantreṇa svasthānamānayedityuktam | (merau)


bhūtaśuddhiṃ vidhāyaivaṃ prāṇasthāpanamācaret || 3-221 ||

(jñānārṇave)

prāṇapratiṣṭhayā paścājjīvaṃ dehe niyojayet |

(merutantre)

prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā mātṛkānyāsamācaret |

(phetkāriṇītantre)

tatrādāvantarmātṛkānyāsaḥ | (merutantre)


athāntarmātṛkānyāsaṃ vakṣye taṃ śuddhikārakam || 3-225 ||

mūlādhārācchiraḥpadmaṃ spṛśantī vidyudākṛtiḥ || 3-227 ||
tayā spṛṣṭaśiraḥpadmādamṛtaughasvarūpiṇaḥ |

p. 169) nirgatānmātṛkāvarṇān suṣumṇāvartmanā tanum || 3-228 ||
sthāpayitvā sthitānetānevaṃ dhyātvā pravinyaset |

kaṇṭhe viśuddhicakrākhyaṃ padmaṃ ṣoḍaśapatrakam || 3-229 ||
urdhvāsyaṃ prāgdalāt tatra saṃsthitāḥ ṣoḍaśa svarāḥ |

evaṃ saṃcintya tārādiṃ vinduyuktaṃ namo'ntakam || 3-230 ||

anāditvānna ṛṣyādirbrahmavaddhyānamasya tu |


atha bahirmātṛkānyāsaḥ | (śāradāyām)


ṛṣirbrahmā samuddiṣṭo gāyatrī chanda īritam || 3-237 ||
sarasvatī samākhyātā devatā deśikottamaiḥ |

aklībahrasvadīrghāntagataiḥ ṣaḍvargakaiḥ kramāt || 3-238 ||
ṣaḍaṅgāni vidheyāni jātiyuktāni deśikaiḥ |

p. 170) (merutantre)

bahirnyāsamatho vakṣye pūrvoktabrahmarandhrake || 3-239 ||

dhyānaṃ tatraiva |


pañcāśadvarṇabhedairvihitavadanadoḥpādayukkukṣivakṣo-

bāhvoḥ saṃdhiṣu sāgreṣu kacavargau nyaset sudhīḥ |

(merau)

vindusargayutān ḍadiṭhāntān varṇān sthitau nyaset |

nyāsāḥ kāryāstu vaṭubhiḥ sthitisaṃhārasṛṣṭayaḥ |

atha kalāmātṛkānyāsastatraiva |

dhyānamāha |


hastaiḥ padmaṃ rathāṅgaṃ guṇamatha hariṇaṃ pustakaṃ varṇamālāṃ

dhyātvaivaṃ tārapūrvāṃ tāmakārādyāṃ ca mātṛkām |

pūrvoktaśuddhamātrāṇāṃ nyāsasthāneṣu vinyaset |


tārapūrvāmityādi | etena oṃ aṃnivṛttyai nama ityādayaḥ prayogāḥ saṃpannāḥ | kalānāmānyāha |


nivṛttiśca pratiṣṭhā ca vidyā śāntistathendhikā || 3-257 ||

dīpitā recikā cāpi mocikā ca parābhidhā |

vyāpinī vyomarūpā cānantā sṛṣṭiḥ samṛddhikā |
smṛtirmedhā tataḥ kāntirlakṣmīrdyutiḥ sthirā sthitiḥ || 3-259 ||

siddhirjayā pālinī ca śāntirīśvarikā ratiḥ |

dīrghā tīkṣṇā tathā raudrī bhayā nidrā ca tandrikā |

pītā śvetāruṇā paścādasitānantayānvitā |

tatra nādātmano jātāḥ svarajāstu sadāśivāt |

pītā akṣasrajaṃ padmamabhayaṃ ca kamaṇḍalum |

makārādrudrato jātāḥ payavargakalā nava |

atha keśavādimātṛkānyāsa uktastatraiva |

keśavādināmānyāha |


keśavaḥ kīrtisaṃyuktaḥ kāntirnārāyaṇānvitā |

p. 176) hrasvadīrghāntara iti | a/ ka/ kha/ ga/ gha/ ṅa/ ā/ hsāmityādibhiḥ ṣaḍaṅgaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ |

bandhūkakāñcananibhaṃ rucirākṣamālāṃ
pāśāṅkuśau ca varadaṃ nijabāhudaṇḍaiḥ |

hsau/ ca mātṛkāmuktvā devānte ceśamaccaret |

tvagasṛṅmāṃsamedosthimajjāśukrāṇyasūn vadet |

tvagiti | hsau/ ya/ tvagātmane bālīśāya sumukhyai nama ityevaṃ yādidaśavarṇeṣu tvagādiyogaḥ kartavyaḥ ityarthaḥ |

śivaśaktināmānyāha |


śrīkaṇṭhapūrṇodaryau cānanto virajayānvitaḥ |

p. 177) krodhīśaśca mahākālyā caṇḍeśena sarasvatī |
pañcāntake sarvasiddhirgauryā yuktaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 3-300 ||

śivottameśo vinyasyo yuktastrailokyavidyayā |
ekarudro mantraśaktyā kūrmeśaścāṣṭaśaktiyuk || 3-301 ||

ekanetro bhūtimātrāyukto'tha caturānanaḥ |
lambodaryā yutaḥ proktastvajeśo drāviṇīyutaḥ || 3-302 ||

sarveśo nāgarīyuktaḥ someśaḥ khecarīyutaḥ |
lāṅgalīśaśca mañjaryā dārukeśena rūpiṇī || 3-303 ||

vīriṇyā tvardhanārīśo hyumākāntaḥ punaryutaḥ |
kākodaryā tathāṣāḍhī pūtanāyukta īritaḥ || 3-304 ||

daṇḍīśo bhadrakālīyugadrīśo yoginīyutaḥ |
mīneśaḥ śaṅkhinīyukto meṣeśastarjanīyutaḥ || 3-305 ||

lohitaḥ kālarātryā ca śikhīśaḥ kūrdinīyutaḥ |
chagalaṇḍaḥ kapardinyā dviraṇḍeśaśca vajrayā || 3-306 ||

mahākālo jayāyukto bālīśaḥ sumukhīyutaḥ |
bhujaṅgo revatīyuktaḥ pinākī mādhavīyutaḥ || 3-307 ||

khaḍgīśo vāruṇīyukto vakeśo vāyavīyutaḥ |
śveto rakṣovadhāriṇyā bhṛguḥ sahajayā yutaḥ || 3-308 ||

nakulīśaśca lakṣmīyuk śiveśo vyāpinīyutaḥ |
saṃvartako mahāmāyāyukta etāstu mātṛkāḥ || 3-309 ||

p. 178) śrīkaṇṭhādyā ya īśāntastatra neśaṃ prayojayet |


ya iti | ya īśānto bhavati tadante punarīśapadaṃ na deyamityarthaḥ | etena śrīkaṇṭhādināmānte īśapadadānamāvaśyakamityanujñātam |

	tathā |


sarverudrāḥ smṛtāraktā dhṛtaśūlakapālakāḥ || 3-310 ||

śaktayo rudrapīṭhasthāḥ sindūrāruṇavigrahāḥ |
raktotpalakapālābhyāmalaṃkṛtakarāmbujāḥ || 3-311 ||


atha bhuvaneśvarīmātṛkānyāsamāha tatraiva |


yenopāsyā mahāvidyā daśa tasyādhikāritāḥ |
bhuvaneśībījapūrvamātṛkānyāsane surāḥ || 3-312 ||

ṛṣiḥ śaktistu gāyatrī chandaḥ syānmātṛkāmayī |
bhuvaneśī devathātha niyogaḥ sveṣṭasiddhaye || 3-313 ||

hrasvadīrghāntaragataiḥ ṣaḍvargairdīrghasaṃyutaiḥ |
prāgvaddharaiḥ ṣaḍaṅgaṃ syādaṅguṣṭhādi tathaiva ca || 3-314 ||


harairiti | ākārādiṣaḍdīrghasvarayuktairhakārarakarairityarthaḥ | prayogastu | a/ ka/ kha/ ga/ gha/ ṅa/ ā/ hrā/ hṛdayāya nama ityādi |

dhyānamāha |


udyatkoṭidivākarapratinibhā tuṅgorupīnastanī
baddhārdhendukirīṭahārarasanāmañjīrasaṃśobhitā |
bibhrāṇā karapaṅkajairjapavaṭīṃ pāśāṅkuśau pustakaṃ
diśyānno jagadīśvarī trinayanā padme niṣaṇṇā sukham || 3-315 ||

lajjābījaṃ pūrvamuktvā mātṛkānyāsa eva hi |
bhuvaneśīmātṛkāṇāṃ nyāsakairyatra sādhane || 3-316 ||


atha lakṣmīmātṛkānyāsastatraiva |


śrībījapūrvakaḥ kāryo lakṣmīkāmairgṛhasthitaiḥ |
p. 179) ṛṣirbhṛguśca gāyatrī chando mātṛsvarupiṇī || 3-317 ||

lakṣmīstu devatā proktā harasthāne śarau kuru |
nyāsārthaṃ tu ṣaḍaṅgādeḥ śeṣaṃ śrībījapūrvakam || 3-318 ||


harasthāne śarau iti | bhuvaneśīmātṛkoktahakārarakārayoḥ sthāne śakārarakarau dadyādityarthaḥ | tena hrāmityādeḥ sthāne śrāmityādi sidhyati |

	dhyānamāha |


vidyuddāmasamaprabhāṃ himagiriprakhyaiścaturbhirgajaiḥ
śuṇḍādaṇḍasamuddhṛtāmṛtaghaṭairāsiñcyamānāmimām |
bibhrāṇāṃ karapaṅkajairjapavaṭīṃ padmadvayaṃ pustakaṃ
bhāsvadratnasamujjvalāṃ kucanatāṃ dhyāyejjagatsvāminīm || 3-319 ||


atha saṃmohanamātṛkānyāsastatraiva |


kāmanāmātrasidhyarthaṃ kāmabījādi pūrvavat |
kuryācca mātṛkānyāsaṃ muniḥ saṃmohano mataḥ || 3-320 ||

gāyatrī chanda uddiṣṭaṃ devatā mātṛkāmayī |
saṃmohinī nāma devī pūrvayuktyā ṣaḍaṅgakam || 3-321 ||


pūrvayukteti | lakṣmīmātṛkoktayuktyā saṃmohanamātṛkāyāḥ kāmabījapūrvakatvena hakārarakārayoḥ sthāne saṃyuktakakāralakārau datvā ṣaḍaṅganyāsaḥ kārya ityarthaḥ |

dhyānamāha |


bālārkakoṭirucirāṃ sphaṭikākṣamālāṃ
kodaṇḍamikṣujanitasmarapañcabāṇām |
vidyāṃ ca hastakamalairdadhatīṃ trinetrāṃ
dhyāyet samastajananīṃ navacandracūḍām || 3-322 ||


saṃmohanamātṛkāntaramāha |


śaktiśrīkāmabījādimātṛkānyāsato nṛṇām |
śatrutvādyā mantradoṣā naśyanti bahuvarṇajāḥ || 3-323 ||

mahāsaṃmohana ṛṣirgāyatrī chanda īritam |
p. 180) saṃmohinī nāma devī nyāsaḥ prāṅmātṛkāsamaḥ || 3-324 ||


dhyānaṃ yathā |


dhyāyeyamakṣavalayekṣuśarāśapāśāṃ
padmadvayāṅkuśaśarāṃ navapustakaṃ ca |
ābribhratīṃ nijabhujairaruṇāṃ kucārttāṃ
saṃmohinīṃ trinayanāṃ navacandracūḍām || 3-325 ||


atha gaṇeśamātṛkānyāsastatraiva |


sarvavighnanirāsārthamavaśyaṃ nyāsamācaret |
gaṇeśamātṛkāyāstu munirgaṇaka īritaḥ || 3-326 ||

nivṛdgāyatrikā chando devaḥ śaktivināyakaḥ |
smṛtyā dīrghāḍhyayā tvaṅgaṃ kṛtvā dhyāyedgajānanam || 3-327 ||


smṛtirgakāraḥ |
dhyānaṃ yathā |


śūlāṅkuśavarābhītipāṇiṃ raktābjahastayā |
priyayāliṅgitaṃ raktaṃ trinetraṃ gaṇapaṃ bhaje || 3-328 ||
evaṃ dhyātvā nyaset svīyabījapūrvākṣarānvitam || 3-328ef ||


svīyeti | svīyabījaṃ gamiti bījaṃ pūrvaṃ yeṣāṃ tādṛśāni yānyakṣarāṇi akārādīni tairanvitaṃ yathā bhavati tathā vakṣyamāṇaśaktisaṃyuktān vakṣyamāṇagaṇeśān nyasedityarthaḥ |
tathā |


vighneśo hrīsamāyukto vighnarājaḥ śriyā yutaḥ |
vināyakaḥ puṣṭiyuktaḥ śāntiyuktaḥ śivottamaḥ || 3-329 ||

vighnakṛt svastisaṃyukto vighnaharttrā sarasvatī |
gaṇastu svāhayā yukta ekadantastu medhayā || 3-330 ||

dvidantaḥ kāntisaṃyukto gajavaktreṇa kāminī |
nirañjano mohinīyuk kapardī tu nadīyutaḥ || 3-331 ||

p. 181) dīrghajihvaḥ pārvatīyuk jvālinyā śaṅkukarṇakaḥ |
vṛṣadhvajo nandayā yuk suyaśāgaṇanāyakau || 3-332 ||

gajendraḥ kāmarūpiṇyā śūrpakarṇastathomayā |
trilocanastejovatyā lambodarastu satyayā || 3-333 ||

mahānandaśca vighneśyā caturmūrtiḥ surūpayā |
sadāśivaḥ kāmadāyugāmodo madajihvayā || 3-334 ||

durmukho bhūtisaṃyuktaḥ sumukho bhautikāyutaḥ |
pramodaḥ sitayā yukta ekapādo ramāyutaḥ || 3-335 ||

dvijihvo mahiṣīyuktaḥ śūrasyāpi bhujaṅginī |
vīro'pi karṇayā yuktaḥ ṣaṇmukho bhṛkuṭīyutaḥ || 3-336 ||

varado lajjayā vāmadevaḥ syāddīrghaghoṇayā |
dhanurdharāvakratuṇḍau dviraṇḍo yāminīyutaḥ || 3-337 ||

senānī rātrisaṃyuktaḥ kāmāndho grāmaṇīyutaḥ |
mattaḥ śaśiprabhāyukto vimale lolalocanā || 3-338 ||

mattavāhanacāñcalye jaṭī dīptisamanvitaḥ |
muṇḍī subhagayā yuktaḥ khaḍgī durbhagayā tathā || 3-339 ||

vareṇyaśca śivāyukto bhagīyugvṛṣaketanaḥ |
bhaktipriyo bhaginyā tu gaṇeśo bhoginīyutaḥ || 3-340 ||

meghanādaḥ subhagayā vyāpī syāt kālarātriyuk |
gaṇeśvaraḥ kāliketi proktā vighneśamātṛkā || 3-341 ||

tvagādiyogo yādīnāṃ pūrvavat parikīrtitaḥ |


(nibandhe)


omādyante namonto vā savindurvinduvarjitaḥ || 3-342 ||

pañcāśadvarṇavinyāsaḥ kramādukto manīṣibhiḥ |
p. 182) caturdhā mātṛkā proktā kevalā vindusaṃyutā || 3-343 ||

savisargā sobhayā ca rahasyaṃ śṛṇu kathyate |
vidyākarī kevalā ca sobhayā bhuktidāyinī || 3-344 ||

savisargā putradātrī savindurvittadāyinī |


(kulāvatāre)


māyālakṣmībījapūrvo vāgbhavādyo'tha vā punaḥ || 3-345 ||

tritārādyo ramādyo vā nyastavyaḥ sādhakottamaiḥ |


tritāraśca |


vāgbhavaṃ kāmarājaṃ ca śrībījaṃ ca tritārakam || 3-346 ||

śaktiśrīkāmabījaṃ vā tritāraṃ munayo viduḥ |


iti tantrāntare proktam |
bījakośe tu |


vāgbhavaṃ bhuvaneśāni śrībījaṃ ca tritārakam- ityuktam || 3-347 ||


(siddhāntasāre)


vāgbhavādyā vākpradā syāt tritārādyā tu kāmadā |
śrībījādyā mahaiśvaryadāyinī mātṛkā smṛtā || 3-348 ||


(vīracūḍāmaṇau)


mātṛkātritayaṃ kuryāt sṛṣṭisaṃhārakasthitim |
nyāsaṃ kuryānmaheśāni kalpoktaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ || 3-349 ||


(kulāvatāre)


sṛṣṭestu sutapuṣṭyādi sthitestatpālanaṃ bhavet |
saṃhṛterdoṣasaṃhāraṃ tasmāt tritayamācaret || 3-350 ||


(siddhāntasāre)


sarge sargānvitān nyasyet saṃhṛtau ca savindukān |
ḍādiṭhāntān nyasedvarṇān sthitau tadubhayānvitān || 3-351 ||


p. 183) (triśaktiratne tu)


akathāditribhedena vibhajya manuvallipim |
kavargaṃ prathamaṃ nyasyedavargaṃ ca tataḥ param || 3-352 ||

tavargaṃ vinyaset paścāt sthitireṣā prakīrtitā- ityuktam |


mātṛkānyāsamudrā uktāstantrāntare |


lalāṭe'nāmikāmadhye nyasecca mukhavṛttake || 3-353 ||

tarjanīmadhyamānāmā vinyaseccaiva netrayoḥ |
tarjanyanāme saṃyojya nyaset sādhakasattamaḥ || 3-354 ||

aṅguṣṭhaṃ karṇayornyasya kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhakau nasoḥ |
madhyamāṃ gaṇḍayornyasya madhyamāmoṣṭhayornyaset || 3-355 ||

anāmāṃ dantayornyasya madhyamāmuttamāṅgake |
mukhe'nāmāṃ madhyamāṃ ca haste pāde ca pārśvayoḥ || 3-356 ||

kaniṣṭhānāmikāmadhyā nyastavyāḥ pṛṣṭhatastathā |
tāḥ sāṅguṣṭhā nābhideśe sarvāḥ kukṣau ca vinyaset || 3-357 ||

hṛdaye sādhakaḥ sarvamaṃsayośca kakutsthale |
hṛtpūrvahastapatkukṣimukheṣu talameva ca || 3-358 ||

etāstu mātṛkāmudrāḥ krameṇa parikīrtitāḥ |
ajñātvā vinyasedyastu nyāsaḥ syāt tasya niṣphalaḥ || 3-359 ||


(mantratantraprakāśe)


puṣpairanāmayā vāpi manasā vā nyasedasūn |


anāmayetyasya sāṅguṣṭhayeti |


aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ tu nyāsaḥ sarvatra saṃmataḥ || 3-360 ||


iti (padyavāhinī) vacanāt |

tena ca | puṣpairdevatāmūrtau aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ svadehe manasā mūlādhārādiṣu nyāso bodhyaḥ | idaṃ ca ṛṣyādikaraṣaḍaṅganyāsabahirmātṛkādivyatirikteṣu tatra mudrāntarābhidhānāt |

p. 184) (ḍāmare)


kṛtvā tu mātṛkānyāsaṃ nyāsānanyān samācaret |
mūlamantrasya ṛṣyādikarāṅgādi tato nyaset || 3-361 ||


(yoginītantre)


ṛṣyādīn vinyaseddevi caturthīhṛdayāntakān |


(bṛhanmatsyasūkte)


ṛṣinyāso mūrdhni deśe chandastu mukhapaṅkaje || 3-362 ||

devatā hṛdaye caiva bījaṃ tu guhyadeśake |
śaktiṃ ca pādayoścaiva sarvāṅge kīlakaṃ nyaset || 3-363 ||


(merutantre)


ṛṣergurutvācchirasi varṇatvācchandaso mukhe |
dhāraṇaṃ procyate citte jñeyatvāddevatā hṛdi || 3-364 ||


ṛṣistu |


maheśvaramukhājjñātvā yaḥ sākṣāt tapasā manum |
saṃsādhayati śuddhātmā sa tasya ṛṣirīritaḥ || 3-365 ||


iti (kālānalatantre) proktam |
(tathā)


gurutvānmastake cāsya nyāsaśca parikīrtitaḥ |


(merutantre)


ṛ-gatāviti dhātostu ṛvarṇaḥ prāpako mataḥ || 3-366 ||

ṣivarṇārthastu vijñānamatastatprāpakastu saḥ |


(bṛhanmatsyasūkte)


sarveṣāṃ mantratattvānāṃ chādanācchanda ucyate || 3-367 ||

akṣaratvāt padatvācca mukhe chandaḥ samīritam |
ṛṣicchando'parijñānānna mantraphalabhāgbhavet || 3-368 ||

daurbalyaṃ cāpi mantrāṇāṃ viniyogamajānatām |


p. 185) ṛṣyādinyāsamudrā uktāstantrāntare |


ṛṣicchandodevatānāṃ nyāse cāṅgulayaḥ smṛtāḥ || 3-369 ||

catasro'ṅguṣṭharahitāḥ śaktyantaṃ vinyaset tataḥ |
kīlakaṃ vyastahastābhyāṃ sarvāṅge sādhakāgraṇīḥ || 3-370 ||


arthāṅgamudrāstatraiva |


hṛdaye madhyamānāmātarjanībhirnyasedbudhaḥ |
madhyamātarjanībhyāṃ ca śirasi nyāsamācaret || 3-371 ||

aṅguṣṭhena śikhāyāṃ ca daśabhiḥ kavace nyaset |
netreṣu tisṛbhiśca syādastramūrdhvordhvagaṃ triśaḥ || 3-372 ||

madhyamātarjanībhyāṃ syādaṅgamudrāḥ smṛtā imāḥ |


ṣaḍaṅgārthamāha |


hṛdayāyeti śabdena hṛtstho devaḥ savigrahaḥ || 3-373 ||

ucyate nama ityasya jñānaṃ tadviṣayaṃ param |
śiraḥśabdo devatāyā utkṛṣṭatvābhidhāyakaḥ || 3-374 ||

svāheti viṣayāḥ sarve devatāyāṃ samarpitāḥ |
śikhāyai tatsvarūpatvaṃ vaṣaḍvāpi taducyate || 3-375 ||

devatāyā vyāpakatvaṃ kavacāyābhidhīyate |
humiti vyāpakaṃ tejo devatāyāḥ prakāśyate || 3-376 ||

netraśabdena devasya nityajñānaṃ prakāśyate |
vauṣaḍiti tadevoktamastraśabdena vāraṇam || 3-377 ||

aniṣṭasya phaṭśabdena dāhakaṃ teja ucyate |
jñātvaivamaṅgamantrārthamaṅganyāsaṃ karoti yaḥ || 3-378 ||

karagāstasya sarvārthā pūjyate tridaśairapi |


(merutantre)


krīḍārthakāddivo dhātoḥ saṃpūrṇaṃ devatāpadam || 3-379 ||

p. 186) sādhakāya dadātīti devatā parikīrtitā |
rajyamāno hṛdātmānaṃ hṛdaye'sti cidātmakaḥ || 3-380 ||

tasmāt tatra namaskuryānmukhyāṅguṣṭhena saṃspṛśet |
svāhayā kriyate samyagviṣayāharaṇaṃ yataḥ || 3-381 ||

anāmātarjanībhyāṃ tairākṛṣṭaiḥ kaṃ niṣevyate |
śikhā maho vaṣaḍvāṅgaṃ śikhayātmatanugrahaḥ || 3-382 ||

yasmāt tasmādvaṣaḍiti kriyate'ṅgulamadhyayā |
grahaṇārtha-kavac-dhāturhu/ mantreṇa sa gṛhyate || 3-383 ||

śarīre'tastu kavacaṃ tarjanyānāmayāpi tat |
yathārthajñānakṛdvauṣaṭ tannetrābhyāṃ prajāyate || 3-384 ||

tasmāduktaṃ tatra vauṣaṭ tarjanyādyaistu tat smṛtam |
ekākṣatvāt kuberādidaivate ṅe'ntamuccaret || 3-385 ||

bhyāntaṃ viṣṇvādike rudrādau tu netratrayātmakam |
ādhyātmikādirūpaṃ yat sādhakasya vināśayet || 3-386 ||

avidyājātamastraṃ tat phaṭtālābhyāṃ prapañcyate |


(yāmale)


nyasedaṅguṣṭhamukhyeṣu jātibhirnamasātha vā || 3-387 ||

hṛdādiṣu ṣaḍaṅgeṣu jātiṣaṭkena vinyaset |
namaḥ svāhā vaṣaṭ hu/ vauṣaṭ phaḍetāśca jātayaḥ || 3-388 ||


anyatrāpi |


jātiṣaṭkamiti proktaṃ hṛdādyastrāntasaṃyutam |


tathā ca (śāradāyām)


hṛdayādiṣu vinyasyedaṅgamantrā/stataḥ sudhīḥ || 3-389 ||

hṛdayāya namaḥ pūrvaṃ śirase vahnivallabhām |
p. 187) śikhāyai vaṣaḍityuktaṃ kavacāya humīritam || 3-390 ||

netratrayāya vauṣaṭ syādastrāya phaḍiti kramāt |
ṣaḍaṅgamantrānityuktān ṣaḍaṅgeṣu niyojayet || 3-391 ||


aṅganyāsasya nityatoktā (jñānamālāyām)


pūjājapārcanāhomāḥ siddhamantrakṛtā api |
aṅganyāsena vidhurā na dāsyanti phalānyamī || 3-392 ||


(merutantre)


ṣaḍaṅgaṃ vinyasenmantrī pañcāṅgaṃ vā yathāvidhi |
yasya mantrasya pañcāṅgaṃ netraṃ tasya vihīyate || 3-393 ||

aṅgairvihīnasya manoraṅgaṃ tenaiva kalpayet |


tathā |


āgamoktena mārgeṇa nyāsān nityaṃ karoti yaḥ || 3-394 ||

devatābhāvamāpnoti mantrasiddhiḥ prajāyate |
yo nyāsakavacacchanno mantraṃ japati taṃ priye || 3-395 ||

dṛṣṭvā vighnāḥ palāyante siṃhaṃ dṛṣṭvā yathā gajāḥ |
akṛtvā nyāsajālaṃ yo mūḍhātmā kurute japam || 3-396 ||

vighnaiḥ sa bādhyate nūnaṃ vyāghrairmṛgaśiśuryathā |
nyāsānāṃ pracuratvena phalānāmapi bhūritā || 3-397 ||

uktanyāso na hi tyājyastvadhikaṃ kāmataścaret |


(yāmale)


tatastu yogapīṭhākhyaṃ svadehe nyāsamācaret || 3-398 ||
ādhāraśaktiṃ prakṛtiṃ tathā kūrmamanantakam |


anyatra tu mūlaprakratyanantaraṃ kālāgnirudro'pyuktaḥ |


tataśca mūlaprakṛtiṃ rudraṃ kalānalāhvayam- iti || 3-399 ||


merau tu mūlaprakṛteḥ prāgeva kālāgnirudra uktaḥ | tadanantaramādhāraśaktiruktā |
p. 188) tathā |


varāhaṃ pṛthivīṃ kṣīrasindhuṃ dvīpaṃ ca ratnajam |


vaiṣṇave tu śvetadvīpa uktaḥ |


maṇijaṃ maṇḍapaṃ kalpapādapaṃ maṇivedikām || 3-400 ||

ratnapīṭhaṃ tathaitāni mantrī hṛdyeva vinyaset |


kva cinmaṇimaṇḍapānantaraṃ nandanodyānamapyuktam |
(śāradāyām)


aṃsoruyugmayorvidvān prādakṣiṇyena deśikaḥ || 3-401 ||

dharme jñānaṃ ca vairāgyamaiśvaryaṃ vinyaset kramāt |
dharmādayaḥ smṛtāḥ pādāḥ pīṭhagātrāṇi cāpare || 3-402 ||

mukhapārśvanābhipārśveṣvadharmādīn prakalpayet |

eṣu svasvakalā nyasyennāmādyakṣarapūrvikāḥ |
akārādyaṃ nyaset sūryamukārādyaṃ niśākaram || 3-404 ||

makārādyaṃ nyasedvahniṃ kalāmaṇḍalasaṃyutam |
sattvādī/strīn guṇān nyasyet tathaivātra gurūttamaḥ || 3-405 ||

sattvaṃ rajastama iti yasya bhedāḥ samīritāḥ |
ātmānamantarātmanaṃ paramātmānamatra tu || 3-406 ||

ādibījatrayaṃ nyasya śaktibījapurassaram |
jñānātmānaṃ pravinyasya nyaset pīṭhamanuṃ tataḥ || 3-407 ||

evaṃ dehamaye pīṭhe cintayediṣṭadevatām |


devatādhyānasya dvaividhyamuktam (merutantre)


dhyānaṃ kuryāt tato tattatkalpoktenaiva vartmanā || 3-408 ||

p. 189) praphulle somasūryāgnimaṇḍalena virājite |
svīyeṣṭadevatāṃ tatra vidyādāgamabodhitām || 3-410 ||

evaṃ yadvedanaṃ taddhi saguṇaṃ dhyānamucyate |
yajjīvabrahmaṇoraikyaṃ so'haṃ syāmiti vedanam || 3-411 ||

tadeva nirguṇaṃ dhyānamiti brahmavido viduḥ- iti |


atra nyāsānantaraṃ dhyānāt prāgeva mudrāpradarśanamuktam |
(merutantre)


karāṅganyāsamukhyā/stu nyāsānanyān samācaret || 3-412 ||

tattatkalpoditān paścāt tattanmudrāḥ pradarśayet- iti |


anyatra tu |


evaṃ dhyātvā maheśāni tato mudrāḥ pradarśayet- iti || 3-413 ||


dhyānāntaraṃ taduktam |
(mahātharvaṇarahasye) tu nyāsadhyānāntaryāgānuktvā |


tataśca tanmayo bhūtvā mudrā etāḥ pradarśayet- ityuktamiti |


tatra kālavikalpo bodhyaḥ |
mudrābandhane tatpradarśanatyāgayośca mantrabhedā (merutantre) uktāḥ |

darśayenmūlamantreṇa hsū/ bījena saṃtyajet |


(sanatkumārasaṃhitāyām)


akṛtvā mānasaṃ yāgaṃ na kuryādbahirarcanam || 3-415 ||


antaryāgavidhiḥ (bṛhaduttaratantre)


hṛtpadmakarṇikāmadhye yogapīṭhopari sthitām |

saṃpūjya vidhivat paścānmānasaṃ homamācaret |


(śmāmārahasya) dhṛtavacanāni |


amāyāmanahaṃkārasarāgamamadaṃ tathā || 3-417 ||

p. 190) amohadamadambhaṃ ca adveṣākṣobhakau tathā |

ahiṃsāparamaṃ puṣpaṃ puṣpamindriyanigraham |

ityuktairuttamaiḥ puṣpaiḥ pūjayet paradevatām |


(merutantre)


mūlādhāre catuṣkoṇamagnikuṇḍaṃ vicintayet || 3-420 ||

(śyāmārahasyādau) tu mūlānte |

svāhā iti prathamāhutiḥ |
mūlānte |

suṣumṇāvartmanā nityamakṣavṛttīrjuhomyaham |

antarnirantaranirindhanamedhamāne

svāhā- iti caturthāhutiḥ | iti ślokacatuṣṭayenāhuticatuṣṭayamuktam | 

(merutantre) īḍayā vāyumākṛṣya gudaṃ kiṃcidākuñcya jihvāṃ tālugatāṃ vidhāya mūlādhārāt kuṇḍalinīmutthāpya sahasradalakamalakarṇikāntargataparamaśivena saha tāṃ saṃyojya proktapīṭhe devatāmarccayediti viśeṣa uktaḥ |
	(yathā)

p. 192) granthiḥ kuṇḍalinī proktā kalānte merusaṃsthitiḥ |

(merutantre)


athārghādikapātrāṇāṃ phalaṃ vakṣye śubhāśubham |

p. 193) śaktiviṣayamidam |

kapālādividhirvīraparaḥ  |

(yāmale)

śaṅkhasthāpanavidhirukto (merutantre) svavāme ityupakramya |

p. 198) arthapradadvādaśeti kalātmane vadet tataḥ |

u/ sa/ kāmapradetyutkā ṣoḍaśeti kalātmane |
namaḥ pañcadaśārṇo'yaṃ mantraḥ śaṅkhaprapūraṇe || 3-494 ||


tathā tantrāntare'pi |


karṇacandrau vinduyutau kāmapradapadaṃ tataḥ |
ṣoḍaśānte kalāśabdamātmānte hṛdayāṇunā || 3-495 ||

gandhapuṣpaiḥ samabhyarcya kalāṣoḍaśakaṃ yajet |


	atra karṇa ukāraścandro'rdhacandro vinduranusvārastena umityekameva bījaṃ vidhāya muktāvalyāmuddhṛtam | sa eva prakāro nṛsiṃhaṭhakkurairapyavicārya svīyagranthe tārābhaktisudhārṇave dhṛtaḥ | vastutaḥ karṇa ukāraścandraḥ sakārastayorvinduyogācca u/ samiti bījadvayameva |


sakāraḥ śaktināmā ca candrapīyūṣaśītalaḥ || 3-496 ||


iti varṇakośāt |


śivaḥ śaktiḥ kāmaḥ kṣitiratha raviḥ śītakiraṇaḥ
smaro haṃsaḥ śakrastadanu ca parāmāraharayaḥ |
amī hṛllekhābhistisṛbhiravasāneṣu ghaṭitā
bhajante varṇāste tava janani nāmāvayavatām || 3-497 ||


	ityādau candravācakaśītakiraṇapadasya sakāraparatvadarśanācca | na ca candrapadasya kvacidardhacandraparatvadarśanādatraikameva bījamastviti vācyam | bījadvayaprakāśakamerutantravirodhāt |
p. 199) tathā |


tat toyaṃ gandhapuṣpādyaiḥ pūjayitvā samarcayet |
kalāṣoḍaśakaṃ cāndraṃ paścāt sarvakalāsu ca || 3-498 ||

prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ kurvīta pūjānte sadhākastataḥ |


vahnyarkacandrāṇāṃ kalāḥ prāguktāḥ | tatpūjanaprakārāśca (kulārṇave)


āgneyā yādivarṇotthā daśa dharmapradāḥ kalāḥ || 3-499 ||

kabhādyā vasudāḥ saurāṣṭhaḍāntā dvādaśeritāḥ |
saumyāḥ kāmapradāyinyaḥ ṣoḍaśa svarajāḥ kalāḥ || 3-500 ||


	vinduyuktān yādidaśa varṇān pūrvaṃ datvāgnikalāḥ | kādiṭhāntān bhādiḍāntān pratilomavarṇā/śca vinduyuktānādau datvā sūryakalāḥ | akārādiṣoḍaśasvarān vinduyuktānādau datvā candrakalāśca pūjyā ityarthaḥ | (merutantre)


prāgvat tīrthaṃ samāvāhya mūlamantreṇa tatra tu |
saṃyojya netramantreṇa puṣpaṃ nyasya śikhāṇunā || 3-501 ||

pradarśya gālinīṃ mudrāṃ mudrayā dhenusaṃjñayā |
amṛtīkṛtya vidhivat sudhābījena sādhakaḥ || 3-502 ||

dhyātveṣṭadevatāṃ tatra sakalīkaraṇaṃ caret |
aṅganyāsena cāstreṇa kalayecca diśo daśa || 3-503 ||

netramantreṇa saṃvīkṣya mudrayā śaṅkhasaṃjñayā |
avaṣṭabhya tato dīpyamudrayā yonirūpayā || 3-504 ||

gandhapuṣpādibhistatra pūjayediṣṭadevatām |
ṣaḍaṅgāni ca saṃpūjya mantrayenmūlamantrataḥ || 3-505 ||

aṣṭakṛttvastataścārghyaṃ chādayenmatsyamudrayā |


vaiṣṇave tu yonimudrāsthāne dhenumudraiva | taduktamāgastyena |


astramantreṇa saṃrakṣya kavacenāvaguṇṭhayet || 3-506 ||

p. 200) darśayeddhenumudrāṃ ca bodhayet tatsvamudrayā- iti |


svamudrayā śaṅkhamudrayā | mudrāstvagre vakṣyāmaḥ | (merau)


tacchaṅkhasthaṃ jalaṃ kiṃ cit kumbhatoye viniḥkṣipet || 3-507 ||


tantrāntare |


svadakṣe prokṣaṇīpātraṃ saṃsthāpya sādhakottamaḥ |
taduttare pādyapātramarghadvandvaṃ ca kalpayet || 3-508 ||


arghadvandvamiti | sāmānyārghaṃ viśeṣārghaṃ cetyarthaḥ |
arghapātrapramāṇamuktam (siddhāntasāre)

vasvaṅgulaṃ vā kurvīta netrāṅgulasamucchritam || 3-509 ||

varddhayet prokṣaṇīpātraṃ tasmānnyūnaṃ na kārayet |


(mahākālasaṃhitāyām)


ādau mukhyatamaṃ jñeyaṃ ṣoḍaśāṅgulasaṃmitam || 3-510 ||

madhyamaṃ dvādaśamitamadhamaṃ syāddaśāṅgulam |
vasvaṅgulavihīnaṃ tu pātraṃ naiva ca kārayet || 3-511 ||

sarvatra svarṇavat tāmramarghyapātraṃ tato'dhikam |


	tatra sāmānyārghasthāpanavidhiḥ prāguktaḥ | viśeṣārgha-sthāpanaṃ tu pūrvoktaśaṅkhasthāpanavat kāryam | śyāmādividyāyāṃ tu viśeṣavidhiruktaḥ | yathā (triśaktiratne)


kalaśāddakṣiṇe bhāge trikoṇaṃ vṛttasaṃyutam || 3-512 ||

ṣaṭkoṇaṃ caturasraṃ ca kṛtvā tanmadhyabhāgataḥ |
vilasaccandraśakalaṃ gandharvaṃ ca samālikhet || 3-513 ||

ujāpūkādikaṃ pīṭhaṃ taccaturdikṣu pūjayet |
aṅgamantraiśca ṣaṭkoṇe śaktau mūlatribhedataḥ || 3-514 ||

ādhāraśaktiṃ saṃpūjya pūrvavadyantrikāmapi |
mahāśaṅkhaṃ nārikelaṃ sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ tathā || 3-515 ||
p. 201) tāmraṃ śuktyudbhavaṃ vāpi pātraṃ tatra nidhāpayet || 3-515ef ||


	gandharvamiti | īkāramityarthaḥ | ujāpūkādīti | uḍḍiyānajālandhara-pūrṇagirikāmarūpākhyāni pīṭhānītyarthaḥ | śaktau iti | trikoṇe kaulikamatamabhipretya mahāśaṅkhamityuktiḥ |
	pātrabhedāstantrāntare'pyuktāḥ |


pātraṃ kāñcanakācarūpyajanitaṃ muktākapālodbhavaṃ
vaiśvāmitramudaṃ ca kāmadamidaṃ haimaṃ priyaṃ sphāṭikam |
tāmraṃ prītidamiṣṭasiddhijanakaṃ śrīnārikelodbhavaṃ
pāpaghnaṃ sphuṭamantrasiddhijanakaṃ muktipradaṃ mauktikam || 3-516 ||


(triśaktiratne)


samarcya pūrvavanmantrī pātraṃ dravyeṇa pūrayet |

(bhairavatantre)


sakūrcaṃ subhagaṃ pātraṃ vinyasya śuddhapāthasā |
kuṇḍagolodbhavenāpi svayambhūkusumena vā || 3-518 ||

vīraḥ srāvyeṇa cāpūrya mūlavidyāṃ samuccaran |


	śuddhapāthasetyādi | dakṣiṇamārgī śuddhajalena | vāmamārgī cet kuṇḍagolodbhavādinā pāthasāpūryetyarthaḥ | (triśaktiratne)


saṃpūjya pūrvavat tatra vṛttaṃ ṣaṭkoṇamālikhet || 3-519 ||

akathāditrirekhābhyāṃ trikoṇaṃ vṛttamadhyataḥ |

ṣaṭkoṇe ca ṣaṅaṅgāni tatastīrthamayaṃ smaret |

pūjayet pañcaratnāni kuladravyāṇi niḥkṣipet |


p. 202) pañcaratnānīti | gaganaratnādīni paddhatau vakṣyante |
(kumārīkalpe)


arghadravyaṃ cārghapātre niḥkṣipedyatnataḥ sudhīḥ || 3-522 ||

(triśaktiratne)


racayet pūrvavaccheṣamayamarghavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |

atra kuṇḍagolodbhavasvayambhūkusumādikuladravyaniḥkṣepavidhir-vīraparaḥ | (paśukalpe tu)

iti merutantroktadravyāṣṭakasyaiva niḥkṣepo bodhyaḥ |
evaṃ ca vāmena pāṇinetyapi vāmamārgiparam | dakṣiṇamārge tu dakṣiṇahastenaivācchādanaṃ saṃpradāyasiddham |
(bhairavatantre)

(devī paddhatau)

devatādibhedena madhuparkapātrabhedo (merutantre)

p. 203) mahāśaṅkho vāmamārge sphāṭikaṃ grahanāyake |


tatraiva |

dadhidugdhaṃ guḍaḥ kṣaudre ghṛtapratinidhirna hi |

	(śrīnṛsiṃhacaraṇārcanapaddhatau)

	asmin kalpe'pi pādyādipātrāṇi sthāpanīyāni pūjopayuktvāditi sudhārṇavakṛtaḥ | pādyaṃ cācamanīyaṃ ca arghaṃ ca navamaṃ viduriti mantracintāmaṇikṛllikhitapāṭhaḥ prāmādikaḥ | sudhārṇavakalpalatādiṣu bahuṣu grantheṣu etādṛśapāṭhasyānupalambhāt | arghapātrasya navamapātratve śrīpātrasyātiriktatvāpatteśca |
ata eva (kularatnāvalyām)

p. 206) tāvubhāviti | ānandabhairavamānandabhairavīṃ cetyarthaḥ | tayordhyāne tvagre vakṣyete |

	devīpātraṃ śrīpātram | tattvamudrā vakṣyate | oghatrayamiti | divyaughaṃ siddhaughaṃ mānavaughaṃ cetyarthaḥ |
	tatra śyāmāyā divyaughā gurupaṅktya uktā (bhāvacūḍāmaṇau)

p. 207) tārāvidyāyā gurupaṅktayo'pi tatraiva |


atha tārāgurūn vakṣye dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalapradān |

	evaṃ tattadvidyāgurupaṅktayastattatkalpebhyo'vagantayāḥ | eṣāṃ tarpaṇaṃ tu śrīamukānandanāthaśrīpādukāṃ tarpayāmi | evaṃ śrī amukyambāśrīpādukāṃ tarpayāmi ityādi |

	atha tattvaśuddhiryathā | tatra oṃ śrutiḥ prāṇāpānavyānodāna-samānā me śuddhyantāṃ jyotirahaṃ virajā vipāpmā bhūyāsaṃ svāhā | 
oṃ pṛthivyaptejovāyvākāśāni me śuddhyantāṃ jyotirityādi | oṃ prakṛtyahaṃkārabuddhimanaḥśrotrāṇi me śuddhyantāmityādi | oṃ vākcakṣuḥśrotrajihvāghrāṇavacāṃsi me śuddhyantāmityādi | oṃ pāṇipādapāyūpasthaśabdā me śuddhyantāmityādi | oṃ sparśarūparasā-kāśā me śuddhyantāmityādi | oṃ vāyutejaḥsalilabhūmyātmāno me śuddhyantāmityādi | sapta mantrān parāmṛśan hastābhyāṃ saptadhā sarvāṅge spṛśediti tattvaśuddhiḥ |
	vāmācāre tvasminneva samaye vaṭukādibhyo valiṃ prayacchati tadvidhiragre vakṣyate | tantrāntare |

secayet prokṣaṇīpātratoyairmūlāstramuccaran |

hastābhyāmañjaliṃ baddhvānāmikāmūlaparvaṇi || 3-702 ||

aṅguṣṭau niḥkṣipet seyaṃ mudrā tvāvāhanī matā |
adhomukhī tviyaṃ vai syāt sthāpanīti nigadyate || 3-703 ||

ucchritāṅguṣṭhamuṣṭyostu saṃyogāt saṃnidhāpanī |
antaḥpraveśitāṅguṣṭhā saiva saṃrodhinī matā || 3-704 ||

uttānamuṣṭiyugalā sanmukhīkaraṇaṃ bhavet |
savyahastakṛtā muṣṭirdīrghādhomukhatarjanī || 3-705 ||

avaguṇṭhanamudreyamabhito bhrāmitā satī |
devatāṅge ṣaḍaṅgānāṃ nyāsaḥ syāt sakalīkṛtiḥ || 3-706 ||

p. 121) amṛtīkaraṇaṃ dhenumudrayā sā ca saṃmatā |
anyonyābhimukhāśliṣṭakaniṣṭhānāmikā punaḥ || 3-707 ||

tathā tattarjanīmadhyāddhenumudrāmṛtapradā |
anyonyagrathitāṅguṣṭhā prasāritaparāṅguliḥ || 3-708 ||

mahāmudreyamuditāparamīkaraṇe priye |
prayojayedimā mudrā devatāhvānakarmaṇi || 3-709 ||


	atrādyapañcamudrāsu ihāgaccha iha tiṣṭha iha saṃnidhehi, iha saṃnidhehi,  iha saṃmukhībhaveti kramādvadanti |
(uḍḍīśe)


ata evābhipūrṇāyā devatāyāḥ prapūjane |
sādaraṃ saṃmukhībhāvamāvāhanamihocyate || 3-710 ||


ata evāvāhanamudrāpradarśanādeva |


vibhorniveśanaṃ yantre sthāpanaṃ kathitaṃ budhaiḥ |
pūjāṃ prapūjyamānāṃ tu gṛhītvānugrahādikam || 3-711 ||

kartuṃ sāmarthyamasyāstu tat sāṃnidhyaṃ pracakṣate |
ākarmakāṇḍaparyantaṃ sāṃnidhyaṃ hi vibhāvayet || 3-712 ||

sa saṃnirodha uddiṣṭo bhaktito mantravittaraiḥ |
ānandāyatanaṃ tattvaṃ saccidānandalakṣaṇam || 3-713 ||

tadatra sakalaṃ vyāptaṃ dheyaṃ syādavaguṇṭhane |
sakalīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā mantrairebhirathaikatām || 3-714 ||

aṅgānāmaṅginā sārdhaṃ vidadhyādamṛtīkṛtim |
kṣamā tasyāparādhānāṃ vijñeyā paramīkṛtiḥ || 3-715 ||


	aparādhānāmiti | aparicchinnasya brahmaṇa āvāhanādimudrayā paricchinnatābhāvanārūpāparādhānāmityarthaḥ |
	(merau) devasya hṛdayaṃ spṛśannityupakramya |


p. 222) kuryāt prāṇapratiṣṭāṃ ca mama sthāne vadet tathā |
devatānāmaśeṣaṃ tu sarvaṃ prāgvavadihaiva hi || 3-716 ||


	mama sthāna iti | bhūtaśuddhyanantaraṃ ca svadehe kartavyam | yat prāṇapratiṣṭhāpanaṃ tasmādetāvān viśeṣa ityarthaḥ | tatra ca jñānārṇavoktasaṃkṣiptaprakāro'smābhirlikhitaḥ |

	vistaratastu (merutantre)


prāṇapratiṣṭhāmantrasya munayo'jeśapadmajāḥ |
chando ṛgyajuṣaṃ sāma prāṇaśaktistu devatā || 3-717 ||

ṛṣiḥ śirasi vaktre tu chandāṃsi hṛdi devatāḥ |
āṃ vijāya namo guhye padoḥ hrī/ śaktaye namaḥ || 3-718 ||

nābhau namaḥ klī/ kīlāya niyogo'susthitāviti |
oṃ āṃ hrī/ kro/ ma/ kavargaṃ sānusvāraṃ nabhomukham || 3-719 ||

pañcatattvaṃ samuccārya svātmane ā/ hṛdi nyaset |
oṃ ā/ hrī/ kro/ cabījaṃ cavargaṃ tadvacchabdādipañcakam || 3-720 ||

ātmane ī/ samuccārya śirase vahnigehinī |
tāni bījāni u/ tādṛk ṭavargaṃ śrotrakādi ca || 3-721 ||

uktvā tadātmane ū/ ca śikhāyai ca vaṣaḍvadet |
tāni bījāni etaṃ ca vāgādīni tadātmane || 3-722 ||

aimuktvā kavacāyeti hu/ vadet tadanantaram |
caturbījaṃ ca pūrvoktamo/ pavargaṃ ca tādṛśam || 3-723 ||

vacanādi ca tadvat syādau/ netrāya tadantakam |
caturbījaṃ ca a/ tādṛgyavargo mana ādi ca || 3-724 ||

tadātmane svaraścānto'strāya phaṭ ceti saṃvadet |
aṅguṣṭhādau hastatalāntaṃ nyasedevameva hi || 3-725 ||

p. 223) nābhyadhaścāpi kaṇṭhādho mūrdhādho'tha tadantakam |
ā/ hrī/ kro/ kramato nyasya hṛtpadmāṣṭadaleṣu ca || 3-726 ||

pūrvataḥ kramato varṇān karṇikāyāṃ hṛdantakān |
ra/ la/ ha/ ṣa/ va/ ca ma/ ca sa/ śa/ kṣamiti vargataḥ || 3-727 ||
dikṣu nyaset tato dhyāyet prāṇaśaktiṃ hṛdi sthitām || 3-727ef ||

ratnābdhipotāruṇapadmasaṃsthāṃ
pāśāṅkuśāvikṣuśarāśavāṇān |
śūlaṃ kapālaṃ dadhatīṃ karābjai
raktāṃ trinetrāṃ praṇamāmi devīm || 3-728 ||

hastaṃ nidhāya hṛdaye prāgbījaṃ yādikāṣṭakam |
haṃsaḥ so'haṃ mama prāṇā vadediha sthitā- iti || 3-729 ||

uktvā tathaiva ca punarmama jīva iha sthitaḥ |
punastaduktvā mama tu vadet sarvendriyāṇi ca || 3-730 ||

punastaduktvā mama tu vāṅmanaścakṣuruccaret |
śrotraghrāṇaprāṇā ihāgatya sukhaṃ ciraṃ tataḥ || 3-731 ||

tiṣṭhantu vahnijāyāntamuktvā tāraṃ tato japet |
vapurjyotirmayaṃ dhyāyediti syāt prāṇasaṃsthitiḥ || 3-732 ||


mametyādi svīyaprāṇasthāpanābhiprāyeṇa |
(tathā)


tattaddaivatakalpoktāstato mudrāḥ pradarśayet |
tataḥ saṃpūjayeddevaṃśaktyā labhyopacārakaiḥ || 3-733 ||


tantrāntare |


vidhāya devatāmūrtau mūlanyāsamaśeṣataḥ |
āsanādiprasūnāntānupacārān samarpayet || 3-734 ||


upacārāstu (navaratneśvare)


p. 224) gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ tathā dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ naivedyameva ca |
ete pañcopacārāśca kaivalyaphaladāyinaḥ || 3-735 ||


iti pañcopacārāḥ |


pādyamarghyaṃ tathā snānaṃ madhuparkācamanaṃ tathā |
gandhādayo nivedyāntā upacārā daśa kramāt || 3-736 ||


(mahākālasaṃhitāyām)


padyārghācamanīyaṃ snānīyaṃ catvāryanukramāt |
gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ ca dhūpaṃ ca dīpaṃ naivedyameva ca || 3-737 ||

punarācamīyaṃ ca daśaitān saṃpracakṣate |


iti daśopacārāḥ |
(kularatnāvalyām)


pādyārghācamanaṃ snānaṃ punarācamanīyakam || 3-738 ||

gandhākṣataprasūnāni dhūpadīpanivedyakam |
tāmbūlaṃ dvadaśa proktā upacārāḥ prapūjane || 3-739 ||


iti dvadaśopacārāḥ |
(navaratneśvare)


āsanaṃ svāgataṃ pādyamarghyamācamanīyakam |
madhuparkācamanasnānavasanābharaṇāni ca || 3-740 ||

sugandhasumanodhūpadīpanaivedyacandanam |
prayojayedarcanāyāmupacārā/stu ṣoḍaśa || 3-741 ||


iti ṣoḍaśopacārāḥ |
(śāradāyāṃ tu)


pādyārghācamanasnānavasanābharaṇāni ca |
gandhapuṣpe dhūpadīpau naivedyācamanaṃ tathā || 3-742 ||

tāmbūlamarcanā stotraṃ tarpaṇaṃ ca namaskriyā |
prayojayedarcanāyāmupacārā/stu ṣoḍaśa || 3-743 ||


p. 225) ityuktam |
(navaratneśvare)


āsanaṃ svāgataṃ pādyamarghyamācamanīyakam |
snānaṃ vāsopavītaṃ ca bhūṣaṇāni ca sarvaśaḥ || 3-744 ||

gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ tathā dhūpaṃ dīpamannaṃ ca tarpaṇam |
mālyānulepanaṃ caiva namaskāraṃ visarjanam || 3-745 ||

aṣṭādaśopacāraistu mantrī pūjāṃ samācaret |


ityaṣṭādaśopacārāḥ |
(sundarīrahasyavṛttau)


āsanābhyañjane tadvadudvartananirīkṣaṇe || 3-746 ||

sanmānaṃ vai sarpirādeḥ snapanāvāhane tataḥ |
pādyārghācamanīye ca snānīyamadhuparkakau || 3-747 ||

punarācamanīyaṃ ca vastraṃ yajñopavītakam |
alaṃkārā gandhapuṣpe dhūpadīpau tathaiva ca || 3-748 ||

tāmbulāni ca naivedyaṃ puṣpamālā tathaiva ca |
anulepanaśayye ca cāmaraṃ vyajanaṃ tathā || 3-749 ||

ādarśadarśanaṃ caiva namaskāro'pyanantaram |
gītavādyavitānāni stutirhomaḥ pradakṣiṇam || 3-750 ||

dantakāṣṭhapradānaṃ ca tato devavisarjanam |
upacārā ime jñeyāḥ ṣaḍtriṃśat surapūjane || 3-751 ||


iti ṣaṭtriṃśadupacārāḥ |
evaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭyupacārādayastantrāntare draṣṭavyāḥ |
(kuloḍḍīśe)


upacāreṣu sarveṣu yat kiṃ ciddurlabhaṃ bhavet |
tat sarvaṃ manasā dhyātvā puṣpakṣepeṇa kalpayet || 3-752 ||

tatra tatra jalaṃ dadyādupacārāntarāntarā |


p. 226) upacārasamarpaṇavidhiruktaḥ (sārasaṃgrahe)


devasya vāmabhāge tu dadyānmūlena cāsanam || 3-753 ||

pauṣpaṃ dārumayaṃ vāstraṃ cārmaṃ kauśeyataijasam |
ṣaḍvidhaṃ cāsanaṃ proktaṃ devatāprītikārakam || 3-754 ||


	pauṣpaṃ sugandhipuṣparacitam | dārumayaṃ kaṇṭakibhinnaṃ candanādighaṭitam | vāstraṃ kārpāsam | kauśeyaṃ kambalādi | kauśayaṃ saptaviṃśatikuśapatrāṇāṃ viṣṭaraḥ |
taduktam (ḍāmare)


saptaviṃśatidarbhāṇāṃ veṇyagre granthibhūṣitā |
viṣṭaraḥ sarvayajñeṣu lakṣaṇaṃ parikīrtitam- iti || 3-755 ||


	taijasaṃ suvarṇādiracitaṃ lohasīsādivarjyam | cārmaṃ mṛgavyāghrādyajinam |
(jñānamālāyām)


upacāravidhiṃ vakṣye datvā cātha śubhāsanam |
svāgataṃ kuśalapraśnaṃ nigadedagratastataḥ || 3-756 ||

(merutantre)

sāṅgaṃ sāvaraṇaṃ devaṃ pūjārthamiha cāgatam |
dhyātvā iti tu mūlānte svāgataṃ parikalpayet || 3-757 ||


jñānamālāyām |


pādyaṃ pādāmbuje dadyāddevasya hṛdayāṇunā |


	hṛdayāṇunā nama iti mantreṇetyarthaḥ |
(yāmale)


hastābjayostato dadyādarghyaṃ toyaiḥ suvāsitaiḥ || 3-758 ||


(jñānamālāyāṃ tu)


arghyaṃ diśet tato mūrdhni śiromantreṇa deśikaḥ- ityuktam |


śiromantreṇeti svāhetyarthaḥ |
mahākālenāpi |


arghyaṃ mūrdhni pradātavyaṃ svāhāmantreṇa sādhakaiḥ- ityuktam || 3-759 ||


p. 227) (kramadīpikāyāṃ)
arghadānānantaraṃ pādyadānamuktam |

datvāsanaṃ svāgatamityudīrya
tathārghapādyācamanīyakāni- iti ||

(siddhāntasāre)


pādyaṃ hṛnmanunā dadyādarghyaṃ jvalanajāyayā |
svadhayācamanīyaṃ ca tenaiva madhuparkakam || 3-760 ||


(śāradāyām)


svadhāmantreṇa vadane dadyādācamanīyakam |
jātīlavaṅgakaṅkolaistaduktaṃ mantravedibhiḥ || 3-761 ||


tathā |


svadhāṇunā tataḥ kuryānmadhuparkaṃ mukhāmbuje |
ājyaṃ dadhi madhūnmiśramayamukto manīṣibhiḥ || 3-762 ||


(kuloḍḍīśe) tu | kṣīraśarkarayorapi madhuparkāṅgatvamuktam |


mākṣikaṃ śarkarā sarpirdadhi kṣīrayutaṃ tathā |
madhuparkaṃ kalpayitvā pradadyādvidhipūrvakam || 3-763 ||

sarvataḥ kṣaudramadhikaṃ dadhisarpiḥsitāḥ samam |
kṣīraṃ tuḥ sarvataḥ svalpaṃ madhuparka udāhṛtaḥ || 3-764 ||


(śāradāyām)


tenaiva manunā kuryādadbhirācamanīyakam |


tenaiva manuneti svāhāmantreṇaivetyarthaḥ |
atra sudhārṇabakṛtaḥ |


vāruṇena ca bījena dadyādācamanīyakam || 3-765 ||

madhuparkaṃ mukhe dadyājjalamantreṇa deśikaḥ |


	iti yāmalasvatantravacanasaṅgatyanurodhena svadhāmantreṇānyatra sudhāmantreṇeti pāṭhamāśritya sudhāśabdasyāmṛtavācakatvāt tadvījena vinduyuktavakāreṇa madhuparkamācamanīyaṃ ca dadyādityāhuḥ |

p. 228) tantrasārakṛtastu tyāgārthakasvāhāśabdenārghyadānavidhānāt tatsamabhivyāhṛtācamanīyadāne tyāgabodhakaḥ svadhāśabdo gṛhyate na tu varuṇabījaṃ tasya tyāgārthakasvābhāvāt |
	tathā ca (kulasāre)


ādau mantraṃ samuccārya paścādevaṃ samarcayet || 3-766 ||

saṃmpradānaṃ tadante tu tyāgārthakapadaṃ tataḥ |
evaṃ krameṇa deveśi upacārān prakalpayet || 3-767 ||


	yat tu |


madhuparkaṃ tataḥ kuryājjalamantreṇa deśikaḥ |
svadhāṇunā tataḥ kuryānmadhuparkaṃ mukhāmbuje || 3-768 ||

tenaiva manunā kuryādadbhirācamanīyakam |
vāruṇena ca bījena dadyādācamanīyakam- iti || 3-769 ||


	vacanadvayaṃ, tadvarṇabhedena vyavasthāpanīyam | śūdrāṇāṃ svadhetyuccāraṇe'nadhikārāt | itthaṃ ca namaḥ svāhā svadhā vauṣaḍityādīnāṃ tyāgārthakatayā taireva mantrairupacāradānaṃ tadvyatiriktamantreṇopacārasamaparṇaṃ tu pramāṇśūnyamiti vyājahruḥ |

	ke cit svadhāmantreṇa madhuparkācamanīyadānasya sakalatantrasaṃmatatvāt jalamantreṇetyādau tṛtīyāyāḥ sāhityamarthastena ca jalabījasahitena svadhāmantreṇa dadyāditi vadanti | anye tvācamanīyasādhane jalamantrastaddāne tu svadheti saṃgamayanti |
	nibandhe |


snānaṃ vāso'laṅkaraṇaṃ gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ hṛdārpayet |


hṛdā namaḥśabdena |
(kālīkalpe tu)


snānaṃ gandhaṃ hṛdā dadyāt puṣpāṇi vauṣaḍityapi- ityuktam || 3-770 ||

tato nivedayāmīti sarvaṃ dadyānmaheśvari |


sarvamiti | vastrālaṅkāradhūpadīpādītyarthaḥ | naivedyadāne tu viśeṣa uktaḥ |
(siddhāntasāre)


nivedayāmi hṛdvahnijāyābhistattvamudrayā || 3-771 ||

devatāyai maheśāni dadyānnaivedyamuttamam- iti |


p. 229) (merutantre)


sugandhitailairabhyañjya sugandhāmalakādibhiḥ || 3-772 ||

udvarttanaṃ vidhāyātha snāpayeduṣṇavāriṇā |
gandhodakaiśca mūlena yathāśaktyābhiṣecayet || 3-773 ||

kṣīradadhyājyamadhubhiḥ khaṇḍena ca pṛthak pṛthak |


(śāradāyām)


gandhādbhiḥ kārayet snānaṃ vāsasī paridhāpayet || 3-774 ||


vastrāṇyuktāni (merutantre)


viṣṇave vagalāmukhyai pītakauśeyamambaram |
śivāya ca sarasvatyai pārśvanāthāya śubhrakam || 3-775 ||

raktaṃ śaktyarkavighnānāṃ bhaumasaugatayorapi |
kṛṣṇaṃ bhairavakālyāde rādhādernīlamambaram || 3-776 ||

acchidraṃ malahīnaṃ ca sadaśaṃ yanna dhāritam |
kārpāsajaṃ vā kauśeyaṃ rāṅkavaṃ vā viśiṣyate || 3-777 ||

tailādidūṣitādrogaḥ sacchidrādvādhyatā bhavet |

dagdhādvipadbhavedāsyadatte gātrāvalambite |
