tatraiva |


yajñopavītaṃ dattvātha bhūṣaṇāni samarpayet |
ṅentaṃ namontaṃ tannām pūjanādau prayojayet || 3-781 ||

tāni nānāvidhāni syurmukuṭādiprabhedataḥ |
strīpuṃprabhedataścāpi vijñeyāni vicakṣaṇaiḥ || 3-782 ||


p. 230) gandhaḥ pañcavidha uktaḥ (siddhāntasaṃgrahe)


saṃgharṣajaḥ parimalaḥ kṣodajaḥ prāṇisaṃbhavaḥ |
kṛṣṇasārābhidhānaśca gandhaḥ pañcavidho mataḥ || 3-783 ||



	saṃgharṣajo ghṛṣṭavandanādiḥ | parimalaḥ sugandhidravyavimarda-janyaḥ | kṣodajo gorocanādicūrṇotpannaḥ | prāṇisaṃbhavaḥ kastūrikādiḥ | kṛṣṇasārastu śrīkhaṇḍādisurabhidravyādyakṛṣṭaḥ | sārabhāgo bhāṣayā atara iti prasiddhaḥ |
	tatraiva |


yākṣaṃ daivaṃ ca gāndharvaṃ kardamaṃ tritayaṃ param |
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ sādhanaṃ devatārpitam || 3-784 ||


kardamatrayaṃ tatraivokam |


kuraṅgamadakakkolakālāgurusitābhrakaiḥ |
pramodakṛddevatānāṃ vijñeyo yakṣakardamaḥ || 3-785 ||

sa eva rocanāyukto devakardama ucyate |
paṭīrayugdevapaṅko jñeyo gandharvakardamaḥ || 3-786 ||


kuraṅgamadaḥ kastūrī | sitābhraṃ karpūraṃ | paṭīraṃ candanam |
(puraścaraṇacandrikāyām)


candanaṃ malayotpannamanāghrātaṃ suśītalam |
karpūrāgurukastūrīhimāmbukṣoditaṃ śubham || 3-787 ||


(merutantre)


kastūrikā caikabhāgo dvau bhāgau mustakasya ca |
tadardhailā jaṭāmāṃsī tvakkuṣṭhaṃ ca śilārasaḥ || 3-788 ||

caturbhāgaṃ vālakaṃ ca ghṛtabhṛṣṭā tathā nakhī |
sugandhi kokilā lohavāṇo rājavacā tathā || 3-789 ||

aṣṭau bhāgāḥ keśarasya murāyāścātha ṣoḍaśa |
kālāguruścākasīraṃ kālīyamatha vārddhayaḥ || 3-790 ||

bhāgāḥ sevantikārkasya candanena vimardayet |
p. 231) kāmodanāmā saṃmardo devatārājatoṣakṛt || 3-791 ||


puṣpabhedāstatraivoktāḥ |


puṣpaṃ pañcavidhaṃ proktaṃ paraṃ cāparameva ca |
uttamaṃ madhyamaṃ hīnaṃ teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam || 3-792 ||

paraṃ suvarṇapuṣpaṃ syāt tat sadā yogyameva hi |
abhyaṅgapūrvakaṃ deyamarcanārhaṃ punarbhavet || 3-793 ||

aparaṃ rājataṃ puṣpaṃ tāmrajaṃ ca prakīrtitam |
sadā yogyaṃ cedamapi trivarṣaṃ caikavarṣakam || 3-794 ||

tulasī ketakī padma karavīraṃ ca mālatī |
pañcaitānyuttamānyāhurmadhyamāni tathā daśa || 3-795 ||

mallikākundamandāratagarārjunakiṃśukāḥ |
parijātamaśokaṃ ca vilvaṃ campakameva ca || 3-796 ||

etadbhinnaṃ suvarṇaṃ ca sugandhaṃ hīnamucyate |
tattanmantravidhāneṣu vihitāni samarpayet || 3-797 ||


atha niṣiddhāni tatraiva |


nārpayedbhūmipatitaṃ korakaṃ kṛmibhakṣitam |
aṅgalagnaṃ samāghrātaṃ mlānaṃ paryuṣitaṃ tathā || 3-798 ||

ugragandhamagandhaṃ ca kṛmikeśādidūṣitam |
aśuddhapātraprāṇyaṅgavāsobhiḥ kutsitātmabhiḥ || 3-799 ||

anītaṃ tanniṣiddhaṃ syānmadhyāhnasnānatastathā |
adhomukhārpitaṃ neṣṭaṃ puṣpāñjalividhau na tat || 3-800 ||

na paryuṣitadoṣo'sti tulasīvilvacampake |
jalaje vakule'gastye mālākāragṛheṣu ca || 3-801 ||

ketakīkuṭajairneśaṃ na varvaryākṣatairharim |
p. 232) na dūrvayā yajeddurgāṃ vilvapatrairdivākaram || 3-802 ||

lambodaraṃ tulasyā ca vṛntahīnaiḥ samarcayet |


devatābhedena puṣpabhedā uktāḥ (pādme)


dūrvārambhāvilvapatraistulasīvarjitaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 3-803 ||

jalajaiḥ sthalajaiḥ puṣpaiḥ śvetaraktairgaṇādhipam |
mālatīkundamandārakumudaiḥ śāradāṃ yajet || 3-804 ||

karavīraṃ javāpuṣpaṃ padmaṃ kokanadaṃ tathā |
nīlotpalamaśokaṃ ca bandhūkaṃ dāḍimaṃ tathā || 3-805 ||

ketakaṃ mallikārkaṃ trisandhyaṃ ca raveḥ priyam |
kanakaṃ kumudaṃ kundaṃ tagaraṃ kaṇṭakārikā || 3-806 ||

girikarṇī vilvapatraṃ niṣiddhaṃ ravipūjane |
hayārikusumaṃ padmaṃ kairavaṃ nāgakeśaram || 3-807 ||

jātīsevantikādroṇapuṣpaṃ nīlotpalaṃ tathā |
arkapuṣpamapāmārgamunmattaṃ kuṭajaṃ tathā || 3-808 ||

śamīpuṣpaṃ vilvapatraṃ harasyātīva vallabham |
ketakaṃ ca japāpuṣpaṃ śirīṣaṃ damanaṃ tathā || 3-809 ||

vibhītakaṃ cātimuktaṃ sarjabandhūkayūthikāḥ |
viṣṇukrāntāmapi śvetāṃ varjayecchivapūjane || 3-810 ||

mālatī mallikā yūthī campakaṃ nāgakeśaram |
pāṭalā kundamandāraṃ kahlāramatimuktakam || 3-811 ||

karṇikāraṃ kuruvakaṃ kadambaṃ karavīrakam |
tamālaṃ vakulaṃ nīlotpalaṃ kamalakairave || 3-812 ||

sevantikā ketakaṃ ca damanaṃ munipuṣpakam |
dhātrīpuṣpaṃ śamīpatramapāmārgasya patrakam || 3-813 ||

p. 233) mṛdūnāṃ caiva durvāṇāmaṅkurāḥ śatapatrikā |
mañjarī sahakārasya tulasī ca hareḥ priyā || 3-814 ||

unmattamarkapuṣpaṃ ca nirgandhamatilohitam |
yadapyutkaṭagandhaṃ tadvarjayedviṣṇupūjane || 3-815 ||

raktapuṣpaṃ vilvapatraṃ nṛsiṃhāya nivedayet |
varjayet ketakaṃ puṣpamanyat sarvaṃ ca pūrvavat || 3-816 ||


(kālītantre)


nānopahārabalibhirnānāpuṣpairmanoharaiḥ |
apāmārgadalairbhṛṅgaistulasīvarjitaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 3-817 ||

pūjanīyā sadā bhaktyā nṛṇāṃ śīghraphalāptaye |


(muṇḍamālāyām)


puṣpāṇyapi tathā dadyādraktakṛṣṇasitāni ca || 3-818 ||

śvetapuṣpaṃ javāpuṣpaṃ karavīraṃ tathā priye |
tagaraṃ mālatī jātī sevantī yūthikā tathā || 3-819 ||

dhattūrāśokavakulaśvetakṛṣṇāparājitāḥ |
vakapuṣpaṃ tathā vilvaṃ campakaṃ nāgakeśaram || 3-820 ||

mallikā jhiṇṭikā kāñcī raktaṃ yat parikīrtitam |
arkapuṣpaṃ varvaraṃ ca bandhūkaṃ ca priyamvadā || 3-821 ||

aṣṭamyāṃ ca viśeṣeṇa tuṣṭā bhavati pārvatī |
padmapuṣpeṇa rektena satuṣṭāḥ sarvadevatāḥ || 3-822 ||

kṛṣṇairvā yadi vā śuklaiḥ kālikā varadā bhavet |


(matsyasūkte)


sugandhi śvetalauhityakusumairarcayet kulaiḥ || 3-823 ||

vilvairmaruvakādyaiśca tulasīvarjitaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
p. 234) kṣaudrapuṣpairviśeṣeṇa vajrapuṣpeṇa miśritam || 3-824 ||

sarvaṃ puṣpaṃ pradadyācca bhaktiyuktena cetasā |
pūjanīyā sadā bhaktyā nṛṇāṃ śīghraphalapradā || 3-825 ||


kulairvajrapuṣpādyaiḥ |
(kālikāpurāṇe)


puṣpaṃ kokanadaṃ padma japā bandhakameva ca |
sarveṣāṃ puṣpajātīnāṃ raktapuṣpaṃ viśiṣyate || 3-826 ||


(yāmale)


vajrapuṣpaṃ kālikāyai pradadyāt siddhihetave |
yonipuṣpaṃ maheśāya vajrapuṣpaṃ nigadyate || 3-827 ||


vajrapuṣpasya traividhyamuktam (muṇḍamālāyām)


āṣoḍaśādanūḍhāyā uttamaṃ sarvasiddhidam |
balātkāreṇa ruḍhāyā madhyamaṃ bhogavarddhanam || 3-828 ||

rajoyogavaśādanyadadhamaṃ sukhadāyakam |
svayambhūkusumaṃ devi trividhaṃ bhuvi jāyate || 3-829 ||


anūḍhāyā anupabhuktāyāḥ | ruḍhāyā upabhuktāyāḥ |
tantrāntare |


vajrapuṣpeṇārpitena yathā tuṣyati kālikā |
na svarṇairna ca ratnaiśca na sparśamaṇibhistathā || 3-830 ||

abhāve vajrapuṣpasya javāpuṣpena pūjayet |
arkapuṣpeṇa vā pūjyā kālikā sarvamaṅgalā || 3-831 ||


	kāliketi dakṣiṇāmnāyādhiṣṭhātrī | uttarāmnāye tvarkapuṣpaniṣedha ukto
(mahākālasaṃhitāyām)


tulasībhirapāmārgairdhattūraiḥ sindhuvārakaiḥ |
arkapuṣpairvāsakaiśca naiva devīṃ prapūjayet- iti || 3-832 ||


vihitāni tatraivoktāni |


śirīṣaiḥ karṇikāraiśca campakaiḥ kovidārakaiḥ |
p. 235) vakulaiścaiva mandāraiḥ kundapuṣpaiḥ kuraṇṭakaiḥ || 3-833 ||

latābhirbrahmavṛkṣasya mṛdudūrvāṅkurairapi |
kāñcanārairaśokaiśca punnāgaiḥ ketakīdalaiḥ || 3-834 ||

sephālikābhiryūthībhirjātībhirdamanairapi |
śatapatrairmallikābhiramlānairbandhujīvakaiḥ || 3-835 ||

jhiṇṭībhiśca japāpuṣpaiḥ karavīraiśca kiṃśukaiḥ |
parijātaiḥ pāṭalaiśca padmairnīlotpalairapi || 3-836 ||

mādhavībhirmaruvakairaparājitayāpi ca |
aśanaiśca kadambaiśca droṇapuṣpaiḥ sakeśaraiḥ || 3-837 ||

arcayet kusumairebhirdevīṃ sādhakasattamaḥ |
vilvapatraṃ yathā prītikaraṃ devyā varānane || 3-838 ||

na tathānyat kiñcidasti puṣpeṣu prītikārakam |
ato yatnena dātavyaṃ vilvapatraṃ tripatrayuk || 3-839 ||


tathā |


nānāpuṣpairviracitā dātavyā puṣpamālikā |


mālābhedāstu tatraivoktāḥ |


ekajātīyakaiḥ puṣpairbhinnajātīyakairapi || 3-840 ||

mālā tathaikavarṇā syādbhinnavarṇāpi vā priye |
sā punastrividhā jñeyā pariṇāhavaśena tu || 3-841 ||

pateddhṛdayaparyantaṃ yā mālāmodaśālinī |
vaikakṣikā sā vijñeyā sarvāvaratayā sthitā || 3-842 ||

adho'valambinī nābheḥ kausumī yā sragucyate |
sādhoriṇī parijñeyā madhyamā pūrvato'dhikā || 3-843 ||

āgulphasraṃsinī yā tu pādapadmopari sthitā |
p. 236) vanamāleti sā khyātā sarvābhyaḥ sragbhya uttamā || 3-844 ||


atha dvitīyavidyāyāḥ | (nīlatantre)


aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ pūjayecca yathāvidhi |
ājñāsiddhimavāpnoti javāpuṣpaṃ ca varvarī || 3-845 ||

candanaṃ cārkakusumaṃ dadyācchvetāparājitām |
bandhūkamālayā tuṣyet tathā chāgapradānavat || 3-846 ||


(triśaktiratne)


vajrapuṣpeṇa saṃmiśrairarcayet siddhihetave |
abhāvādvajrapuṣpasya smaraṇāt siddhimāpnuyāt || 3-847 ||

tulasīmālatīvarjaṃ puṣpaṃ dadyāt prasannadhīḥ |


(matsyasūkte)


puṣpaṃ śraṣṭhaṃ raktakokaṃ bandhūkaṃ śatapatrakam || 3-848 ||

varvarīdvitayaṃ caiva karṇikāradvayaṃ tathā |
vakamandāracūtāni karavīrāṇi śasyate || 3-849 ||


(kulacūḍāmaṇau)


varjayenmālatīpuṣpaṃ varjayet tulasīdalam |


tantrāntare |


vajrapuṣpeṇa sarvatra pūjayeccanisundarīm || 3-850 ||


upacārāntarasamarpaṇe'pi vajrapuṣpaṃ pratīccha svāheti vaktavyamityarthaḥ |
(śrīvidyāpaddhatau)


jalajānīha yāvanti śvetakṛṣṇāruṇāni ca |
japābandhūkamukhyāni raktāni sthalajānyapi || 3-851 ||

śvetānyapi sugandhīni mallikāvarjitāni ca |
pītānyapi tathā vilvapatrāṇi ca samarpayet || 3-852 ||

nirgandhaṃ kṛṣṇapuṣpaṃ ca tulasīṃ ca parityajet |
tulasīgandhamātreṇa kruddhā bhavati sundarī || 3-853 ||

p. 237) nārcayet tripurāmambāṃ palāśakusumairapi |


kāmye tu mallikā na niṣiddhā |
taduktam (jñānārṇave)


mallikāmālatījātīkundaiśca śatapatrakaiḥ || 3-854 ||

śvetotpalasuśītaiśca pūjayenmāsamātrakam- iti |


(kaulālavīye)


sundarībhairavīkālībrahmaviṣṇuvivasvatām || 3-855 ||

tulasīvarjitā pūjā sā pūjā saphalā bhavet |


	viphaleti kvacit pāṭhastatrāpyakārapraśleṣāt saphaletyarthaḥ paryavasyati | brahmaviṣṇuvivasvatāmiti puṣpacintāmaṇikṛllikhitapāṭhaḥ prāmādikaḥ | viṣṇupūjāyāṃ tulasīniṣedhasya svapne'pyaśrutatvāt |
	(nirbandhe)


raktapuṣpairviśeṣeṇa santuṣṭā bhuvaneśvarī || 3-856 ||

tathaiva cchinnamastāpi pītaistu vagalāmukhī |
guñjayā tu viśeṣeṇa mātaṅgī varadā bhavet || 3-857 ||


(muṇḍamālāyām)


śmaśānadhattureṇaiva tuṣṭā dhūmāvatī parā |



evaśabdaḥ prāśastyaparo na tvanyayogavyavacchedakaḥ |



śrīvidyoditapuṣpaistu sarvā vidyāḥ prapūjayet || 3-858 ||


iti tantrāntaravacanāt |
(merutantre)


kundamandārakumudamālatīpadmaketakam |
nandyāvarttāhvayaṃ jātī kahlāraṃ campakaṃ tathā || 3-859 ||

raktotpalādipuṣpāṇi lakṣmyāścātipriyāṇi hi |


durgāyāstu (bhaviṣyapurāṇe)


mallikāmutpalaṃ padmaṃ śamīpunnāgacampakam || 3-860 ||

aśokaṃ karṇikāraśca droṇapuṣpaṃ viśeṣataḥ |
karavīraṃ japāpuṣpaṃ kuṅkumaṃ nāgakeśaram || 3-861 ||

p. 238) yaḥ prayacchati puṇyārthaṃ puṣpāṇyetāni bhārata |
caṇḍikāyai naraśreṣṭa śraddhābhaktisamanvitaḥ || 3-862 ||

sa kāmānakhilān prāpya śrīdurgānucaro bhavet |


tathā |


punnāgacampakaḥ kundo yūthikā navamallikā || 3-863 ||

tagarārjunamallī ca bṛhatī śatapatrikā |
tathā kumudakahlāravilvapāṭalamālatī || 3-864 ||

javāvicakilāśokaraktanīlotpalāni ca |
damano marupatraṃ ca śatapuṣpaṃ vivṛddhaye || 3-865 ||


tathā |


sugandhigandhapuṣpaistu pūjayedyastu caṇḍikām |
muktibhirmālayā vāpi so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 3-866 ||


tathā |


nīlotpalasahasreṇa yo mālāṃ saṃprayacchati |
caṇḍikāyai naraśreṣṭha tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu || 3-867 ||

varṣakoṭisahasrāṇi varṣakoṭiśatāni ca |
śrīdurgānucaro bhūtvā rudraloke sa modate || 3-868 ||


grahāṇāmapi tatraiva |


hayārikusumaiḥ sūryaṃ kadambaiścandramarcayet |
kṣitijaṃ tu japāpuṣpaiścampakena tu somajam || 3-869 ||

śatapatrairguruḥ pūjyo jātīpuṣpaistu bhārgavaḥ |
mallikākusumaiḥ paṅguḥ kundapuṣpairvidhuntudaḥ || 3-870 ||

ketuśca vividhaiḥ puṣpaiḥ śāntikāleṣu sarvadā |


(yāmale)


puṣpairdevāḥ prasīdanti puṣpedevāśca saṃsthitāḥ || 3-871 ||

p. 239) kiṃ cātibahunoktena puṣpasyoktirmatallikā |
paraṃ jyotiḥ puṣpagataṃ puṣpeṇaiva prasīdati || 3-872 ||

trivargasādhanaṃ puṣpaṃ puṣṭiśrīsvargamokṣadam |
puṣpamūle vasedbrahmā puṣpamadhye tu keśavaḥ || 3-873 ||

puṣpāgre tu mahādevaḥ sarve devāḥ sthitā dale |
tasmāt puṣpairyajeddevaṃ nityaṃ bhaktiyuto naraḥ || 3-874 ||


(kriyāsāre)


sarvaiḥ puṣpaiḥ sadā pūjyā vihitāvihitairapi |
kartavyā sarvadevānāṃ bhaktiyogo'tra kāraṇam || 3-875 ||

devīpūjā sadā śastā jalajaiḥ sthalajairapi |
vihitairvā niṣiddhairvā bhaktiyuktena cetasā || 3-876 ||


(merutantre)


puṣpābhāve pravālairvā tadabhāve ca korakaiḥ |
tadabhāve phalaiḥ patraistadabhāve tṛṇāṅkuraiḥ || 3-877 ||


tatrāntare |


puṣpaṃ vā yadi vā patraṃ yathotpannaṃ tathārpaṇam |
vilvasya khadirasyāpi tathā dhātrīphalasya ca || 3-878 ||

tamālasya ca padmasya chinnabhinne na dūṣyataḥ |


(mālinītantre)


pūjayet parameśāni puṣpābhāve tu patrakaiḥ || 3-879 ||

patrāṇāmapyalābhe tu drumagulmodbhavādibhiḥ |
tadbhuvāmapyalābhe tu tatphalena prapūjayet || 3-880 ||

akṣatairvā jalairvāpi tadabhāve tu sarṣapaiḥ |
sitaisteṣāmapyalābhe mānasaṃ pūjanaṃ caret || 3-881 ||


p. 240) (merutantre)


atha puṣpopacārānte kuryādāvaraṇārcanam |


	idaṃ ca gauḍādisaṃpradāye | anyatra tu dhūpadīpanaivedyādyantaṃ pūjanaṃ bodhyam |
(siddhāntasāre)


prathamaṃ mantramuccārya śrīpūrvaṃ pādukāpadam || 3-882 ||

dvitīyāntaṃ samuccārya pūjayāmīti pūjayet |


(somabhujaṅgāvalyām)


tataśca mūlamantreṇa trivāraṃ pūjayet sudhīḥ || 3-883 ||

gṛhītvājñāṃ mahādevyāḥ parivārān samarcayet |
ṣaḍaṅgaiḥ prathamaḥ kalpo gurubhistu dvitīyakaḥ || 3-884 ||

parivārastṛtīyaḥ syāllokapālaiścaturthakaḥ |
tadastraiḥ pañcamaḥ prokta iti sāmānyataḥ kramaḥ || 3-885 ||


(yāmale)


pādyādinā tathā paścāt pūjayedaṅgadevatāḥ |


(merutantre)


mantrasaṃpuṭitairvarṇānrmātṛkāyāḥ savindukaiḥ || 3-886 ||

krameṇa gandhapuṣpādyairdevasyāṅgaṃ samarcayet |


tathā |


keśareṣvagnikoṇādau hṛdayādīni pūjayet || 3-887 ||

netramagnerdiśāsvastraṃ dhyātavyā aṅgadevatāḥ |
tuṣārasphaṭikaśyāmanīlakṛṣṇāruṇārciṣaḥ || 3-888 ||

varadābhayadhāriṇyaḥ pradhānatanavaḥ striyaḥ |
agnīśāsuravāyavyamadhyadiṣu kramāt sthitāḥ || 3-889 ||


(tūrṇāyāge)


abhīṣṭasiddhiṃ me dehi śaraṇāgatavatsala |
bhaktyā samarpaye tubhyaṃ prathamāvaraṇārcanam || 3-890 ||

p. 241) iti puṣpāñjaliṃ dadyāt praṇamedatibhaktitaḥ |
pratyāvaraṇamevaṃ tu tattannāmnā samarpayet || 3-891 ||


(merutantre)


devasya purataḥ prācī jñeyāvaraṇapūjane |
tadādiparivārāṇāṃ prādakṣiṇyena pūjanam || 3-892 ||


(mānasollāse)


pūjyapūjakayormadhye prācī saṃkīrttyate budhaiḥ |
taddakṣiṇaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ syāduttaraṃ cottaraṃ bhavet || 3-893 ||


(jñānārṇave)


uttarābhimukho devi yadā cakraṃ samuddharet |
uttarāśā tadā devi pūrvāśaiva na saṃśayaḥ || 3-894 ||

paścimābhimukho mantrī yadā cakraṃ samuddharet |
paścimāśā tadā jñeyā pūrvāśaiva na saṃśayaḥ || 3-895 ||

voyukoṇaṃ tadāgneyamīśānaṃ rākṣasaṃ bhavet |
dakṣiṇāśāmukho mantrī yadā cakraṃ samuddharet || 3-896 ||
pūrvāśaiva tadā rakṣo rakṣaḥkoṇaṃ tu vahnimat || 3-896ef ||


	paścimāśādyabhimukhatvaṃ ca kāmyapūjāviśeṣaparam | nityādipūjāyāṃ tu prāgānano dhanadadigvadano'thavetyādinā digdvayasyaiva niyamāt |
	(śyāmārahasye) tantrāntare ca |


hotuḥ pūrvaṃ pūrvabhāgaṃ pradiṣṭaṃ savyaṃ bhāgaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ tvāgamajñaiḥ |
dakṣaṃ vidyāduttaraṃ bhāgamagryaṃ prajñāvadbhiḥ paścimaṃ pīṭhamuktam || 3-897 ||


	pūrvamiti purataḥ | agryaṃ svasaṃmukhaṃ tena devapṛṣṭhameva pratīcītyarthaḥ | yat tu indrādīn sāhajikaprācyādikrameṇa pūjayanti ke cit tat pramāṇaśūnyam |
	(śāradāyām)


paścādabhyarcanīyāḥ syuḥ kalpoktāvṛttayaḥ kramāt |
ante yajellokapālānmūlapāriṣadānvitān || 3-898 ||

p. 242) hetijātyadhipopetān dikṣu pūrvāditaḥ kramāt |


indrādilokapālānāṃ dhyānāni (merutantre)


indraṃ surādhipaṃ pītamairāvatagataṃ vibhum || 3-899 ||

sahasranetraṃ dvibhujaṃ pītavajradharaṃ bhaje |
agniṃ tejo'dhipaṃ raktaṃ śvetaśaktikaraṃ prabhum || 3-900 ||

subhūṣaṇaṃ meṣavāhaṃ vande raktāṅgalocanam |
yamaṃ pretādhipaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ mahiṣopari saṃsthitam || 3-901 ||

kāladaṇḍadharaṃ krūraṃ vṛtaṃ dūtairbhayānakaiḥ |
rakṣo'dhipaṃ ca niṛtiṃ śavāsanasamāśritam || 3-902 ||

dhūmrābhaṃ khaḍgahastaṃ ca piṅgalaśmaśrukūrcakam |
makare saṃsthitaṃ vidyunnibhayā sakarāmbujam || 3-903 ||

śubhrāṅgaṃ tundilaṃ vande varuṇaṃ yādasāṃ patim |
vāyuṃ prāṇādhipaṃ dhūmraṃ raktāṅkuśakaraṃ param || 3-904 ||

vātapramīsamāruḍhaṃ dhāvantaṃ vegato bhaje |
yakṣanāthaṃ kuberaṃ ca śuklaṃ śuklagadādharam || 3-905 ||

narasiṃhāsanārūḍhaṃ medulaṃ citriṇīyutam |
vidyādhipaṃ nīlaśūladharaṃ vṛṣabhavāhanam || 3-906 ||

trinetraṃ bhālacandraṃ ca jaṭājūṭadharaṃ sitam |
lokādhipaṃ vidhātāraṃ raktapadmakaraṃ śubham || 3-907 ||

haṃsārūḍhaṃ vedapāṭhaṃ kurvantaṃ tamahaṃ bhaje |
gauraṃ nāgādhipaṃ mūrdhni phaṇāmaṇḍalabhūṣitam || 3-908 ||

pīnottuṅgaṃ cakradharamanantaṃ viśvagaṃ bhaje |


(śāradāyām)

gaṇeśakṣetrapālābhyāṃ pūrvavadvalimutsṛjet |

dakṣiṇe vaṭukaṃ dadyāduttare yoginīgaṇe || 3-1022 ||

pūrve bhūtavaliṃ dadyāt kṣetrapālāya paścime |

tathā |

aṅguṣṭhanāmikābhyāṃ tu vaṭukāya valirmataḥ || 3-1023 ||

kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhayogena yoginīvalimāharet |
aṅguṣṭhatarjanībhyāṃ tu kṣetrapālavaliṃ haret || 3-1024 ||

aṅgulībhiśca sarvābhirukto bhūtavaliḥ priye |
tathyamuṣṭestu madhyasthāmaṅguliṃ daṇḍavat kuru || 3-1025 ||

p. 255) gajadantamahāmudrā gaṇapasya valau bhavet |
aṅgulībhiśca sarvābhirukto bhūtavaliḥ priye || 1026 ||


validravyāṇyuktāni (mahākālasaṃhitāyām)
tatrādau dakṣiṇācāre |


māṣabhaktaṃ tathā lājā dhānāḥ śaktava eva vā |
pṛthukāstaṇḍulā vāpi svinnā vrīhaya eva vā || 1027 ||

āmikṣā vā yavāgūrvā kṛśaraṃ pāyasaṃ tathā |
ājyābhiṣiktaṃ dadhi vā pakvānnāni phalāni vā || 1028 ||

vāmācāre |

dagdhamīno bharjitaṃ vā temanīkṛtameva vā |
madirā vā pradātavyā valitvena varānane || 1029 ||

sarvasādhāraṇatvena valirnaiṣa prakīrttyate |
adhikāriviśeṣeṇa dātuṃ śakyo valistvayam || 1030 ||

validānamantrāstu (jñānārṇave)

ehyehi devīputrānte vaṭukānte ca nātha ca |
kapilānte jaṭābhāra bhāsurānte trinetra ca || 1031 ||

jvālāmukhaṃ ca sarvānte vighnān nāśaya nāśaya |
sarvopacārasahitaṃ valiṃ gṛhṇa dvidhā padam || 1032 ||

vahnijāyānvito mantro vaṭukasya samīritaḥ |
anena manunā devi vaṭukasya valiṃ haret || 1033 ||

(merau)

yoginīnāṃ valermantraṃ sādhanānte paṭhedimam |

p. 256) yā/ yoginībhyaḥ svāheti kvaṅkārṇaṃ sragdharānvitam ||
rurdhvaṃ brāhmāṇḍato vā divi gaganatale bhūtale niṣkale
pātāle vānale vā salilapavanayoryatra tatra sthitā vā |
kṣetre pāṭhopīṭhādiṣu ca kṛtapadāḥ puṣpadhūpādikena
prītādevyaḥ sadānaḥ śubhavalividhināpāntu vīrendravandyāḥ ||

(jñānārṇave) tūktaślokamuktvā-

etadante maheśāni yā/ bījaṃ yoginī tataḥ || 1034 ||

bhyaḥ svāhā sarvavarṇānte yoginīpadamālikhet |
kavacaṃ cāstramālikhya vahnijāyāṃ punarlikhet || 1035 ||

anena manunā devi yoginīvalimāharem- ityuktam |

tayā |

ṣaḍdīrghasvarasaṃbhinnaṃ kṣakāraṃ punarālikhet || 1036 ||

punaḥ kṣetrapadaṃ pāladhūpadīpādi cālikhet |
sahitaṃ valimālikhya gṛhṇa gṛhṇa tato vadet || 1037 ||

vahnijāyānvito mantraḥ kṣetrapālasya sundari |
anena manunā devi kṣetrapāla valiṃ haret || 1038 ||

gā/ gī/ gū/ trayamālikhya ṅentaṃ gaṇapatiṃ vadet |
varānte varadānte ca sarvānte janamālikhet || 1039 ||

me vaśaṃ cānayetyuktvā valiṃ gṛhṇa dvidhā padam |
vahnijāyānvito mantro gaṇapasyavaliṃ haret || 1040 ||


	siddhāntasāroddhṛtasāmānyapaddhatau tu pūrvādiprādakṣiṇyena gaṇeśādibhyo valipradānamuktam |


p. 257) gaṇeśavaṭukakṣetrapālebhyo valimāharet |
yoginībhyaśca pūrvādiprādakṣiṇyena deśikaḥ- iti || 1041 ||


mahākālenāpi gaṇeśādikrama uktaḥ |


gaṇādhipataye paścādvaṭukāya nivedayet- ityādi |


vastuto dakṣiṇamārge gaṇeśādikramo vāmamārge vaṭukādikramaḥ |


avaśyamādau vāmādau kuryādvaṭukapūjanam || 1042 ||

mārge'smin vaṭuko mukhyaḥ surādyaṃ tena nirmitam |


iti vāmamārgaprakaraṇīya- (merutantre-) vacanāt |
atha bhūtavaliḥ (merutantre)


upaviśyāsane mantrī dadyādbhūtavaliṃ tataḥ || 1043 ||

īśāne maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā sādharaṃ tatra niḥkṣipet |
annaṃ vyañjanatoyāḍhyaṃ pātraṃ puṣpādipūritam || 1044 ||

tatra bhūtāni saṃpūjya tatastebhyo valiṃ haret |
ye raudrā raudrakarmāṇo raudrasthānanivāsinaḥ || 1045 ||

yoginyo'pyugrarūpāśca gaṇānāmadhipāśca ye |
bhūcarāḥ khecarāścaiva ye tiṣṭhantyantarikṣagāḥ || 1046 ||

sarve te prītamanaso bhūtā gṛhṇantvimaṃ valim |
vāmahastena tatpātre jalādhāraṃ samarpayet || 1047 ||
puṣpāñjaliṃ samādāya bhūtāni prārthayediti || 1047ef ||

bhūtāni yānīha vasanti bhūtale
valiṃ gṛhītvā vidhivatprayuktām |
santoṣamāsādya vrajantu sarve
kṣamantu tānyatra namo'stu tebhyaḥ || 3-1048 ||

datvā puṣpāñjaliṃ natvā viharantu yathāsukham |
bhūtānīti ca nārācamudrayā visṛjet tataḥ || 3-1049 ||

p. 258) (śāradāyām)

sahasrāvṛttyā saṃjapya mūlamantramananyadhīḥ |
taṃ japaṃ sarvasaṃpattyai devatāyai samarpayet || 3-1050 ||

(merutantre)

aṣṭottarasahasraṃ tu tadardhe triśataṃ tu vā |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ vāpi japenmūlamatandritaḥ || 3-1051 ||

samāpte tu jape mālāṃ nidadhyānmastakopari |
arghapātrājjalaṃ haste gṛhītvedaṃ paṭhet tataḥ || 3-1052 ||

guhyātiguhyagoptā tvaṃ gṛhāṇāsmatkṛtaṃ japam |
siddhirbhavatu me deva tvatprasādājjagatpate || 3-1053 ||

devasya dakṣiṇe haste toyaṃ datvā samarpayet |

tathā |

tatastu gītastutibhirghaṇṭāvādanapūrvakam || 3-1054 ||

brāhmaṇo vedamantrādyaiḥ kṣatriyaḥ saṃhitādibhiḥ |
paurāṇikādyairvaiśyastu śūdrastu prākṛtādibhiḥ || 3-1055 ||

(yāmale)

tataśca devatāṃ bhaktyā parikramya namedbudhaḥ |


parikramyeti pradakṣiṇīkṛtya |
pradakṣiṇaphalamuktaṃ tantrāntare |


vedavidbhyo dharāṃ datvā yat phalaṃ labhate naraḥ || 3-1056 ||

tat phalaṃ labhate bhaktyā kṛṣṇaṃ kṛtvā pradakṣiṇam |


kṛṇṣamityupalakṣaṇam |
pradakṣiṇabhedā uktā (yāmale)


trikoṇākārakaṃ devi śakteḥ kuryāt pradakṣiṇam || 3-1057 ||

ardhacandraṃ maheśasya pṛṣṭhataśca samīritam |
p. 259) śivapradakṣiṇe mantrī ardhacandrakrameṇa ca || 3-1058 ||

savyāsavyakrameṇaiva somasūtraṃ na laṅghayet |
prasārya dakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ svayaṃ namraśirāḥ punaḥ || 3-1059 ||

darśayeddakṣiṇaṃ pārśvaṃ manasāpi ca dakṣiṇaḥ |
tridhā ca veṣṭayet samyagdevatāyāḥ pradakṣiṇe || 3-1060 ||

ekahastapramāṇo vā ekaṃ vāpi pradakṣiṇam |
akāle darśanaṃ viṣṇorhanti puṇyaṃ purākṛtam || 3-1061 ||

tantrāntare |

ekaṃ caṇḍyāṃ ravau sapta trīṇi dadyādvināyake |
catvāri keśave dadyācchivasyārdhaṃ pradakṣiṇam || 3-1062 ||


praṇāmabhedāstu viṣṇukalpalatāyāmuktāḥ |



tadbhyāṃ1 karābhyāṃ2 jānubhyā3 murasā4 śirasā5 dṛśā6 |
vacasā7 manasā8 ceti praṇāmo'ṣṭāṅga īritaḥ |
bāhubhyāṃ1 ca sajānubhyāṃ2 śirasā3 vacasā4 dṛśā5 || 3-1078 ||

pacāṅgo'yaṃ praṇāmaḥ syāt pujāsu pravarāvimau |

tathā |

bhūmau nipatya yaḥ kuryāt kṛṣṇe dṛṣṭe natiṃ sudhīḥ || 3-1063 ||

sahasrajanmajaṃ pāpaṃ tyaktvā vaikuṇṭhamāpnuyāt |

(viṣṇudharme)

jānubhyāṃ caiva pāṇibhyāṃ śirasā ca vicakṣaṇaḥ || 3-1064 ||

kṛtvā praṇāmān devasya sarvān kāmānavāpnuyāt |
paramāṇūni yāvanti lalāṭe puruṣasya tu || 3-1065 ||

pārthivāni bhavantīha namato madhusūdanam |
tāvato'bdā/stu viprendra svargaloke mahīyate || 3-1066 ||

p. 260) (śāradāyām)

tataḥ śambhordiśi gururvikire pūrvasaṃcite |
hemavastrādisaṃyuktāṃ karkarīṃ toyapūritām || 3-1067 ||

saṃsthāpya tasyāṃ siṃhasthāṃ khaḍgakheṭakadhāriṇīm |
ghorarūpāṃ paścimāsyāṃ pūjayedastradevatām || 3-1068 ||

calāsanena saṃpūjya tāmādāya guruḥ punaḥ |
rakṣeti lokapālānāṃ nālamuktena vāriṇā || 3-1069 ||

devājñāṃ śrāvayannantaḥ parivṛtya pradakṣiṇam |
astramantraṃ samuccārya yathāpūrvaṃ niveśayet || 3-1070 ||

abhyarcya bhūyo gandhādyairvaktraṃ tasya sthirāsanaḥ |


karkarī varddhanī sā ca sanālā ghaṭī |
(kriyāsaṃgrahe)


yathāsthānaṃ niveśyātha karkarīmastrarūpiṇīm || 3-1071 ||

bibhratyadbhutabhūtibhūtapavacovaicitryacitrāmṛta-

iti śrīmanmahārājādhirājaśrīpratāpasiṃhaviracite puraścaryārṇave dīkṣāvidhau guruvaraṇādipūrvakadevatārcanavidhānaṃ nāma tṛtīyastaraṅgaḥ || 3 ||


athāgnisaṃskāravidhiḥ pradarśyate |
tatra prathamaṃ kuṇḍasaṃskāra ukto (merutantre)


vahnestu sthāpanaṃ kuryādvaidikaḥ kalpamārgataḥ |
tāntrikaḥ kuṇḍasaṃskārānaṣṭādaśa samācaret || 4-1 ||

mūlamantreṇa saṃpaśyet prokṣayedastramantrataḥ |
kuśaiḥ saṃtāḍayet kuṇḍamastramantraṃ paṭha/stridhā || 4-2 ||

tataḥ kavacamantreṇa kuṇḍasyābhyukṣaṇaṃ matam |
astramantreṇa khananaṃ kuṇḍasyāṅgulamānataḥ || 4-3 ||

aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ tāṃ mṛdamastreṇa coddharet |
punaḥ śuddhamṛdā taṃ ca pūrayeddhṛdayāṇunā || 4-4 ||

samīkuryāt tato'streṇa secayet kavacena ca |
kāṣṭhādinā kuṭṭayet tamastreṇa syādyathā dṛḍham || 4-5 ||

kuśaiḥ saṃmārjayet kuṇḍaṃ tataśca kavacāṇunā |
vilepanaṃ gomayādbhiḥ kavacenaiva kārayet || 4-6 ||

kavacenāgnisūryendukalāḥ kuṇḍe vicintayet |
trisūtrīkṛtasūtreṇa veṣṭayet pratimekhalam || 4-7 ||

trivāraṃ varmamantreṇa kuṇḍapūjāṃ tataścaret |
tāraścāmukakuṇḍāya āsanaṃ ca namo iti || 4-8 ||

eṣo'rgha ityādyūhaiśca sakalaṃ pūjanaṃ caret |
nābheśca mekhalānāṃ ca kuryādevaṃ prapūjanam || 4-9 ||

saṃcintayet tato'streṇa tat kuṇḍaṃ vajravaddṛḍham |

(śāradāyām)

astreṇa vajrīkaraṇaṃ hṛnmantreṇa kuśaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 4-10 ||

p. 262) catuṣpathaṃ tanutreṇa tanuyādakṣapāṭanam |
yāge kuṇḍāni saṃskuryāt saṃskārairebhirīritaiḥ || 4-11 ||

atha vā tāni saṃskuryāccaturbhirvīkṣaṇādibhiḥ |

(akṣapāṭhanaṃ tu)

acchinnāgraistato darbhairastramantrābhimantritaiḥ || 4-12 ||

kuṇḍabhittigaṇaṃ sarvaṃ varmaṇā paribhūṣayet |
ityevamakṣapāṭākhyaṃ kuṇḍasaṃskāra īritaḥ || 4-13 ||

iti nāradapañcarātre proktam |
(merutantre)

vāmahome tu saṃskārā ādyāścatvāra eva hi |

(nāradapañcarātre)

kuṇḍasyārambhakāle tu saṃskārā na kṛtā yadi || 4-14 ||

niṣpannasya ca te sarve vidheyāḥ syuḥ krameṇa vai |

	ete saṃskārāḥ kuṇḍanirmāṇakāle yadi na kṛtāstadā siddhe tasminnavaśyamācaraṇīyā ityarthaḥ |
(śāradāyām)

tisrastisro likhellekhā hṛdā prāgudagagragāḥ || 4-15 ||

atra likhanakramaḥ (sautrāmaṇītantre)

prācīmudaṅmūrdhasaṃsthaṃ dakṣiṇārambhamālikhet |
udagagraṃ puraḥsaṃsthaṃ paścimārambhamālikhet || 4-16 ||

tisrastisro likhitvaivaṃ prokṣayedvāgbhavena ca |

(śāradāyām)

prāgagrāṇāṃ smṛtā devā mukundeśapurandarāḥ || 4-17 ||

rekhāṇāmudagagrāṇāṃ brahmavaivasvatendavaḥ |

(yāmale)

(merutantre)

trikoṇopari ṣaṭkoṇaṃ trikoṇadvitayasya tu |
p. 263) viṣṇvīśavāsavādīnāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ tatra prapūjanam || 4-19 ||

(śāradāyāmalayostu)

atha vā ṣaṭkoṇavṛttaṃ trikoṇaṃ tatra saṃlikhet |

trikoṇaṣaṭkoṇādilikhanaṃ pakṣāntarābhiprāyeṇoktam |
(śāradāyām)

sarvāṇyabhyukṣya tāreṇa yogapīṭhamathārcayet |

(merutantre)

svāgrādiprādakṣiṇyena tatpadmakeśareṣu ca || 4-21 ||

madhyāntaṃ pūjayedetān jayāṃ ca vijayāṃ tathā |
ajitākhyāṃ cāparājitāṃ nityāṃ ca vilāsinīm || 4-22 ||

dogdhrīmaghorāṃ ca tathā maṅgalāmatha tatra tu |
hrī/ sarvaśaktikamalāsanāya nama uccaran || 4-23 ||

datvā puṣpāñjaliṃ kuṇḍe pūjayedbhuvaneśvarīm |

tantrāntare |

vāgīśvarīśvarayoryogapīṭhātmane namaḥ || 4-24 ||

hrīmādyaḥ pīṭhamantro'yamāvāhyāsmin prapūjayet |

(merutantre) pūjayedbhuvaneśvarīmityadhikṛtya |

kāmonmattaṃ śivaṃ tatra devīmṛtumatīṃ tathā || 4-25 ||

gaṇeśaśaivaśākteṣu dhyāyan saṃpūjayediti |

(śāradāyām)

p. 264) pūjayediti śeṣaḥ |
(rudrayāmale) tu

tataḥ śivāya śaktyai ca namontau kathitau manū |
ābhyāṃ tristriśca saṃpūjya praṇamenmanunāmunā || 4-28 ||

vāgīśvarīmṛtusnātāṃ samastābharaṇānvitām |
vāgīśvareṇa saṃyuktāṃ nāmāmyakhilasiddhaye- ityuktam || 4-29 ||

	vaiṣṇave tu vāgīśvarīvāgīśvarayoḥ sthāne lakṣmīnārāyaṇau pūjyau | taduktam (tattvasārasaṃhitāyām)

lakṣmīmṛtumatīṃ tatra prabhornārāyaṇasya ca |
grāmyadharmeṇa saṃyuktamagniṃ tatra vicintayet || 4-30 ||

evaṃ saure'pi | taduktam (merutantre)

vaiṣṇave ca tathā saure lakṣmīmṛtumatīṃ smaret |
nārāyaṇasya saṃyogājjātamagniṃ vicintayet || 4-31 ||

atra ca |

vibhūtirunnatiḥ kāntiḥ sṛṣṭiḥ kīrtiśca sannatiḥ |

	iti pīṭhaśaktayaḥ | bījādyamāsanaṃ datveti vacanāt śrīlakṣmīnārāyaṇayoryogapīṭhātmane nama iti pīṭhamantraḥ |
	śrīvidyāyāṃ tu (jñānārṇave)

kāmeśvarīṃ kuṇḍamadhye sakāmāmṛtusaṃyutām |
samāvāhya ca saṃpūjya tadgarbhaṃ vahnimāvahet || 4-33 ||

(śāradāyām)

sūryakāntādisaṃbhūtaṃ yadvā śrotriyagehajam |

p. 265) (gautamīye)

pāṣāṇabhavamagniṃ ca yadi vāraṇisaṃbhavam |
śrotriyāṇāṃ gehajaṃ ca vanasthamatha vā bhavet || 4-35 ||

yadṛcchālābhasaṃprāptaścāyogyo yāgakarmaṇi |
niragnibrāhmaṇāllabdho hyardhalābhakaro bhavet || 4-36 ||

kṣatrāllabdhe caturthāṃśaṃ phalaṃ dadyāddhutāśanaḥ |
vaiśyācchradrācca viphalaṃ jāyate homakarma tat || 4-37 ||

(uḍḍīśe)

śrotriyāgārajaṃ vāpi sūryakāntodbhavaṃ tu vā |
araṇīsambhavaṃ vāpi kṣiptaṃ svarṇādibhājane || 4-38 ||

tāmrapātrādinā mantrī pihitaṃ tu samānayet |
atha vānītamabhitaḥ sthāpayet kāṃsyabhājane || 4-39 ||

(merutantre)

araṇyā maṇito vipragehādvahniṃ kumārikā |
suvāsinī vā sadvastrā kṛtaśaucā samāharet || 4-40 ||

pātrāntareṇa pihite tāmrapātrādike śubhe |
agnipraṇayanaṃ kuryāccharāve vātha nūtane || 4-41 ||

dadyādastreṇa pātre'gniṃ kavacena pidhāya ca |
jīrṇe śarāve kāpāle bhinnapātre tatholmuke || 4-42 ||

nāgnipraṇayanaṃ kuryādvyādhihānibhayāvaham |


kravyādāṃśaparityāge viśeṣastantrāntare |


astreṇāgniṃ samādhāya kavacena pidhāya tu || 4-43 ||

kravyādāṃśaṃ tu cāstreṇa nairṛtye saṃkṣipet priye |

(mantramuktāvalyāmapi)

ānīyāstreṇa nairṛtye kravyādāṃśaṃ parityajet || 4-44 ||

p. 266) devāṃśaṃ mūlamantreṇa sthāpayet purataḥ sudhīḥ |

(uḍḍīśe) ca

kūrcāstrābhyāṃ tu nairṛtyāṃ kravyādāṃśaṃ parityajet- ityuktam || 4-45 ||

(gaṇeśvare'pi)

hu/ phaḍantena mūlena kravyādāṃśaṃ parityajet |

(kriyāsāre'pi)

śmaśānādāvāmamāṃsamatti yo vahnirucyate || 4-46 ||

kravyādaṃ tasya nāmāsya tyajet kiṃciddhutāśanāt |

(śāradāyām)

kuṇḍasyottarataḥ sthāpyā praṇītā vāripūritā |

ājyasthālī carusthālī gavyakṣīrasamanvitā |
sruksruvau ca ghṛtaṃ gavyaṃ taṇḍulāḥ kuśamūlakāḥ || 4-147 ||

pālāśāḥ samidhastisraḥ pūrṇapātramataḥ param |

(merutantre)

svagṛhyoktavidhānena vāyudeśācca pūrvataḥ || 4-148 ||

sruksruvau ca praṇītā ca prokṣaṇīpātrayugmakam |
ājyasthālī ca paridhīnidhmaṃ ca kuśamuṣṭayaḥ || 4-149 ||

adhomukhāni pātrāṇi homadravyaṃ valiṃ tathā |
p. 278) gandhapuṣpākṣatādīni sarvāṇyāsādayedatha || 4-150 ||

tānyabhyukṣya pavitreṇa cottānāni nidhāya ca |
prokṣaṇīpātrayugalaṃ paripūrya śubhāmbunā || 4-151 ||

tantrāntare |

svavāmabhāge prāgagrān darbhānāstīryaṃ copari |
yathāvittānusāreṇa sauvarṇādīni karmaṇi || 4-152 ||

adhomukhāni pātrāṇi dvandvarūpāṇi vinyaset |
uttānīkṛtya pātrāṇi jalādyaiḥ saṃprapūrayet || 4-153 ||

(yāmale)

vahnerūttarataḥ svasya vāme deśikasattamaḥ |
darbhānāstīrya pūrvāgrān pātrāṇyāsādayedatha || 4-154 ||

svasyeti pūrvābhimukhasyeti veditavyam |
(tripurāsārasamuccaye'pi)

tato vāmabhāge paristīrya darbhān
pulomātmajāvallabhāśāgatāgrān |

rhṛdā savyabhāgasthitaṃ dravyajātam || 4-156 ||

p. 279) śelvakṣadrumaśigrubhūruhakṛtānyetāni vidveṣaṇo-
ccāṭotsādanamāraṇādiṣu bhṛśaṃ śastāni pātrāṇyapi || 4-157 ||

(śāradāyām)

paścādādāya pāṇibhyāṃ sruksruvau tāvadhomukhau |

tadagramūlamadhyāni śodhayet tairyathākramāt |

punaḥ pratāpya tau mantrī darbhānagnau viniḥkṣipet |

tantrāntare |

gṛhītvā sruksruvāvūrdhvamukhau cādhomukhau kramāt |

sruci śaktiṃ sruve śambhuṃ vinyaseddhṛdayāṇunā |

icchādikā iti icchājñānakriyā ityarthaḥ |

p. 280) (śaivāgame tu)

p. 282) suṣuṇmāṃ madhyato dhyātvā kuryāddhomaṃ yathāvidhi |

tato'nyeṣvapi kuṇḍeṣu saṃskṛteṣu yathāvidhi || 4-247 ||

ācāryo virahedagniṃ pūrvādiṣu samāhitaḥ |
ṛtvijo gandhapuṣpādyairaṅgādyāvaraṇānvitām || 4-248 ||

tatroktadevatāmiṣṭāṃ pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyayā |
mūlenājyena juhuyuḥ sājyena caruṇā tathā || 4-249 ||

p. 289) (somaśambhau)

carostṛtīyabhāgaṃ tu grāsatritayasaṃmitam |
aṣṭagrāsapramāṇaṃ vā darśanasparśavarjitam || 4-250 ||

palāśapuṭake bhuktau bhuktau pippalapatrake |
hṛdā saṃbhojayenmantrī pūtairācāmayejjalaiḥ || 4-251 ||

(śāradāyām)

ācāntaṃ śiṣyamānīya sakalīkṛtya deśikaḥ |
tālapramāṇaṃ hṛjjaptaṃ kṣīravṛkṣādisaṃbhavam || 4-251 ||

dantakāṣṭhaṃ tadā dadyācchiṣyāya vinayātmane |
dantān viśodhyaṃ yatnena tat prakṣālya visarjayet || 4-253 ||

(merutantre'pi)

vitastimātraṃ hṛjjaptaṃ yathoktaṃ dantadhāvanam |
śiṣyāya dadyāt sa radān viśodhya kṣālitaṃ tyajet || 4-254 ||

	dantakāṣṭhaparīkṣā ca maṇḍapapraveśāt prāgeva kartavyā | atra tu dantaśodhanamātramiti saṃpradāyaḥ | atraiva parīkṣā kartavyeti kecit | anye tu kāladvaye'pi tatparīkṣā kartavyaivetyāhuḥ |
	(śāradāyām)

yathāvidhi tamācāntaṃ śikhābandhābhirakṣitam |
vidhāya sārdhamamunā vedyāṃ darbhāstare guruḥ || 4-255 ||

śayīta tasyāṃ tāṃ rātrimadhivāsaḥ samīritaḥ |

(nārāyaṇīye)

punastaṃ śiṣyamācāntaṃ śikhābandhābhirakṣitam || 4-256 ||

kṛtvāṃ vedyāṃ sahānena svapeddarbhāstare guruḥ |

(piṅgalāmate)

svapnamānavamāśritya svāpayet pūrvamastakam || 4-257 ||

p. 290) (vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām)

devasya dakṣiṇe bhāge śiṣyaṃ tamadhivāsayet |
ahatāstaraṇāstīrṇasadarbhaśayane śuciḥ || 4-258 ||

(merutantre)

ācāntamatra ca yathāvidhi taṃ śikhāyāṃ
bandhaiḥ surakṣitamamuṃ pravidhāya mantrī |
vedyāṃ śayīta kuśasaṃstareṇa rajanyāṃ
śiṣyeṇa sardhamanunā gadito'dhivāsaḥ || 4-259 ||

(mahākapilapañcarātre)

gurupādārcanaṃ kṛtvā upavāsī jitendriyaḥ |
darbhaśayyāṃ gato rātrau dṛṣṭvā svapnaṃ nivedayet || 4-260 ||

(yoginītantre) (merutantre) ca

yajjāgrato dūramudaiti daiva-
māvṛtya mantrān prayatastriretān |
laghveṃkabhugdakṣiṇapārśvaśāyī
svapnaṃ parīkṣeta yathopadeśam || 4-261 ||

bhagavan devadeveśa śūlabhṛdvṛṣavāhana |
iṣṭāniṣṭe samācakṣva mama suptasya śāśvataḥ || 4-262 ||

evaṃ suptaḥ kuśāstīrṇe tataḥ prayatamānasaḥ |
niśānte paśyati svapnaṃ śubhaṃ vā yadi vāśubham || 4-263 ||

(piṅgalāmate) (merutantre) ca |

tāro hilidvayaṃ śūlapāṇaye dviṭha īritaḥ |
svapnamānavamantro'yaṃ śambhunā parikīrtitaḥ || 4-264 ||

namo jaya trinetrāya piṅgalāya mahātmane |
vāmāya viśvarūpāya svapnādhipataye namaḥ || 4-265 ||

p. 291) svapne kathana me tathyaṃ sarvakāryeṣvaśeṣataḥ |
kriyāsiddhiṃ vidhāsyāmi tvatprasādānmaheśvara || 4-266 ||

(mantratantraprakāśe)

oṃ hṛtsakalalokāya viṣṇave prabhaviṣṇave |
viśvāya viśvarūpāya svapnādhipataye namaḥ || 4-267 ||

svapnamānavamantro'yaṃ kathito nāradādibhiḥ |

(nāradīye)

parabrahmasvarūpastvamantaścarasī viśvadhṛk || 4-268 ||

śubhāśubhagatiṃ deva svapne me vinivedaya |
ityuccārya namaskuryājjānubhyāmavanīṃ gataḥ || 4-269 ||

prasanno vāgyatastasmin śayīteṣṭaṃ vicintayet |

atha śubhasvapnāḥ | (yoginītantre)

trividhaṃ darśanaṃ tasya yathārthamayathārthakam || 4-270 ||

apākajaṃ yat svasthānāṃ saṃyatānāṃ hi darśanam |
yathārthamayathārthaṃ tadasvasthānāṃ vikārajam || 4-271 ||
apākajaṃ mānasaṃ ca yathārthaphalamucyate || 4-271ef ||

(āgamasiddhānte)

ādye varṣādvatsarārdhāddvitīye
yāme pāko varṣapādāt tṛtīye |
yāme pākaḥ śarvarīturyapāde
sadyaḥ pāko yo visargeṣu dṛṣṭaḥ || 4-272 ||

tathā |

svapne paśyati vai devaṃ nijeṣṭaṃ sarvato mukham |
guruṃ prasādasumukhaṃ nirmalaṃ candramaṇḍalam || 4-273 ||

gaṅgāṃ bhāgīrathīṃ bhānuṃ liṅginaṃ liṅgamaiśvaram |
p. 292) prasannāṃ tatra jānīyāt siddhiṃ svapnanidarśane || 4-274 ||

(nāradapañcarātre)

kṣitilābhaḥ kṣataṃ cābdhitaraṇaṃ cāgnipūjanam |
homaśca jvalite vahnau saṃgrāmavijayastathā || 4-275 ||

haṃsakokamayūrāṇāṃ sthārohaṇameva ca |

(mahākapilapañcarātre)

kanyāṃ chatraṃ rathaṃ dīpaṃ prāsādaṃ kamalaṃ nadīm || 4-276 ||

kuñjaraṃ vṛṣabhaṃ mālāṃ samudraphenilaṃ drumam |
parvataṃ ca hayaṃ madhyamāmamāṃsasurāsavam || 4-277 ||

evamādīni sarvāṇi dṛṣṭvā siddhimāpnuyāt |
yadā karmasu kāmyeṣu striyaṃ svapneṣu paśyati || 4-278 ||

samṛddhiṃ tatra jānīyāt tasmin svapnanidarśane |
nadīsamudrataraṇamākāśagamanaṃ tathā || 4-279 ||

bhāskarodayanaṃ caiva prajvalantaṃ hutāśanam |
grahanakṣatratārāṇāṃ candramaṇḍaladarśanam || 4-280 ||

harmyaprarohaṇaṃ caiva prāsādaśiraso'pi vā |
nāgāśvavṛṣabhendrāṇāṃ taruśailāgrarohaṇam || 4-281 ||

vimānagamanaṃ caiva siddhamantrasya darśanam |
evamādīni saṃdṛṣṭvā naraḥ siddhimavāpnuyāt || 4-282 ||

svapne tu madirāpānamāmamāṃsasya bhojanam |
kṛmiviṣṭhānulepaṃ ca rudhireṇābhiṣecanam || 4-283 ||

bhojanaṃ dadhibhaktasya śvetavastrānulepanam |
p. 293) siṃhāsanaṃ rathaṃ yānaṃ dhvajaṃ rājyābhiṣecanam || 4-284 ||

ratnānyābharaṇādīni dṛṣṭvā svapne prasīdati |

(nāradapañcarātre)

gururdevo dvijaḥ kanyā gogajāśvāśca keśarī || 4-285 ||

darpaṇaṃ śaṅkhabheryau ca tantrīvādyaṃ ca rocanam |
tāmbūlabhakṣaṇaṃ caiva tathā badhyābhivandanam || 4-286 ||

siddhānnamāmamāṃsaṃ ca madyastrīmadirāvasāḥ |
chatraṃ yānaṃ sitaṃ vastraṃ tathānyaṃ śvetacandanam || 4-287 ||

mālāmuktāphale hāraḥ pūrṇaḥ samuditaḥ śaśī |
pracaṇḍakiraṇaḥ sūryo nimnagātha mahodadhiḥ || 4-288 ||

praphullapādapaḥ śālī rocanā kuṅkumaṃ madhu |

upāsanno'pyathācāryo gāyatrī ca vaśaṃ gatā |
sarve svapne śubhāḥ proktāḥ siddhimokṣaphalapradāḥ || 4-290 ||

(nāradīye)

grahaṇaṃ tu parastrīṇāmagamyāgamanaṃ tathā |
daṃśanaṃ śvetanāgena bandhanaṃ śṛṅkhalādibhiḥ || 4-291 ||

evamādīni cānyāni śubhānyāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ |

(merutantre)

pūrṇakumbho darpaṇaṃ ca mīnamājyaṃ ca rocanā || 4-294 ||

p. 294) śavaṃ rodanahīnaṃ ca śastrāṇi ca patākikā |
bhāradvājo nṛpālaśca vedaśabdaśca gāyanam || 4-295 ||

kaśāmāṅgalyagītāni cāṅkuśaṃ yaddhṛdaḥ priyam |
svapne dṛṣṭāni caitāni sādhakaḥ siddhimāpnuyāt || 4-296 ||

vyādhyārte nāsti duḥkhaṃ syāt sāmānyaṃ sukhamāpnuyāt |
yadā karmasu kāmyeṣu striyaṃ svapneṣu paśyati || 4-297 ||

kāmayantīṃ nadīsindhutaraṇaṃ bhramaṇaṃ ca khe |
bhāskarodayanaṃ caiva grahaṇaṃ candrasūryayoḥ || 4-298 ||

harmyaprāsādanāgāśvavṛṣaśailāgnirohaṇam |
bhojanaṃ dadhibhaktasyāpakvamāṃsasya bhojanam || 4-299 ||

kṛmiviṣṭhānulepaśca rudhireṇābhiṣecanam |
tāmbūlabhakṣaṇaṃ madyapānaṃ badhyābhivandanam || 4-300 ||

pracaṇdakiraṇaḥ svasthaḥ kāryasiddhestu lakṣaṇam |
śvetāmbaradharā nārī śvetamālyānulepanā || 4-301 ||

avagūhati yaṃ svapne tasya śrīḥ sarvatomukhī |
āsane śayane pāne śarīre vāhane gṛhe || 4-302 ||

jvalamāne vibudhyeta tasya śrīḥ sarvatomukhī |
sitoragasya daṃśaśca jāyate dakṣiṇe kare || 4-303 ||

daśāhobhirbhavellābho rogī rogādvimucyate |
nigaḍairbāhupāśena badhyate vā suto bhavet || 4-304 ||

pratiṣṭhā vardhate tasya dugdhapānād dhanakṣayaḥ |
surāraktaṃ piban vidyāṃ labhedvipro dhanaṃ param || 4-305 ||

dadhi dṛṣṭvā bhavet prītirgodūmaiśca dhanāgamaḥ |
p. 295) siddhāviṣṭāgamaṃ vidyāllābhaḥ siddhārthakaistathā || 4-306 ||

dadhilābhādbhavedartho ghṛtalābhādbhavejjayaḥ |
ghṛtāśanāddhanakleśo jayastu dadhibhakṣaṇe || 4-307 ||

liṅgādyabhyarcanaṃ dṛṣṭvā vyavahāre jayo bhavet |
yastu madhye taḍāgāderbhuñjīta ghṛtapāyasam || 4-308 ||

niṣkampapuṣkare patre taṃ vidyāt pṛthivīpatim |
devatāliṅgino gāvaḥ pitaro brāhmaṇā nṛpāḥ || 4-309 ||

yadvadanti naraṃ svapne tat tathaiva bhaviṣyati |
antraiḥ saṃveṣṭayedyastu grāmaṃ nagarameva vā || 4-310 ||

grāme maṇḍalarājyaṃ syānnagare pārthivo bhavet |
candratārārkagrasanaṃ parimārgaṇameva ca || 4-311 ||

bhūmyambudhīnāṃ grasanaṃ śatrūṇāṃ ca badhakriyā |
jayo vivādadyūteṣu śatrūṇāṃ ca tathā jayaḥ || 4-312 ||

vaḍavāmahiṣīsiṃhīgohastinyādidohanam |
prasādo guruvipreṣṭadevasiddhādikācchubhaḥ || 4-313 ||

snānaṃ rājyābhiṣekaśca svaśiracchedanaṃ mṛtiḥ |
tīrthodakānāṃ taraṇaṃ tathā viṣamalaṅghanam || 4-314 ||

gohastivaḍavāsūtivahnilābhastathaiva ca |
parastryāliṅganaṃ tasyā lābho rodanameva ca || 4-315 ||

viṣṭhālepaḥ kundajātīvakulakṣmeśadarśanam |
raṇadyūtivimardeṣu jayo nigaḍabandhanam || 4-316 ||

paśumarttyamṛgāṇāṃ ca labdhiradhyasanaṃ tathā |
śikhilābhaḥ śvetavastrapuṣpacandanadhāraṇam || 4-317 ||

p. 296) maṇitārasuvarṇādipātre bhojanakaṃ yadā |
paśyet tadā sādhakasya jñeyā siddhirupasthitā || 4-318 ||

kāryārttānāṃ kāryasiddhiritareṣāṃ dhanāgamaḥ |

athāśubhasvapnāḥ |

kṛṣṇāmbaradharā nārī kṛṣṇagandhānulepanā || 4-319 ||

evaṃ tu raktayā vyādhiḥ śoko'ruṇasamānayā || 4-320 ||

nīlayā kalaho hatyā pītayā kīrayā vipat |

tathā |

bandhyācarmatuṣāsthyādilavaṇāṅāramindhanam || 4-321 ||

viṭklībonmattatailāni jaṭilārivanauṣadham |

vimuktakeśaḥ patito vyaṅgāsṛk śaraṭākṣatam |

guḍastakraṃ ca kāṣāyī paṅkaṃ raṇḍā kalirgṛhe |
vastrādikasya skhalanaṃ lulāpasamaraṃ tathā || 4-324 ||

etairdṛṣṭairna kāryaṃ syādrogī mṛtyumavāpnuyāt |

svapne'pi salilaṃ dṛṣṭvā naraḥ śokamavāpnuyāt |
rūpāntarapraveśaśca dakṣiṇāśāgatistathā || 4-327 ||

paṅke nimajjanaṃ snānaṃ putrabhāryāvināśadam |
gāhāstīdvajedevāde (?) varjya kṛṣṇaṃ na śobhanam || 4-328 ||

p. 297) karpāsabhasmāsthyādīni tyaktvā bhuktaṃ śubhāvaham |
raktagandhāmbarasragvī kṛṣṇo vā dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ || 4-329 ||

kharoṣṭramahiṣārūḍhastrimāsaṃ na sa jīvati |
śvabhre vā patanaṃ gehe dvāraṃ cāsya pidhīyate || 4-330 ||

na cottiṣṭati yastasmāt tadabdaṃ tasya jīvitam |

śukrasya paśyedyaḥ svapne tasyāyurdaśamāsikam |
tāḍyate vikṛtaiḥ kṛṣṇaiḥ puruṣairudyatāyudhaiḥ || 4-332 ||

pāṣāṇaistāḍyate vāpi tasya mṛtyurna saṃśayaḥ |
mahiṣoṣṭrakharārūḍhastailābhyaktaśca muṇḍitaḥ || 4-333 ||

dakṣiṇāśāṃ prasthito yastasya mṛtyurna saṃśayaḥ |

parvatānāṃ ca vṛkṣāṇāmadhikāracyutirbhavet |
hāniścaiva svagotrāṇāṃ vyādhinābhibhavastathā || 4-335 ||

gṛhasaṃmārjanaṃ lopo gomayena mṛdāpi vā |
tīrttvā nadīṃ samāyātastīre'nyadvasanaṃ dhṛtam || 4-336 ||

rogī rogādvimucyeta hāniḥ svasthasya jāyate |
puṃnāmāno mṛgakhagāḥ potakī śukarī ralā || 4-337 ||

piṅgalā chucchukā pallī vāmāṅge kokilā śivā |
strīnāmāno mṛgakhagāḥ śrīkaṇṭho vānaro ruruḥ || 4-338 ||

chikkaraḥ pippalo bhāso dakṣe kākarkṣakukkuṭāḥ |

svasthasya codyamāllābho digvyatyāsānna śobhanam |

p. 298) atha svapnanivedanam (nibandhe)

śiṣyasya śucirācāntaḥ puṣpahasto gurūttamaḥ || 4-340 ||

praṇamya śirasā hṛṣṭastasmai svapnaṃ nivedayet |

(piṅgalāmate)

śubhe śubhaṃ bhavet tasya juhuyādaśabhe śatam || 4-343 ||

astreṇeti kramādvidvān �- �- �- �- �- �- �- iti |

(nāradapañcarātre)

dṛṣṭvā samācareddhomaṃ dantakāṣṭhoditaṃ mune |

	astramantragāyatrīsiṃhamantrāṇāṃ vikalpaḥ | tārāvajranakhaṃ daṃṣṭrāyudhāya mahāsiṃhāya hu/phaṭ namaḥ | siṃhasya manuriti (merau proktaḥ)
	atha dīkṣādinakṛtyam (merutantre)

sarve te svasvakuṇḍeṣu juhuyuḥ saghṛtaistilaiḥ |

p. 299) dravyairvidhānaproktairvā hunedaṣṭādhikaṃ śatam |

(śrībhaviṣye)

(tripurāsāre)

agneḥ karṇe homato vyādhivādhā

tantrāntare |

yataḥ kāṣṭhaṃ tataḥ śrotraṃ yatra dhūmo'tra nāsikā |

p. 300) anyatrāpi |

tanmukhaṃ tatra vijñeyaṃ caturaṅgulamānataḥ |

(navadurgākalpe)

yatrālpajvalanaṃ netraṃ yatra bhasma tu tacchiraḥ |
yatraiva jvalito vahnistatra jihvā prakīrtitā || 4-360 ||

(śāradāyām)

vaiśvānaraṃ sthitaṃ dhyāyet samiddhomeṣu deśikaḥ |
śayānamājyahomeṣu niṣaṇṇaṃ śeṣavastuṣu || 4-361 ||

tathā |

svarṇasindūrabālārkakuṅkumakṣaudrasannibhaḥ |
suvarṇaretaso varṇaḥ śobhanaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 4-362 ||

bherīvāridahastīndradhvanirvahneḥ śubhāvahaḥ |
nāgacampakapunnāgapāṭalāyūthikānibhaḥ || 4-363 ||

padmendīvarakahlārasarpirguggulusannibhaḥ |
pāvakasya śubho gandha ityuktastantravedibhiḥ || 4-364 ||

pradakṣiṇāstyaktakampāśchatrābhāḥ śikhinaḥ śikhāḥ |
śubhadā yajamānasya rājyasyāpi viśeṣataḥ || 4-365 ||

kundendudhavalo dhūmo vahneḥ proktaḥ śubhāvahaḥ |
kṛṣṇaḥ kṛṣṇagate varṇo yajamānaṃ vināśayet || 4-366 ||

śveto rājyaṃ nihantyāśu vāyasasvarasannibhaḥ |
kharasvarasamo vahnerdhvaniḥ sarvavināśakṛt || 4-367 ||

p. 301) pūtigandho hutabhujo doturduḥkhaprado bhavet |
chinnavṛttāṃ śikhāṃ kuryānmṛtyuṃ dhanaparikṣayam || 4-368 ||

śukapakṣanibho dhūmaḥ pārāvatasamaprabhaḥ |
hāniṃ turagajātīnāṃ gavāṃ ca kurute sadā || 4-369 ||

evaṃ vidheṣu doṣeṣu prāyaścittāya deśikaḥ |
mūlenājyena juhuyāt pañcaviṃśatimāhutim || 4-370 ||

(tripurāsāracamuccaye)

vasvadhikaṃ śatamājyairjuhuyācchāntikamamīṣu doṣeṣu |
astreṇa sādhakendrastanuyādatha cakrapūjāṃ ca- ityuktam || 4-371 ||

(śāradāyām)

tataḥ sudhautaṃ dantādyaṃ snātaṃ śiṣyaṃ samāhitam |
pāyayitvā pañcagavyaṃ kuṇḍasyāntikamānayet || 4-372 ||

(prayogasāre'pi)

pañcagavyaṃ yathāproktaṃ pītvācāntaṃ yathāvidhi |
dvāreṇa dakṣiṇenātha yāgasthānaṃ praveśayet || 4-373 ||

(śāradāyām)

vilokya divyadṛṣṭyā taṃ taccaitanyaṃ hṛdambujāt |
gururātmani saṃyojya kuryādadhvaviśodhanam || 4-374 ||

tantrāntare |

hṛdi sthitaṃ taccaitanyaṃ prasphurattārakākṛtim |
ādāya athāpayet svīye hṛdaye'ṅkuśamudrayā || 4-375 ||

(ṣaḍanvayamahāratne)

śodhanaṃ nāma tattvānāṃ kāraṇaikatvacintanam |
varṇādīnāṃ kalānāṃ ca tasyāṃ cidaikyacintanam || 4-376 ||

p. 302) (śāradāyām)

uktaṃ kalādhvā tattvādhvā bhuvanādhveti ca trayam |
varṇādhvā ca padādhvā ca mantrādhveti paraṃ trayam || 4-377 ||

(vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām)

tatra śābdāstrayo'dhvānastrayastvārthāḥ samīritāḥ |

tantrāntare |

mantrādhvā ca padādhvā ca varṇādhveti ca śabdataḥ || 4-378 ||

bhuvanādhvā ca tattvādhvā kalādhvā cārthatastathā |

	atra tu saṃhārakrameṇoktiḥ | (śāradāyām) sṛṣṭikramaṇeti jñeyam |
(merutantre)

śiṣyasya pādayościntyā guruṇā tu kalā imāḥ || 4-379 ||

nivṛttiśca pratiṣṭhā ca vidyā śāntistathaiva ca |
śāntyatīteti pañcātha śodhanakrama ucyate || 4-380 ||

kūrcena ca guruḥ śiṣyaṃ spṛśan kuṇḍe svake hunet |
tilairghṛtāktaistārādyamamukādhvānamatra ca || 4-381 ||

śodhayāmi dviṭhāntena mantreṇāṣṭāhutirhunet |
tattvādhvani vilīnaṃ tu kalādhvānaṃ vicintayet || 4-382 ||

tato liṅgapradeśe tu tattvānyetāni cintayet |
gāṇeśāni ca śaivāni tatraitāni rasāgnayaḥ || 4-383 ||

tadyathā śivaśaktī ca sadāśivamaheśvarau |
vidyeti pañcaśuddhāni śuddhāśuddhāni sapta ca || 4-384 ||

māyā vidyā kalā rāgaḥ kālo niyatireva ca |
puruṣaścātha śuddhāni prakṛtirbuddhyahaṅkṛtī || 4-385 ||

manaścendriyadaśakaṃ tanmātrābhūtapañcakam |

	śuddhānīti | āṇavakārmaṇamāyīyākhyamalatrayarahitānītyarthaḥ | tatrāṇavo nāma sadāśivatvena svasthānāvamarṣaḥ | kārmaṇo nāma puṇyapāpavānahamiti pratītiḥ | māyīyo nāmāhaṅkārādāvātmatvabuddhiḥ | śuddhaśuddhānīti | kāraṇatvena malatrayarāhityācchuddhāni | kāryarūpatvena tadyuktatvādaśuddhānītyarthaḥ |

atha vaiṣṇavatattvāni dvātriṃśatpramitāni tu || 4-386 ||

jīvaḥ prāṇo mano buddhistanmātrā bhūtapañcakam |
daśendriyāṇi hṛtpadmaṃ sūryasomāgnayastathā || 4-387 ||

vāsudevaḥ saṃkarṣaṇaśca pradyumnaścāniruddhakaḥ |
saurāṇi bhūtatanmātrāmanogarvendriyāṇi ca || 4-388 ||

prakṛtirbuddhiriti ca caturviṃśatisaṃmitam |

(śāradāyām)

nivṛttyādyāḥ kalāḥ pañca tato vinduḥ kalā punaḥ || 4-389 ||

nādaḥ śaktiḥ sadāpūrvaśivaśca prakṛterviduḥ |
ātmavidyā śivaḥ paścācchivo vidyā svayaṃ punaḥ || 4-390 ||

sarvatattvaṃ ca tattvāni proktāni tripadātmanaḥ |
tattvādhvā kathito hyeṣa tattadāgamavedibhiḥ || 4-391 ||

(merutantre)

daśaiva śaktitattvāni ātmavidyā śivā iti |
śivāvidyā tathātmeti cātmavidyā śivā punaḥ || 4-392 ||

sarvatattvaṃ ceti punaḥ prāgvadūhena homayet |
tattvādhvānaṃ vilīnaṃ tu cintayedbhuvanādhvani || 4-393 ||

śiṣyasya nābhau cintyāni bhuvanāni caturdaśa |
tāni krameṇa pātālarasātalamahātalam || 4-394 ||

talātalaṃ ca sutalaṃ vitalaṃ cātalaṃ ca bhūḥ |
bhuvaśca svarmahaścāpi janastapa iti kramāt || 4-395 ||

satyaṃ ca bhuvanādhvānaṃ homādyūhastu pūrvavat |
vilīnaṃ bhuvanādhvānaṃ varṇādhvani vicintayet || 4-396 ||

p. 304) akārādikṣakārāntān varṇān saṃcintayeddhṛdi |
varṇādhvānaṃ vilīnaṃ tu padādhvani vicintayet || 4-397 ||

homohau prāgvadevātra lalāṭe cintayet tataḥ |
padādhvānaṃ mūlamantrapadavarṇasvarūpakaḥ || 4-398 ||

padādhvātrohahomādi prāgvadeva samācaret |
mantrādhvani vilīnaṃ tu padādhvānaṃ vicintayet || 4-399 ||

saṃpūrṇamantro mantrādhvā cintayecchiṣyamūrdhani |
homohaḥ prāgvadevātra vilīnohaṃ pare śive || 4-400 ||

brahamrandhrasthite dhyāyedataḥ syādadhvaśodhanam |
sṛjet sṛṣṭyā vilīnāntācchivāntācca gurūttamaḥ || 4-401 ||

deśikendraśca taṃ śiṣyaṃ dṛṣṭvā dṛṣṭyā ca divyayā |

(śāradāyāmapi)

ātmasthitaṃ taccaitanyaṃ punaḥ śiṣye niyojayet || 4-404 ||

(kriyāsaṃgrahe)

(śāradāyām)

srucā pūrṇāhutiṃ datvā mūlamantreṇa deśikaḥ |

p. 305) (vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām)

p. 306) dadyāt pūrṇāhutirmantrī parāmṛtadhiyā tataḥ |

(gautamīyatantre)

sruksruvau tu samādāya ghṛtenāpūrya tat punaḥ || 4-418 ||

homadravyāṇi niḥkṣipya nābhau saṃsthāpya tau punaḥ |

itaḥ pūrvaṃ prāṇabuddhiṃ dehe dharmādhikārataḥ |

	jāgratsvapnasuṣuptyavasthāsu manasā vācā karmaṇā hastābhyāṃ padbhyāmudareṇa śiśnā yat smṛtaṃ yaduktaṃ yat kṛtaṃ tat sarvaṃ brahmārpaṇaṃ bhavatu svāhā |

evaṃ homavidhiṃ kṛtvā kuṇḍasthaṇḍiladevatāḥ |

ānayed kalaśe cāpi kṛṣṇaikyaṃ bhāvayedguruḥ |

p. 307) vyāhṛtibhirmahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ |

	oṃbhūrbhuvaḥsvaścandramase nakṣatrebhyo digbhyaśca mahate svāhā | pāvakaṃ yojayitveti |

tathā |

pūjitāṃ punarādāya varddhinīmastrarūpiṇīm |
tasyāḥ susādhitaistoyaiḥ siñcedrakṣārthamañjasā || 4-471 ||

avaśiṣṭena toyena śiṣyamācāmayedguruḥ |
tatastaṃ sakalīkṛtya devatātmānamātmavit || 4-472 ||

p. 312) utthāya śiṣyo vimale vāsasī paridhāya ca |
ācamya vāgyato bhūtvā niṣīdet sannidhau guroḥ || 4-473 ||

devatāmātmanaḥ śiṣye saṃkrāntāṃ deśikottamaḥ |
pūjayedgandhapuṣpādyairaikyaṃ saṃbhāvaya/stayoḥ || 4-474 ||

dadyādvidyāṃ tatastasmai vinītāyāmbupūrvakam |

(vaśiṣṭhasaṃhitāyām)

tatastacchirasi svasya hastaṃ datvā śataṃ japet || 4-475 ||

aṣṭottaraśataṃ vāpi dadyādudakapūrvakam |

(gautamīye)

nyāsajālaṃ tasya dehe guruḥ saṃnyasya yatnataḥ || 4-476 ||

dakṣakarṇe vadenmantraṃ trivāraṃ pūrṇamānasaḥ |

(merau)

dakṣakarṇe vadedvidvān viprāyodakapūrvakam || 4-477 ||

anyebhyastu vadedevameva mantraṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ |

(kulāmṛte)

vidyāṃ triḥ kathayet karṇe cāvijñāne ca vā punaḥ || 4-478 ||

(dīpikāyām)

ṛṣyādiyuktamatha mantravaraṃ yathāvad-
bhūyastriśo gururanarghyamavāmakarṇe |

anyatrāpi |

dakṣakarṇe triśo vidyāmekoccāreṇa coccaret |
eṣa vidhirdvijātīnāṃ strīśūdrāṇāṃ ca vāmataḥ || 4-479 ||

tantrāntare |

anayostulyaphalado bhavatvevamudīrayet |
prasannavadanastasmai śiṣyāya munipuṅgavaḥ || 4-480 ||

p. 313) (merutantre)

svato jyotirmayīṃ vidyāṃ gacchantīṃ bhāvayedguruḥ |
āgatāṃ bhāvayecchiṣyo nato'smīti viśeṣataḥ || 4-481 ||

aṣṭottaraśataṃ śiṣyaḥ prajapedguruṇoditam |
aṣṭāviṃśativāraṃ vā hyaṣṭakṛttvo'thavā japet || 4-482 ||

gurudevātmamantrāṇāmaikyaṃ śiṣyo vibhāvayet |
praṇāmaṃ gurave kuryāt sādhako bhiktimān muhuḥ || 4-483 ||

(kriyāsaṃgrahe)

pādayoḥ patitaṃ śiṣyaṃ tata utthāpayedguruḥ |
uttiṣṭha vatsa mukto'si samyagācāravān bhava || 4-484 ||

kīrtiśrīkāntimedhāyurbalārogyaṃ sadā'stu te |

(gautamīye)

gaṇeśādimanuṃ coktvā samayāt kathayedguruḥ || 4-485 ||

gaṇeśādimantradānaṃ pañcāyatanadīkṣāpakṣe |
(dakṣiṇāmūrtisaṃhitāyām)

samīpe cakrarājaṃ ca samyagabhyarcya deśikaḥ |
ādyākṣareṇa saṃlagnaṃ sundaraṃ nāma kalpayet || 4-486 ||

ātmānaṃ yojayitvā tu garubhirmānavaistataḥ |
smarttavyā guravaḥ sarve samyak praṇatipūrvakam || 4-487 ||

bhūmau likhitvā vidyāṃ ca samyak puṣpādibhiryajet |
śiṣyāya darśayedvidyāṃ prajaptāṃ śatadhā priye || 4-488 ||

yadi syānmahatī prītiḥ śiṣyasya praṇatasya ca |
tadaikoccāramātreṇa dakṣakarṇe japet parām || 4-489 ||

(tantrārāje)

uktalakṣaṇasaṃpannaṃ śiṣyamācārabhūṣaṇam |
p. 314) pañcaṣaṭkūṭavidyābhyāṃ śodhitaṃ bahuvāsaraiḥ || 4-490 ||

kalaśairabhiṣiñcyāmuṃ śrīcakre saṃniveśya  vai |
ādhāre hṛdaye mūrdhni cakraṃ saṃcintya madhyataḥ || 4-491 ||

svāntādāvāhya saṃsthāpya saṃpūjya nyāsasaṃyutām |
triśo vidyāṃ vadet karṇe devyātmāpūrṇamānasaḥ || 4-492 ||

devatāgurumantrātmatattvaikyaṃ bhāvayenmudā |
śataṃ japet tadagrastho nikaṭe tridinaṃ vaset || 4-493 ||

na cet saṃcāriṇī śaktirgurumeti na saṃśayaḥ |
tasmāt tadantike tasya pūjādeśādikṛdvaset || 4-494 ||

(gautamīye)

tridinaṃ nivasedbhaktyā siddhaye gurusannidhau |
anyathā tadgataṃ tejo gurumeti na saṃśayaḥ || 4-495 ||

(mantratantraprakāśe)

ācāryādanabhiprāptaḥ prāptaścādattadakṣiṇaḥ |
satataṃ japyamāno'pi mantraḥ siddhiṃ na yacchati || 4-496 ||

sarvasvaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā vittaśāṭhyavivarjitām |
gurave dakṣiṇāṃ datvā tato mantragraho mataḥ || 4-497 ||

(tantrāntare)

gobhūhiraṇyaṃ vipulaṃ gṛhakṣetrādikaṃ bahu |
na cedardhaṃ tadardhaṃ vā taddaśāṃśamathāpi vā || 4-498 ||

akleśādannavastrādi dadyādvittānusārataḥ |

(brahmasaṃhitāyām)

adakṣiṇaṃ kṛtaṃ karma vṛthā nārada niścitam || 4-499 ||

vīṇāṃ me dakṣiṇāṃ dehi sākṣasūtrakamaṇḍalum |
tato mantraṃ gṛhāṇa tvaṃ yathāvidhi mahāmune || 4-500 ||

p. 315) vidhihīnaṃ kṛtaṃ karma siddhiṃ naiva prayacchati |

(gautamīye)

gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyādvittārdhaṃ muktitatparaḥ || 4-501 ||

tadardhaṃ vā tato dadyādyathāśaktyātha bhaktitaḥ |
vittaśāṭhyaṃ na kurvīta kṛte'narthaṃ samāvahet || 4-502 ||

gobhūhiraṇyavastrādi gurave'tha nivedayet |
guruputrāya tatpatnyai tacchiṣye'pi svaśaktitaḥ || 4-503 ||

vastrālaṅkaraṇaṃ dadyādrājyamiṣṭaṃ yathāruci |
ṛtvigbhyo dakṣiṇāṃ dadyādvastrālaṅkārasaṃyutām || 4-504 ||

(merutantre)

alaṅkārā/śca vāsāṃsi dadyādṛtvigbhya ādarāt |
īśvarārpaṇabuddhyā tu dvijāśīrbhiśca nanditaḥ || 4-505 ||

pradadyādgurave śiṣyaḥ svadhanārdhaṃ tadardhakam |
daśāṃśaṃ vā pradadyācca vittaśāṭhyavivarjitaḥ || 4-506 ||

gobhūhiraṇyavipinagṛhakṣetrādikaṃ tathā |
akleśādannavastrādi dadyādvittānusārataḥ || 4-507 ||

prakārāntaramālambya guruṃ yatnena toṣayet |
tadadhīnamanā nityaṃ taccintanaparāyaṇaḥ || 4-508 ||

tatpādābjarajobhūṣo bhavet sādhakasattamaḥ |
tato nānāvidhairbhakṣyairlehyaiścoṣyaistathā paraiḥ || 4-509 ||

brāhmaṇān bhojayet samyak pradadyādbhūridakṣiṇām |

(vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām)

maṇḍapaṃ gurave dadyādyāgopakaraṇaiḥ saha || 4-510 ||

(nārāyaṇīyamahākapilapañcarātrayoḥ)

mantraṃ datvā sahasraṃ vai svasiddhyai deśiko japet |

p. 316) anyatrāpi |

aṣṭottaraṃ sahasraṃ svaśaktihīnānavāptaye japyāt || 4-511 ||

(kulāmṛte)

daṇḍavat praṇataṃ śiṣyaṃ sarvācārā/śca śikṣayet |
snānasandhyāsadācāraṃ nityaṃ kāmyaṃ tathaiva ca || 4-512 ||

mantrasiddhiprakārā/śca śiṣyāyābhivadedguruḥ |

dīkṣādivase upavāso niṣiddhastaduktam (yoginītantre)

mantraṃ datvā guruścaiva upavāsaṃ ya ācaret || 4-513 ||

mahāndhakāre narake kṛmirbhavati nānyathā |
dīkṣāṃ datvā yadā mantrī copavāsaṃ samācaret || 4-514 ||

tasya devaḥ sadā ruṣṭaḥ śāpaṃ datvā puraṃ vrajet |

(bṛhatsiddhāntasāre)

hutaśeṣaṃ tataḥ prāśya kukkuṭāṇḍapramāṇakam || 4-515 ||

caturvidhaṃ ca naivedyaṃ devatāyai yadarpitam |
tadbhaktebhyaḥ pradatvā tadbhuñjīyātāṃ yathāruci || 4-516 ||

guruśiṣyāviti śeṣaḥ |
(merutantre)

śaivastu śaivanirmālyaprasādasyādhikāravān |
anyo nirayamāyāti na doṣaḥ pañcasaṃgame || 4-517 ||

svayaṃbhūtīrthaliṅaṃ vā prasādaṃ vāpi bhaktitaḥ |
aśaivā vātha śaivā vā mucyante pāparāśitaḥ || 4-518 ||

(siddhāntasāre)

dinatrayaṃ gurukule sthitvā turye'hni devatām |
arcayitvā gṛhaṃ gacchedgurvājñāṃ śirasā vahan || 4-519 ||

	atha vīradīkṣāyāṃ viśeṣaḥ | tatra bhūtaśuddhimātṛkānyāsa-ṣoḍhānyāsādikaṃ sarvaṃ guruḥ svaśarīre śaktiśarīre ca vidhāya kalaśasthāpanaṃ śrīpātrādisthāpanaṃ ca kṛtvā (p. 317) dūtīyāgakrameṇa kuṇḍagolotthadravyamutpādya saṃskṛtya matsyamāṃsāderapi saṃskāraṃ vidhāya vīrapūjākrameṇa devatāṃ saṃpūjya śiṣyāya mantradānasamaye |

śiṣyahaste maheśāni pātraṃ datvā kuleśvari- iti |

(vīracūḍāmaṇyu-) ktavīrapātram |

idaṃ pavitramamṛtaṃ pīyatāṃ bhavabheṣajam || 4-520 ||

paśupāśasamucchedakāraṇaṃ bhairavoditam |

iti (kurlārṇava-) krameṇa śiṣyahaste dadyāt |
śiṣyo'pyanenaiva mantreṇa pīyatāmityatra pibāmīti paṭhitvā-

dharmādharmahavirdīpte svātmāgnau manasā srucā || 4-521 ||

suṣumṇā vartmanā nityamakṣavṛttīrjuhomyaham |

ityādimantreṇa pibet | tato gururmantraṃ dadyāt |
tatra (yāmale)

ṛṣyādisahitāmenāṃ likhitvā bhūmimadhyagām || 4-522 ||

puṣpādinā samabhyarcya śiṣyāyātha pradarśayet |
yadi śiṣye mahāprītistadā karṇe vadedguruḥ || 4-523 ||

tato'pyatitarāṃ śiṣyo guroścet prītibhājanam |
tadā gaṇḍūṣapūrvaṃ hi dadyādenāṃ gurūttamaḥ || 4-524 ||

nirvikalpo'pi śiṣyastāṃ gṛhnīyāddhṛṣṭamānasaḥ |
tadabhāve na siddhiḥ syāddevatāśāpa?māpnuyāt || 4-525 ||

(dakṣiṇāmūrtisaṃhitāyām)

kalaśasthaiḥ pallavaistu trivāraṃ procchayecchubham |
bhavedyadābhiṣiktastu tadāsau deśikāgraṇīḥ || 4-526 ||

gaṇḍūṣatattvasaṃyuktastadārho'yaṃ śivārcane |
anyathā naiva siddhiḥ syāt savikalpasya pārvati || 4-527 ||

gaṇḍūṣadāne śiṣyasya vikalpo yadi jāyate |
na siddhistasya kutrāpi śrīvidyāpi parāṅmukhī || 4-528 ||

p. 318) śeṣaṃ paśudīkṣāsamānam |
dvitīyavidyādīkṣāyāṃ viśeṣa ukto (nīlatantre)

aṣṭottarasahasrahomaṃ vilvapatreṇa kārayet |
atha vāṣṭaśataṃ kuryādaṣṭāviṃśatimeva vā || 4-529 ||

pāyasaṃ kṛśaraṃ dadyānmodakaṃ khaṇḍaśarkarām |
valiṃ datvā chāgalaṃ vai godhāmāṃsamanuttamam || 4-530 ||

rambhāpuṣpaṃ sāramatsyaṃ juhuyācca prayatnataḥ |
yavakṣīraṃ tato hutvā śālyannaṃ madhunā saha || 4-531 ||

gobhūhiraṇyavastrādaistoṣayedgurumātmanaḥ- iti |

(merutantre)

evaṃ kriyāvatī dīkṣā saṃpaddātrī nirūpitā || 4-532 ||

rājñāṃ dhanāḍhyavaiśyānāṃ viprāṇāṃ vyavasāyinām |
ayaṃ dīkṣākrasaḥ prokto bhogayuksiddhimicchatām || 4-533 ||

iti kriyāvatī dīkṣā paripūrṇā |

bibhratyadbhutabhūtibhūtapavacovecitryacitrāmṛtasrotaḥ-
śālini sannibandhanasaritsaṃbhedamabhyarhitam |
turyaṃ śrīnaradevanirmitapuraścaryārṇave prasphurat-
sadyuktisphuṭamaukti**?kararucottuṅgastaraṅgo gataḥ || 4-534 ||



iti śrīmanmahārājādhirājaśrīpratāpasiṃhasāhaviracite puraścaryārṇave kriyāvatīdīkṣāvidhirnāma caturthastaraṅgaḥ || 4 ||
